# 3.10 Review and Rating Module

The **Review and Rating Module** enables customers to provide feedback on restaurants, food items, and delivery experiences. It helps maintain service quality, improve customer trust, improve platform transparency, and assist future customers in making informed decisions. The module also supports review moderation, fraud prevention, business analytics, restaurant responses, and compliance auditing.

The functional requirements included in the **Review and Rating Module** are:

## Customer Review & Rating

### 3.10.1 Rate Restaurant

#### 3.10.1.1 Description

The **Rate Restaurant** use case enables an eligible customer to submit a star rating for a restaurant after successfully completing an order. Restaurant ratings capture the customer's overall dining experience, including food quality, packaging, hygiene, service, value for money, and overall satisfaction. The submitted rating contributes to the restaurant's overall rating, customer review summary, and platform analytics while ensuring that only verified customers who have completed eligible orders can provide ratings.

#### 3.10.1.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Restaurant
- Review & Rating Service
- Order Management Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.1.3 Preconditions

1. Customer must be registered on the platform.
2. Customer must be authenticated.
3. Customer account must be active.
4. Customer must have placed an order for the restaurant.
5. The order must belong to the authenticated customer.
6. The order must be successfully delivered.
7. The order must not be cancelled.
8. The order must not be permanently deleted from the system.
9. The rating submission window must still be valid.
10. Customer must satisfy review eligibility rules.
11. Customer must not be blocked from submitting reviews.
12. Restaurant must exist in the system.
13. Restaurant must be active.
14. Restaurant must not be permanently removed from the platform.
15. The order must contain at least one item from the restaurant.
16. Review service must be operational.
17. Database connectivity must be available.
18. Audit logging service must be available.

#### 3.10.1.4 Trigger

The customer selects **"Rate Restaurant"** from the completed order details or restaurant order history screen.

#### 3.10.1.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Restaurant ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the restaurant being rated. |
| Order ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the completed order associated with the rating. |
| Customer ID | UUID | System Generated | Identifier of the authenticated customer submitting the rating. |
| Rating Value | Integer | Yes | Overall restaurant rating provided by the customer (1–5 stars). |
| Rating Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Date and time when the rating is submitted. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Device used for rating submission (Android, iOS, Web). |
| Device Identifier | String | System Generated | Unique identifier of the customer's device, if available. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | IP address from which the rating is submitted. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | Version of the QuickBite application used during submission. |
| Customer Time Zone | String | System Generated | Customer's local time zone at the time of submission. |
| Submission Source | Enum | System Generated | Source of rating submission (Order Details, Order History, Restaurant Page, Notification). |
| Eligibility Status | Boolean | System Generated | Indicates whether the customer is eligible to submit the rating. |
| Verification Status | Enum | System Generated | Verification result for the rating request (Verified, Pending Verification, Rejected). |
| Rating Status | Enum | System Generated | Processing status of the rating (Submitted, Accepted, Rejected). |
| Remarks | String | No | Optional internal remarks generated during rating validation or moderation. |

#### 3.10.1.6 Business Rules

1. Only verified customers who have successfully completed an order shall be allowed to rate a restaurant.
2. A restaurant rating shall always be associated with a valid completed order.
3. Customers shall not rate restaurants for cancelled orders.
4. Customers shall not rate restaurants for failed or refunded orders unless permitted by platform policy.
5. Each eligible order shall allow only one active restaurant rating from the customer.
6. Customers may update their rating within the configured rating edit window.
7. Once the rating edit window expires, further modifications shall not be permitted.
8. The rating value shall be an integer between **1 and 5**.
9. Decimal or fractional ratings shall not be accepted.
10. Ratings submitted outside the configured review submission period shall be rejected.
11. Ratings shall immediately contribute to the restaurant's overall rating after successful validation.
12. The restaurant's average rating shall be recalculated after every accepted rating.
13. Ratings shall be excluded from analytics if marked as fraudulent or invalid.
14. Customers shall not submit ratings on behalf of another customer.
15. Rating requests shall be processed only for authenticated sessions.
16. Duplicate rating submissions for the same order shall be prevented.
17. If a duplicate submission is detected, the existing rating shall be updated only when platform rules permit.
18. Restaurants shall not directly modify customer ratings.
19. Administrators shall not alter customer ratings except through approved moderation procedures.
20. Ratings submitted from suspended customer accounts shall not be accepted.
21. Ratings associated with deleted customer accounts shall follow the platform's data retention policy.
22. Every accepted rating shall be linked to the corresponding customer, restaurant, and order records.
23. Rating timestamps shall be recorded using the system time.
24. All rating activities shall be recorded in the audit log.
25. Rating submission failures shall be logged for troubleshooting purposes.
26. The system shall validate customer eligibility before accepting a rating.
27. The system may automatically flag suspicious rating activities for moderation.
28. Ratings violating platform policies may be hidden pending administrative review.
29. Restaurant owners shall be able to view aggregated ratings but shall not access customers' internal validation details.
30. Rating submission shall not affect the order lifecycle.
31. Rating processing shall be performed securely without exposing sensitive customer information.
32. The platform shall ensure transactional consistency while storing rating data.
33. Rating statistics displayed to customers shall reflect only approved ratings.
34. Rating data shall be available for reporting and analytics after successful processing.
35. All rating operations shall comply with the platform's security, privacy, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.1.7 Validations

1. The customer shall be authenticated before submitting a restaurant rating.
2. The customer account shall be active and not suspended.
3. The Restaurant ID shall exist in the system.
4. The Order ID shall exist in the system.
5. The order shall belong to the authenticated customer.
6. The order shall be associated with the specified restaurant.
7. The order status shall be **Delivered**.
8. Cancelled orders shall not be eligible for restaurant rating.
9. Returned or invalid orders shall follow platform review eligibility policies.
10. The customer shall not have exceeded the review submission time limit.
11. The customer shall satisfy all review eligibility criteria.
12. The customer shall not have an existing active rating for the same order unless editing is permitted.
13. The Rating Value shall be mandatory.
14. The Rating Value shall be an integer.
15. The Rating Value shall be between **1** and **5** inclusive.
16. Decimal, alphabetic, special characters, or negative values shall not be accepted as rating values.
17. System-generated Customer ID shall match the authenticated session.
18. Restaurant status shall be **Active**.
19. Restaurant shall not be permanently deleted.
20. Database transaction shall complete successfully before confirming the submission.
21. Duplicate submission requests shall be detected and handled safely.
22. The request shall contain all mandatory system-generated identifiers.
23. Invalid or expired authentication tokens shall be rejected.
24. Rating requests from blocked devices or IP addresses shall be rejected according to security policies.
25. Rating submission shall pass fraud detection and risk validation checks.
26. System-generated timestamps shall be recorded successfully.
27. Audit log creation shall succeed before completing the transaction.
28. If any validation fails, the system shall reject the rating request and return an appropriate error message without storing partial data.

#### 3.10.1.8 Main Flow

1. The customer navigates to the **Completed Orders** section.
2. The system displays the customer's delivered orders.
3. The customer selects a completed order.
4. The system retrieves the associated restaurant details.
5. The system verifies that the order is eligible for restaurant rating.
6. The system validates that the authenticated customer owns the selected order.
7. The system checks whether the restaurant is active.
8. The system verifies that the rating submission window has not expired.
9. The system checks whether an active restaurant rating already exists for the selected order.
10. If editing is permitted, the existing rating information is retrieved.
11. Otherwise, the system prepares a new rating submission form.
12. The system displays the restaurant rating interface.
13. The customer selects a rating between **1 and 5 stars**.
14. The customer reviews the selected rating.
15. The customer submits the rating.
16. The system receives the rating request.
17. The system validates all mandatory request fields.
18. The system authenticates the customer session.
19. The system verifies the customer's review eligibility.
20. The system validates the rating value.
21. The system performs duplicate submission checks.
22. The system performs fraud detection and security validation.
23. The system creates or updates the restaurant rating record based on platform rules.
24. The system records the rating timestamp.
25. The system associates the rating with the corresponding customer.
26. The system associates the rating with the completed order.
27. The system associates the rating with the restaurant.
28. The system recalculates the restaurant's average rating.
29. The system updates the restaurant's total rating count.
30. The system refreshes the restaurant rating summary.
31. The system records the rating activity in the audit log.
32. The system triggers review-related analytics processing.
33. The system notifies relevant downstream services, if applicable.
34. The system stores the completed transaction successfully.
35. The system displays a confirmation message indicating that the restaurant rating has been submitted successfully.

#### 3.10.1.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Existing Rating Found**

1. The system detects that the customer has already submitted a rating for the selected order.
2. If the rating edit window is still active, the system redirects the customer to the **Edit Restaurant Rating** flow.
3. Otherwise, the system informs the customer that the rating has already been submitted and cannot be modified.

---

**AF-2: Order Not Eligible for Rating**

1. The system determines that the selected order does not satisfy the review eligibility criteria.
2. The system rejects the rating request.
3. The customer is informed that restaurant ratings can only be submitted for eligible completed orders.

---

**AF-3: Rating Submission Window Expired**

1. The system determines that the permitted rating submission period has expired.
2. The rating request is rejected.
3. The system displays an appropriate validation message.

---

**AF-4: Invalid Rating Value**

1. The submitted rating value fails validation.
2. The system highlights the invalid field.
3. The customer is prompted to select a valid rating between **1** and **5** stars.

---

**AF-5: Restaurant Becomes Unavailable**

1. During processing, the restaurant is identified as inactive or removed.
2. The system terminates the rating submission process.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. The fraud detection engine flags the submitted rating as suspicious.
2. The rating is stored with a **Pending Moderation** status or rejected according to platform policy.
3. The rating is excluded from public rating calculations until moderation is completed.

---

**AF-7: Database Transaction Failure**

1. The system encounters a database failure while saving the rating.
2. The transaction is rolled back completely.
3. No partial data is stored.
4. The customer is informed that the submission could not be completed.

---

**AF-8: Authentication Session Expired**

1. The customer's authentication session expires before submission completes.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer is redirected to the login page.
4. After successful authentication, the customer may retry the submission.

---

**AF-9: Network or Service Failure**

1. A network interruption or dependent service failure occurs during processing.
2. The system safely aborts the transaction.
3. The customer is informed that the request could not be completed and may retry later.

---

**AF-10: Audit Logging Failure**

1. The rating is processed successfully, but audit log creation fails.
2. The system records the failure through internal monitoring mechanisms.
3. The incident is reported for administrative investigation in accordance with platform operational policies.

#### 3.10.1.10 Postconditions

1. The restaurant rating shall be successfully stored in the system upon successful validation.
2. The rating shall be associated with the corresponding customer, restaurant, and completed order.
3. The restaurant's average rating shall be recalculated.
4. The restaurant's total rating count shall be updated.
5. The submitted rating shall become available for review summary calculations according to platform policy.
6. The rating shall be available for reporting and analytics.
7. The rating activity shall be recorded in the audit log.
8. The rating submission timestamp shall be stored.
9. The customer's review history shall be updated.
10. The system shall notify dependent services, if applicable.

#### 3.10.1.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK / 201 Created |
| Message | Restaurant rating submitted successfully. |
| Restaurant ID | Identifier of the rated restaurant. |
| Order ID | Identifier of the associated completed order. |
| Rating Value | Submitted star rating (1–5). |
| Rating ID | Unique identifier generated for the rating. |
| Submission Time | Timestamp of successful submission. |
| Updated Average Rating | Latest calculated average restaurant rating. |
| Total Ratings | Updated number of restaurant ratings. |

#### 3.10.1.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid request data. |
| 401 | Customer authentication failed. |
| 403 | Customer is not eligible to rate this restaurant. |
| 404 | Restaurant or order not found. |
| 409 | Restaurant rating already exists for the selected order. |
| 410 | Rating submission window has expired. |
| 422 | Invalid rating value. |
| 429 | Too many rating requests submitted. |
| 500 | Internal server error occurred while processing the request. |
| 503 | Review service is temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.1.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authenticated customers shall be able to rate a restaurant.
2. Only customers with eligible delivered orders shall be allowed to submit ratings.
3. The system shall accept only ratings between **1** and **5**.
4. Duplicate active ratings for the same order shall not be permitted.
5. Restaurant ratings shall be linked to the corresponding completed order.
6. Restaurant average ratings shall be recalculated after every accepted rating.
7. Rating statistics shall be updated successfully.
8. Audit logs shall be created for every rating transaction.
9. Invalid rating requests shall be rejected with appropriate error messages.
10. The customer shall receive confirmation after successful submission.

#### 3.10.1.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to submit restaurant ratings.
2. Authorization checks shall verify ownership of the completed order.
3. Customer identity shall be validated before processing the request.
4. All communications shall use secure encrypted channels (HTTPS/TLS).
5. Rating requests shall be protected against replay and duplicate submission attacks.
6. Input data shall be validated to prevent injection attacks.
7. Fraud detection mechanisms shall identify suspicious rating activities.
8. Personally identifiable information (PII) shall be protected according to platform privacy policies.
9. Rating operations shall comply with applicable data protection regulations.
10. All security-related events shall be recorded for monitoring and investigation.

#### 3.10.1.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information for every restaurant rating transaction:

- Audit Log ID
- Rating ID
- Restaurant ID
- Order ID
- Customer ID
- Rating Value
- Action Performed (Create/Update)
- Submission Source
- Device Type
- IP Address
- Timestamp
- Processing Status
- Fraud Detection Result
- Request Correlation ID
- System Component
- User Session Identifier

#### 3.10.1.16 Assumptions

1. Restaurant ratings are permitted only for eligible completed orders.
2. Customers act on their own behalf while submitting ratings.
3. Restaurant information exists and is maintained accurately.
4. The Review & Rating Service is available during the submission process.
5. System clocks are synchronized across platform services.
6. Downstream analytics services process rating updates asynchronously where applicable.

#### 3.10.1.17 Dependencies

- Customer Management Module
- Restaurant Management Module
- Order Management Module
- Authentication & Authorization Module
- Review & Rating Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

### 3.10.2 Review Restaurant

#### 3.10.2.1 Description

The **Review Restaurant** use case enables an eligible customer to submit a detailed textual review for a restaurant after the successful completion of an order. The review allows customers to share their overall dining experience, including food quality, taste, freshness, packaging, hygiene, staff behavior, delivery coordination, pricing, and overall satisfaction. The submitted review helps other customers make informed decisions while providing valuable feedback to restaurants for service improvement. All reviews are subject to platform validation, moderation, fraud detection, and content policies before being publicly displayed.

#### 3.10.2.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Restaurant
- Review & Rating Service
- Order Management Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.2.3 Preconditions

1. Customer must be registered on the platform.
2. Customer must be authenticated.
3. Customer account must be active.
4. Customer must have successfully completed an order.
5. The completed order must belong to the authenticated customer.
6. The restaurant must exist in the platform.
7. The restaurant must be active.
8. The review submission window must be valid.
9. Customer must satisfy review eligibility criteria.
10. Customer must not be suspended from submitting reviews.
11. Customer must comply with community guidelines.
12. The completed order must not be cancelled.
13. The completed order must not be permanently deleted.
14. Review service must be operational.
15. Content moderation service must be available.
16. Fraud detection service must be operational.
17. Database connectivity must be available.
18. Audit logging service must be available.

#### 3.10.2.4 Trigger

The customer selects **"Write Review"** from the completed order details, restaurant page, or order history.

#### 3.10.2.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Restaurant ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the restaurant. |
| Order ID | UUID | Yes | Completed order associated with the review. |
| Customer ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Review Title | String | No | Short title summarizing the review. |
| Review Description | Text | Yes | Detailed restaurant review submitted by the customer. |
| Review Language | String | System Generated | Detected language of the review. |
| Contains Images | Boolean | System Generated | Indicates whether review images are attached. |
| Submission Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Date and time of review submission. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Device Identifier | String | System Generated | Unique device identifier. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| App Version | String | System Generated | Application version. |
| Submission Source | Enum | System Generated | Order Details, Order History, Restaurant Page, Notification. |
| Moderation Status | Enum | System Generated | Pending, Approved, Rejected, Hidden. |
| Fraud Check Status | Enum | System Generated | Passed, Flagged, Pending. |

#### 3.10.2.6 Business Rules

1. Only verified customers shall be allowed to submit restaurant reviews.
2. Reviews shall always be associated with a completed order.
3. Cancelled orders shall not be eligible for reviews.
4. Only one active review shall be allowed per completed order.
5. Customers may edit reviews only within the configured edit window.
6. Reviews shall comply with platform community guidelines.
7. Reviews shall not contain abusive language.
8. Reviews shall not contain hate speech.
9. Reviews shall not contain discriminatory content.
10. Reviews shall not contain threats or harassment.
11. Reviews shall not contain sexually explicit content.
12. Reviews shall not contain illegal content.
13. Reviews shall not expose another person's personal information.
14. Reviews shall not contain promotional advertisements.
15. Reviews shall not contain external URLs unless permitted by platform policy.
16. Reviews shall pass profanity filtering before publication.
17. Reviews shall pass spam detection checks.
18. Duplicate review content shall be identified.
19. AI-assisted moderation may classify suspicious reviews.
20. Flagged reviews may require manual moderation.
21. Hidden reviews shall not be publicly displayed.
22. Approved reviews shall contribute to restaurant review statistics.
23. Rejected reviews shall not appear publicly.
24. Restaurants shall not modify customer reviews.
25. Administrators may moderate reviews according to platform policies.
26. Every review shall be linked to the customer, restaurant, and completed order.
27. Every review submission shall be recorded in the audit log.
28. Review statistics shall be updated after approval.
29. Review analytics shall be refreshed after successful processing.
30. Review search indexes shall be updated after publication.
31. Notifications may be generated after successful review submission.
32. Review processing shall maintain transactional consistency.
33. Customer privacy shall be protected throughout processing.
34. Review data shall comply with applicable data protection regulations.
35. All review operations shall comply with platform security, privacy, moderation, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.2.7 Validations

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Restaurant ID shall exist.
4. Order ID shall exist.
5. Order shall belong to the authenticated customer.
6. Order status shall be Delivered.
7. Restaurant shall be active.
8. Review submission window shall be valid.
9. Customer shall satisfy review eligibility criteria.
10. Review Description shall be mandatory.
11. Review Description shall not be empty.
12. Review Description shall satisfy minimum character requirements.
13. Review Description shall not exceed maximum allowed characters.
14. Review Title shall not exceed configured character limits.
15. HTML tags shall not be accepted.
16. Script injection attempts shall be rejected.
17. SQL injection attempts shall be rejected.
18. Cross-site scripting (XSS) content shall be sanitized.
19. Review shall pass profanity validation.
20. Review shall pass spam detection.
21. Duplicate reviews shall be rejected according to platform policy.
22. Invalid authentication tokens shall be rejected.
23. Blocked customer accounts shall not submit reviews.
24. Fraud detection validation shall complete successfully.
25. Database transaction shall complete successfully.
26. Audit log creation shall succeed.
27. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
28. Failed validation shall prevent review persistence and return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.2.8 Main Flow

1. The customer navigates to the **Completed Orders** section.
2. The system displays the customer's delivered orders.
3. The customer selects a completed order.
4. The system retrieves the associated restaurant details.
5. The customer selects **"Write Review."**
6. The system verifies that the customer is eligible to submit a review.
7. The system validates that the selected order belongs to the authenticated customer.
8. The system verifies that the review submission window is still active.
9. The system checks whether an active restaurant review already exists for the selected order.
10. If an existing review is found, the system redirects the customer to the **Edit Review** flow when permitted.
11. Otherwise, the system displays the restaurant review form.
12. The customer optionally enters a review title.
13. The customer enters the review description.
14. The customer optionally attaches review images.
15. The customer reviews the entered information.
16. The customer submits the review.
17. The system receives the review request.
18. The system authenticates the customer session.
19. The system validates all mandatory fields.
20. The system validates the review title and description length.
21. The system sanitizes the submitted content.
22. The system performs profanity detection.
23. The system performs spam detection.
24. The system performs fraud detection.
25. The system performs AI-assisted content moderation.
26. The system verifies duplicate review submission rules.
27. The system determines the review moderation status.
28. The system stores the review.
29. The system associates the review with the customer.
30. The system associates the review with the restaurant.
31. The system associates the review with the completed order.
32. The system stores uploaded review images, if any.
33. The system updates review analytics and search indexes.
34. The system records the activity in the audit log and triggers notifications.
35. The system displays a confirmation message indicating that the restaurant review has been submitted successfully.

#### 3.10.2.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Existing Review Found**

1. The system detects an existing review for the selected order.
2. If editing is permitted, the customer is redirected to the **Edit Review** use case.
3. Otherwise, the system informs the customer that another review cannot be submitted.

---

**AF-2: Review Window Expired**

1. The configured review submission period has expired.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer is informed that the review submission period has ended.

---

**AF-3: Inappropriate Content Detected**

1. The moderation engine detects prohibited content.
2. The review is rejected or placed under manual moderation.
3. The customer receives an appropriate validation message.

---

**AF-4: Duplicate Review Detected**

1. The submitted review is identified as substantially similar to an existing review.
2. The system prevents duplicate submission according to platform policy.
3. The customer is notified accordingly.

---

**AF-5: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. The fraud detection engine flags the review.
2. The review status is changed to **Pending Moderation**.
3. The review remains hidden until moderation is completed.

---

**AF-6: Image Upload Failure**

1. The review is successfully validated.
2. One or more attached images fail to upload.
3. The system handles the failure according to platform policy and informs the customer.

---

**AF-7: Authentication Session Expired**

1. The customer session expires during submission.
2. The review request is rejected.
3. The customer is redirected to the login page.

---

**AF-8: Database Transaction Failure**

1. A database failure occurs while saving the review.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. No partial data is persisted.

---

**AF-9: Dependent Service Failure**

1. The moderation, analytics, or notification service is temporarily unavailable.
2. The review processing follows configured fallback or retry mechanisms.
3. The customer receives an appropriate status message.

---

**AF-10: Audit Logging Failure**

1. The review is successfully processed.
2. Audit log generation fails.
3. The failure is recorded through platform monitoring for administrative investigation.

#### 3.10.2.10 Postconditions

1. The restaurant review is stored successfully.
2. The review is associated with the customer, restaurant, and completed order.
3. The moderation status is assigned.
4. Review images are linked to the review, if uploaded.
5. Review statistics are updated according to moderation status.
6. Search indexes are refreshed where applicable.
7. Analytics data is updated.
8. Notifications are triggered, if applicable.
9. Audit logs are created.
10. The customer's review history is updated.

#### 3.10.2.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK / 201 Created |
| Message | Restaurant review submitted successfully. |
| Review ID | Unique identifier of the submitted review. |
| Restaurant ID | Restaurant identifier. |
| Order ID | Associated completed order. |
| Moderation Status | Pending, Approved, or Hidden. |
| Submission Time | Review submission timestamp. |

#### 3.10.2.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid request data. |
| 401 | Customer authentication failed. |
| 403 | Customer is not eligible to submit a review. |
| 404 | Restaurant or order not found. |
| 409 | Review already exists for the selected order. |
| 410 | Review submission period has expired. |
| 413 | Review content exceeds the permitted size. |
| 422 | Review content violates platform policies. |
| 429 | Too many review requests submitted. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Review service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.2.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authenticated eligible customers shall be allowed to submit reviews.
2. Reviews shall be accepted only for completed orders.
3. Mandatory review content shall be validated successfully.
4. Invalid or prohibited content shall not be published.
5. Duplicate reviews shall be prevented.
6. Review moderation status shall be assigned correctly.
7. Review images shall be associated successfully when uploaded.
8. Review analytics shall be updated after successful processing.
9. Audit logs shall be generated for every review submission.
10. Customers shall receive confirmation after successful submission.

#### 3.10.2.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to submit reviews.
2. Authorization shall verify ownership of the completed order.
3. Review content shall be sanitized before storage.
4. SQL Injection and XSS attacks shall be prevented.
5. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
6. All communications shall use HTTPS/TLS.
7. Fraud detection mechanisms shall monitor suspicious review activity.
8. Review moderation shall enforce community guidelines.
9. Uploaded images shall undergo malware and file validation.
10. Security events shall be recorded for monitoring and investigation.

#### 3.10.2.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information:

- Audit Log ID
- Review ID
- Restaurant ID
- Order ID
- Customer ID
- Review Title
- Review Submission Timestamp
- Moderation Status
- Fraud Detection Result
- Device Type

#### 3.10.2.16 Assumptions

1. Only verified completed orders are eligible for restaurant reviews.
2. Community guidelines are published and accepted by customers.
3. Moderation services remain operational.
4. Fraud detection policies are configured.
5. System clocks are synchronized.
6. Analytics processing may occur asynchronously.

- Customer Management Module
- Restaurant Management Module
- Order Management Module
- Authentication & Authorization Module

### 3.10.3 Rate Food Item

#### 3.10.3.1 Description

The **Rate Food Item** use case enables an eligible customer to submit a star rating for an individual food item purchased through a successfully completed order. Food item ratings help customers evaluate menu quality, taste, freshness, portion size, packaging, presentation, and value for money. The submitted rating contributes to the food item's overall rating, improves menu recommendations, supports restaurant quality analysis, and assists future customers in making informed purchasing decisions. Only verified purchases are eligible for food item ratings.

#### 3.10.3.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Restaurant
- Menu Management Service
- Review & Rating Service
- Order Management Service
- Recommendation Service
- Analytics Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.3.3 Preconditions

1. Customer must be registered on the platform.
2. Customer must be authenticated.
3. Customer account must be active.
4. Customer must have successfully completed an order.
5. The completed order must belong to the authenticated customer.
6. The order must contain the selected food item.
7. The selected food item must exist.
8. The selected food item must be active.
9. The associated restaurant must exist.
10. The associated restaurant must be active.
11. The rating submission window must be valid.
12. Customer must satisfy rating eligibility rules.
13. Customer must not be restricted from submitting ratings.
14. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
15. Menu Management Service must be operational.
16. Database connectivity must be available.
17. Analytics Service must be operational.
18. Audit Logging Service must be available.

#### 3.10.3.4 Trigger

The customer selects **"Rate Food Item"** from the completed order details, order history, or purchased menu items section.

#### 3.10.3.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Food Item ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the food item. |
| Restaurant ID | UUID | Yes | Identifier of the restaurant serving the food item. |
| Order ID | UUID | Yes | Completed order associated with the rating. |
| Customer ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Rating Value | Integer | Yes | Rating provided for the food item (1–5 stars). |
| Rating Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Date and time of submission. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Device Identifier | String | System Generated | Device identifier. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | QuickBite application version. |
| Submission Source | Enum | System Generated | Order Details, Order History, Menu History, Notification. |
| Eligibility Status | Boolean | System Generated | Indicates whether the customer is eligible to rate the food item. |
| Verification Status | Enum | System Generated | Verified, Pending Verification, or Rejected. |
| Rating Status | Enum | System Generated | Submitted, Accepted, Pending Moderation, or Rejected. |
| Remarks | String | No | Optional internal remarks generated during validation or moderation. |

#### 3.10.3.6 Business Rules

1. Only verified customers who purchased the food item shall be allowed to rate it.
2. Food item ratings shall always be associated with a completed order.
3. Cancelled orders shall not be eligible for food item ratings.
4. Refunded orders shall follow platform rating policies.
5. Each purchased food item shall allow only one active rating per completed order.
6. Customers may update ratings within the configured edit period.
7. Rating modifications shall not be permitted after the edit window expires.
8. The rating value shall be an integer between **1** and **5**.
9. Fractional ratings shall not be accepted.
10. Ratings shall be submitted only within the configured rating period.
11. Accepted ratings shall contribute to the food item's average rating.
12. Food item rating statistics shall be recalculated after every accepted rating.
13. Fraudulent ratings shall be excluded from public statistics.
14. Ratings shall only be accepted from authenticated sessions.
15. Duplicate rating submissions shall not be permitted.
16. Restaurants shall not modify customer ratings.
17. Administrators may moderate ratings according to platform policies.
18. Every rating shall be linked to the customer, food item, restaurant, and completed order.
19. Rating timestamps shall be generated by the system.
20. Every rating submission shall be recorded in the audit log.
21. Rating failures shall also be logged.
22. Customer eligibility shall be verified before processing.
23. Fraud detection shall analyze suspicious rating activity.
24. Hidden ratings shall not affect public rating calculations.
25. Rating submission shall not modify the order lifecycle.
26. Rating processing shall maintain transactional consistency.
27. Customer privacy shall be protected throughout processing.
28. Rating analytics shall be updated after successful processing.
29. Recommendation services may consume approved food item ratings.
30. Restaurant menu statistics shall be refreshed after rating acceptance.
31. Ratings shall comply with platform security policies.
32. Ratings shall comply with privacy regulations.
33. Rating processing shall support future analytical reporting.
34. Rating data shall remain traceable through audit logs.
35. All rating operations shall comply with platform governance, moderation, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.3.7 Validations

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Food Item ID shall exist.
4. Restaurant ID shall exist.
5. Order ID shall exist.
6. Order shall belong to the authenticated customer.
7. Order status shall be Delivered.
8. Selected food item shall exist in the completed order.
9. Food item shall be active.
10. Restaurant shall be active.
11. Customer shall satisfy food item rating eligibility.
12. Rating submission window shall be valid.
13. Rating Value shall be mandatory.
14. Rating Value shall be an integer.
15. Rating Value shall be between **1** and **5** inclusive.
16. Decimal, negative, alphabetic, or special character values shall not be accepted.
17. Customer ID shall match the authenticated session.
18. Duplicate ratings shall be prevented according to platform policy.
19. Invalid authentication tokens shall be rejected.
20. Suspended customer accounts shall not submit ratings.
21. Fraud detection validation shall complete successfully.
22. Database transaction shall complete successfully.
23. Audit log creation shall succeed.
24. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
25. Invalid device or request metadata shall be handled according to security policy.
26. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
27. Rating request shall satisfy platform security checks.
28. Validation failure shall prevent rating persistence and return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.3.8 Main Flow

1. The customer navigates to the **Completed Orders** section.
2. The system displays the customer's delivered orders.
3. The customer selects a completed order.
4. The system retrieves the ordered food items.
5. The customer selects a food item.
6. The customer clicks **"Rate Food Item."**
7. The system verifies that the customer is eligible to rate the selected food item.
8. The system verifies that the selected food item belongs to the completed order.
9. The system checks whether the rating submission window is still valid.
10. The system checks whether an active rating already exists for the selected food item.
11. If editing is permitted, the existing rating is retrieved.
12. Otherwise, the system displays the food item rating interface.
13. The customer selects a rating between **1** and **5** stars.
14. The customer reviews the selected rating.
15. The customer submits the rating.
16. The system receives the rating request.
17. The system authenticates the customer session.
18. The system validates all mandatory request fields.
19. The system validates the submitted rating value.
20. The system verifies duplicate submission rules.
21. The system performs fraud detection.
22. The system performs platform security validation.
23. The system creates or updates the food item rating record according to platform policy.
24. The system records the rating timestamp.
25. The system associates the rating with the customer.
26. The system associates the rating with the completed order.
27. The system associates the rating with the selected food item.
28. The system associates the rating with the restaurant.
29. The system recalculates the food item's average rating.
30. The system updates the total rating count for the food item.
31. The system refreshes recommendation and analytics data.
32. The system records the transaction in the audit log.
33. The system notifies dependent services, if applicable.
34. The system commits the transaction successfully.
35. The system displays a confirmation message indicating that the food item rating has been submitted successfully.

#### 3.10.3.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Existing Rating Found**

1. The system detects an existing rating for the selected food item.
2. If editing is permitted, the customer is redirected to the **Edit Review** use case.
3. Otherwise, the system informs the customer that another rating cannot be submitted.

---

**AF-2: Food Item Not Eligible**

1. The selected food item is not eligible for rating.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer is informed accordingly.

---

**AF-3: Rating Window Expired**

1. The permitted rating submission period has expired.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer is notified with an appropriate validation message.

---

**AF-4: Invalid Rating Value**

1. The submitted rating value fails validation.
2. The system highlights the validation error.
3. The customer is prompted to select a valid rating between **1** and **5**.

---

**AF-5: Food Item Unavailable**

1. During processing, the food item becomes inactive or unavailable.
2. The system terminates the request.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. The fraud detection service flags the rating.
2. The rating is marked as **Pending Moderation** or rejected according to platform policy.
3. The rating is excluded from public statistics until moderation is completed.

---

**AF-7: Authentication Session Expired**

1. The customer session expires before submission completes.
2. The request is rejected.
3. The customer is redirected to the login page.

---

**AF-8: Database Transaction Failure**

1. A database failure occurs while saving the rating.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. No partial data is persisted.
4. The customer is informed to try again later.

---

**AF-9: Dependent Service Failure**

1. Analytics, recommendation, or notification services are temporarily unavailable.
2. The system completes or retries processing according to platform policy.
3. The customer receives an appropriate status message.

---

**AF-10: Audit Logging Failure**

1. The rating is processed successfully.
2. Audit log generation fails.
3. The incident is recorded through internal monitoring for administrative investigation.

#### 3.10.3.10 Postconditions

1. The food item rating shall be successfully stored.
2. The rating shall be associated with the customer, food item, restaurant, and completed order.
3. The food item's average rating shall be recalculated.
4. The total food item rating count shall be updated.
5. Recommendation data shall be refreshed.
6. Analytics data shall be updated.
7. Audit logs shall be created.
8. Rating timestamps shall be recorded.
9. Customer rating history shall be updated.
10. Dependent services shall be notified where applicable.

#### 3.10.3.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK / 201 Created |
| Message | Food item rating submitted successfully. |
| Rating ID | Unique identifier of the submitted rating. |
| Food Item ID | Identifier of the rated food item. |
| Restaurant ID | Associated restaurant identifier. |
| Order ID | Completed order identifier. |
| Rating Value | Submitted rating (1–5 stars). |
| Submission Time | Rating submission timestamp. |

| 401 | Customer authentication failed. |

#### 3.10.3.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to rate purchased food items.
2. Ratings shall be accepted only for completed eligible orders.
3. Only purchased food items shall be eligible for rating.
4. Rating values shall be restricted to **1–5** stars.
5. Duplicate ratings shall be prevented according to platform policy.
6. Average food item ratings shall be recalculated after successful submission.
7. Recommendation and analytics data shall be updated.
8. Audit logs shall be generated for every rating transaction.
9. Invalid requests shall be rejected with appropriate error messages.
10. Customers shall receive confirmation after successful submission.

#### 3.10.3.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to submit food item ratings.
2. Authorization shall verify ownership of the completed order.
3. Rating requests shall be protected against duplicate and replay attacks.
4. All communications shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
5. Input validation shall prevent injection attacks.
6. Fraud detection shall monitor suspicious rating activity.
7. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
8. Rating processing shall comply with platform privacy policies.
9. Security-related events shall be logged.
10. Rating operations shall comply with applicable regulatory requirements.

#### 3.10.3.15 Audit Log Requirements

3. Recommendation services consume approved rating data.
4. Fraud detection policies are configured.
5. Platform services remain operational.

### 3.10.4 Review Food Item

#### 3.10.4.1 Description

The **Review Food Item** use case enables an eligible customer to submit a detailed textual review for an individual food item purchased through a successfully completed order. The review allows customers to share their experience regarding taste, freshness, aroma, texture, quantity, presentation, packaging, temperature, quality, and value for money. The submitted review assists future customers in making informed decisions, helps restaurants improve menu quality, and supports platform recommendations and analytics. All food item reviews are subject to validation, content moderation, fraud detection, and platform community guidelines before being published.

#### 3.10.4.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Restaurant
- Menu Management Service
- Review & Rating Service
- Order Management Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Recommendation Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.4.3 Preconditions

1. Customer must be registered on the platform.
2. Customer must be authenticated.
3. Customer account must be active.
4. Customer must have successfully completed an order.
5. The completed order must belong to the authenticated customer.
6. The completed order must contain the selected food item.
7. The selected food item must exist.
8. The selected food item must be active.
9. The associated restaurant must exist.
10. The associated restaurant must be active.
11. The review submission window must be valid.
12. Customer must satisfy review eligibility criteria.
13. Customer must not be suspended from submitting reviews.
14. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
15. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
16. Fraud Detection Service must be operational.
17. Database connectivity must be available.
18. Audit Logging Service must be available.

| Customer ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated customer identifier. |

| Review Description | Text | Yes | Detailed customer feedback about the food item. |

| Contains Images | Boolean | System Generated | Indicates whether review images are attached. |

| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Device Identifier | String | System Generated | Customer device identifier. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | Application version used during submission. |
| Moderation Status | Enum | System Generated | Pending, Approved, Rejected, or Hidden. |
| Fraud Check Status | Enum | System Generated | Passed, Pending, or Flagged. |

#### 3.10.4.6 Business Rules

1. Only verified customers who purchased the food item shall be allowed to submit reviews.
2. Food item reviews shall always be associated with a completed order.
3. Cancelled orders shall not be eligible for reviews.
4. Each purchased food item shall allow only one active review per completed order.
5. Customers may edit reviews within the configured edit window.
6. Reviews shall comply with platform community guidelines.
7. Reviews shall not contain abusive language.
8. Reviews shall not contain hate speech.
9. Reviews shall not contain discriminatory content.
10. Reviews shall not contain misleading or fraudulent information.
11. Reviews shall not contain advertisements or promotional content.
12. Reviews shall not contain external website links unless permitted.
13. Reviews shall not disclose personal or confidential information.
14. Reviews shall pass profanity detection.
15. Reviews shall pass spam detection.
16. AI-assisted moderation may classify suspicious reviews.
17. Duplicate review content shall be identified.
18. Flagged reviews may require manual moderation.
19. Approved reviews shall be publicly displayed.
20. Hidden or rejected reviews shall not be displayed publicly.
21. Restaurants shall not modify customer reviews.
22. Administrators may moderate reviews according to platform policy.
23. Every review shall be linked to the customer, food item, restaurant, and completed order.
24. Every review submission shall be recorded in the audit log.
25. Review analytics shall be updated after approval.
26. Food item review statistics shall be refreshed after approval.
27. Search indexes shall be updated after publication.
28. Recommendation services may consume approved review data.
29. Notifications may be generated after successful submission.
30. Review processing shall maintain transactional consistency.
31. Customer privacy shall be protected during review processing.
32. Review data shall comply with applicable privacy regulations.
33. Fraudulent reviews shall be excluded from recommendation engines.
34. Review history shall remain available for audit purposes.
35. All review operations shall comply with platform security, moderation, governance, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.4.7 Validations

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Food Item ID shall exist.
4. Restaurant ID shall exist.
5. Order ID shall exist.
6. Order shall belong to the authenticated customer.
7. Order status shall be Delivered.
8. Selected food item shall exist in the completed order.
9. Food item shall be active.
10. Restaurant shall be active.
11. Customer shall satisfy review eligibility rules.
12. Review submission window shall be valid.
13. Review Description shall be mandatory.
14. Review Description shall not be empty.
15. Review Description shall satisfy the minimum character limit.
16. Review Description shall not exceed the maximum configured length.
17. Review Title shall not exceed the configured character limit.
18. HTML tags shall not be accepted.
19. Script injection attempts shall be rejected.
20. SQL injection attempts shall be rejected.
21. Cross-site scripting (XSS) content shall be sanitized.
22. Review shall pass profanity detection.
23. Review shall pass spam detection.
24. Duplicate review validation shall succeed.
25. Fraud detection validation shall complete successfully.
26. Database transaction shall complete successfully.
27. Audit log creation shall succeed.
28. Validation failure shall prevent review persistence and return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.4.8 Main Flow

1. The customer navigates to the **Completed Orders** section.
2. The system displays the customer's delivered orders.
3. The customer selects a completed order.
4. The system retrieves the purchased food items.
5. The customer selects a food item.
6. The customer clicks **"Write Review."**
7. The system verifies that the customer is eligible to review the selected food item.
8. The system verifies that the food item belongs to the completed order.
9. The system checks whether the review submission window is still active.
10. The system checks whether an active review already exists for the selected food item.
11. If an existing review is found and editing is permitted, the system redirects the customer to the **Edit Review** use case.
12. Otherwise, the system displays the food item review form.
13. The customer optionally enters a review title.
14. The customer enters the review description.
15. The customer optionally uploads review images.
16. The customer reviews the entered information.
17. The customer submits the review.
18. The system receives the review request.
19. The system authenticates the customer session.
20. The system validates all mandatory fields.
21. The system validates the review title and description length.
22. The system sanitizes the submitted content.
23. The system performs profanity detection.
24. The system performs spam detection.
25. The system performs fraud detection.
26. The system performs AI-assisted content moderation.
27. The system verifies duplicate review rules.
28. The system determines the review moderation status.
29. The system stores the review.
30. The system associates the review with the customer, food item, restaurant, and completed order.
31. The system stores uploaded review images, if any.
32. The system updates food item review statistics, recommendation data, and analytics.
33. The system updates search indexes for approved reviews.
34. The system records the transaction in the audit log and triggers notifications.
35. The system displays a confirmation message indicating that the food item review has been submitted successfully.

#### 3.10.4.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Existing Review Found**

1. The system detects an existing review for the selected food item.
2. If editing is permitted, the customer is redirected to the **Edit Review** use case.
3. Otherwise, the system informs the customer that another review cannot be submitted.

---

**AF-2: Food Item Not Eligible for Review**

1. The selected food item does not satisfy the review eligibility criteria.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer receives an appropriate validation message.

---

**AF-3: Review Submission Window Expired**

1. The configured review submission period has expired.
2. The review request is rejected.
3. The customer is informed accordingly.

---

**AF-4: Inappropriate Content Detected**

1. The moderation engine detects prohibited content.
2. The review is rejected or moved to manual moderation.
3. The customer is notified with an appropriate validation message.

---

**AF-5: Duplicate Review Detected**

1. The submitted review is identified as duplicate content.
2. The system prevents duplicate review submission.
3. The customer is informed according to platform policy.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. The fraud detection engine flags the submitted review.
2. The review status becomes **Pending Moderation**.
3. The review remains hidden until moderation is completed.

---

**AF-7: Image Upload Failure**

1. The review is successfully validated.
2. One or more attached images fail to upload.
3. The system processes the failure according to platform policy and informs the customer.

---

**AF-8: Authentication Session Expired**

1. The customer's authentication session expires during submission.
2. The review request is rejected.
3. The customer is redirected to the login page.

---

**AF-9: Database Transaction Failure**

1. A database error occurs while saving the review.
2. The transaction is rolled back completely.
3. No partial data is stored.

---

**AF-10: Audit Logging Failure**

1. The review is successfully processed.
2. Audit log creation fails.
3. The failure is recorded through platform monitoring for administrative investigation.

6. Recommendation data shall be refreshed.

### 3.10.5 Rate Delivery Partner

#### 3.10.5.1 Description

The **Rate Delivery Partner** use case enables an eligible customer to submit a star rating for the delivery partner after the successful completion of an order. The rating reflects the customer's delivery experience, including punctuality, professionalism, communication, behavior, order handling, navigation efficiency, and overall service quality. The submitted rating contributes to the delivery partner's overall performance score, supports incentive calculations, improves service quality, and assists the platform in monitoring delivery performance. Only verified completed deliveries are eligible for delivery partner ratings.

#### 3.10.5.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Delivery Management Service
- Review & Rating Service

2. Customer must be authenticated.
3. Customer account must be active.
4. Customer must have successfully completed an order.
5. The completed order must belong to the authenticated customer.
6. The order must have been delivered by the selected delivery partner.
7. The delivery partner must exist.
8. The delivery partner must be active.
9. The delivery partner must be assigned to the completed order.
10. The rating submission window must be valid.
11. Customer must satisfy rating eligibility criteria.
12. Customer must not be restricted from submitting ratings.
13. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
14. Delivery Management Service must be operational.
15. Performance Management Service must be operational.
16. Database connectivity must be available.
17. Analytics Service must be operational.
18. Audit Logging Service must be available.

#### 3.10.5.4 Trigger

The customer selects **"Rate Delivery Partner"** from the completed order details or delivery summary screen.

| Eligibility Status | Boolean | System Generated | Indicates customer eligibility. |
| Verification Status | Enum | System Generated | Verified, Pending Verification, or Rejected. |
| Rating Status | Enum | System Generated | Submitted, Accepted, Pending Moderation, or Rejected. |
| Remarks | String | No | Internal validation or moderation remarks. |

#### 3.10.5.6 Business Rules

1. Only verified customers who received the delivery shall be allowed to rate the delivery partner.
2. Delivery partner ratings shall always be associated with a completed order.
3. Cancelled orders shall not be eligible for delivery partner ratings.
4. Failed delivery attempts shall not be eligible unless permitted by platform policy.
5. Each completed order shall allow only one active delivery partner rating.
6. Customers may update ratings within the configured edit period.
7. Rating modifications shall not be permitted after the edit window expires.
8. Rating values shall be integers between **1** and **5**.
9. Fractional ratings shall not be accepted.
10. Ratings shall be accepted only during the configured submission period.
11. Accepted ratings shall contribute to the delivery partner's overall rating.
12. Delivery partner performance statistics shall be recalculated after every accepted rating.
13. Fraudulent ratings shall be excluded from public statistics.
14. Ratings shall only be accepted from authenticated sessions.
15. Duplicate rating submissions shall not be permitted.
16. Delivery partners shall not modify customer ratings.
17. Administrators may moderate ratings according to platform policy.
18. Every rating shall be linked to the customer, delivery partner, restaurant, and completed order.
19. Rating timestamps shall be generated by the system.
20. Every rating submission shall be recorded in the audit log.
21. Rating failures shall also be logged.
22. Customer eligibility shall be verified before processing.
23. Fraud detection shall analyze suspicious rating activity.
24. Hidden ratings shall not affect public performance metrics.
25. Rating submission shall not modify the delivery lifecycle.
26. Rating processing shall maintain transactional consistency.
27. Customer privacy shall be protected throughout processing.
28. Performance dashboards shall be updated after successful processing.
29. Analytics services shall consume approved rating data.
30. Notifications may be generated after successful submission.
31. Ratings shall comply with platform security policies.
32. Ratings shall comply with applicable privacy regulations.
33. Rating data shall support operational reporting.
34. Rating history shall remain available for auditing.
35. All rating operations shall comply with platform governance, moderation, security, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.5.7 Validations

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Delivery Partner ID shall exist.
4. Restaurant ID shall exist.
5. Order ID shall exist.
6. Order shall belong to the authenticated customer.
7. Order status shall be Delivered.
8. Delivery partner shall be assigned to the completed order.
9. Delivery partner shall be active.
10. Customer shall satisfy rating eligibility rules.
11. Rating submission window shall be valid.
12. Rating Value shall be mandatory.
13. Rating Value shall be an integer.
14. Rating Value shall be between **1** and **5** inclusive.
15. Decimal, negative, alphabetic, or special character values shall not be accepted.
16. Customer ID shall match the authenticated session.
17. Duplicate rating validation shall succeed.
18. Invalid authentication tokens shall be rejected.
19. Suspended customer accounts shall not submit ratings.
20. Fraud detection validation shall complete successfully.
21. Database transaction shall complete successfully.
22. Audit log creation shall succeed.
23. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
24. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
25. Invalid device or request metadata shall be handled according to security policy.
26. Platform security validation shall complete successfully.
27. Dependent service validation shall succeed before persistence.
28. Validation failure shall prevent rating persistence and return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.5.8 Main Flow

1. The customer navigates to the **Completed Orders** section.
2. The system displays the customer's delivered orders.
3. The customer selects a completed order.
4. The system retrieves the associated delivery details.
5. The customer selects the assigned delivery partner.
6. The customer clicks **"Rate Delivery Partner."**
7. The system verifies that the customer is eligible to rate the delivery partner.
8. The system verifies that the selected delivery partner completed the delivery.
9. The system checks whether the rating submission window is still valid.
10. The system checks whether an active delivery partner rating already exists for the selected order.
11. If editing is permitted, the existing rating is retrieved.
12. Otherwise, the system displays the delivery partner rating interface.
13. The customer selects a rating between **1** and **5** stars.
14. The customer reviews the selected rating.
15. The customer submits the rating.
16. The system receives the rating request.
17. The system authenticates the customer session.
18. The system validates all mandatory request fields.
19. The system validates the submitted rating value.
20. The system verifies duplicate rating rules.
21. The system performs fraud detection.
22. The system performs platform security validation.
23. The system creates or updates the delivery partner rating according to platform policy.
24. The system records the rating timestamp.
25. The system associates the rating with the customer.
26. The system associates the rating with the delivery partner.
27. The system associates the rating with the completed order.
28. The system associates the rating with the restaurant.
29. The system recalculates the delivery partner's average rating.
30. The system updates the delivery partner's total rating count and performance metrics.
31. The system refreshes analytics and operational dashboards.
32. The system records the transaction in the audit log.
33. The system notifies dependent services, if applicable.
34. The system commits the transaction successfully.
35. The system displays a confirmation message indicating that the delivery partner rating has been submitted successfully.

#### 3.10.5.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Existing Rating Found**

1. The system detects an existing rating for the selected delivery partner.
2. If editing is permitted, the customer is redirected to the **Edit Review** use case.
3. Otherwise, the system informs the customer that another rating cannot be submitted.

---

**AF-2: Delivery Partner Not Eligible**

1. The selected delivery partner is not eligible for rating.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer receives an appropriate validation message.

---

**AF-3: Rating Submission Window Expired**

1. The configured rating submission period has expired.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer is informed that the submission window has expired.

---

**AF-4: Invalid Rating Value**

1. The submitted rating value fails validation.
2. The system highlights the validation error.
3. The customer is prompted to select a valid rating between **1** and **5**.

---

**AF-5: Delivery Partner No Longer Active**

1. During processing, the delivery partner becomes inactive.
2. The system terminates the request.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. The fraud detection engine flags the submitted rating.
2. The rating is marked as **Pending Moderation** or rejected according to platform policy.
3. The rating is excluded from public performance metrics until moderation is completed.

---

**AF-7: Authentication Session Expired**

1. The customer's authentication session expires before submission completes.
2. The request is rejected.
3. The customer is redirected to the login page.

---

**AF-8: Database Transaction Failure**

1. A database failure occurs while saving the rating.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. No partial data is stored.
4. The customer is informed to try again later.

---

**AF-9: Dependent Service Failure**

1. Analytics, performance management, or notification services are temporarily unavailable.
2. The system follows configured retry or fallback mechanisms.
3. The customer receives an appropriate status message.

---

**AF-10: Audit Logging Failure**

1. The rating is processed successfully.
2. Audit log creation fails.
3. The incident is recorded through platform monitoring for administrative investigation.

#### 3.10.5.10 Postconditions

1. The delivery partner rating shall be successfully stored.
2. The rating shall be associated with the customer, delivery partner, restaurant, and completed order.
3. The delivery partner's average rating shall be recalculated.
4. The delivery partner's total rating count shall be updated.
5. Performance metrics shall be refreshed.
6. Analytics data shall be updated.
7. Operational dashboards shall be refreshed.
8. Audit logs shall be generated.
9. Customer rating history shall be updated.
10. Dependent services shall be notified where applicable.

#### 3.10.5.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK / 201 Created |
| Message | Delivery partner rating submitted successfully. |
| Rating ID | Unique identifier of the submitted rating. |
| Delivery Partner ID | Identifier of the rated delivery partner. |
| Restaurant ID | Associated restaurant identifier. |
| Order ID | Completed order identifier. |
| Rating Value | Submitted rating (1–5 stars). |
| Submission Time | Rating submission timestamp. |
| Updated Average Rating | Latest calculated delivery partner rating. |
| Total Ratings | Updated number of delivery partner ratings. |

#### 3.10.5.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid request data. |
| 401 | Customer authentication failed. |
| 403 | Customer is not eligible to rate the delivery partner. |
| 404 | Delivery partner or order not found. |
| 409 | Delivery partner rating already exists for the selected order. |
| 410 | Rating submission window has expired. |
| 422 | Invalid rating value. |
| 429 | Too many rating requests submitted. |
| 500 | Internal server error occurred while processing the request. |
| 503 | Rating service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.5.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to rate delivery partners.
2. Ratings shall be accepted only for successfully completed deliveries.
3. Only assigned delivery partners shall be eligible for rating.
4. Rating values shall be restricted to **1–5** stars.
5. Duplicate ratings shall be prevented according to platform policy.
6. Performance metrics shall be updated after successful submission.
7. Analytics dashboards shall be refreshed.
8. Audit logs shall be generated for every rating transaction.
9. Invalid requests shall be rejected with appropriate error messages.
10. Customers shall receive confirmation after successful submission.

#### 3.10.5.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to submit delivery partner ratings.
2. Authorization shall verify ownership of the completed order.
3. Rating requests shall be protected against replay and duplicate attacks.
4. All communications shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
5. Fraud detection shall monitor suspicious rating activity.
6. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
7. Platform security policies shall be enforced throughout processing.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Rating processing shall comply with privacy regulations.
10. Sensitive operational data shall not be exposed to unauthorized users.

#### 3.10.5.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information:

- Order ID

- Rating Value

- IP Address

1. Only verified completed deliveries are eligible for customer ratings.
2. Delivery partner assignment records are maintained accurately.
3. Performance management services consume approved rating data.
4. Fraud detection policies are configured.
5. Platform services remain operational.
6. Analytics processing may occur asynchronously.
