### 3.10.6 Review Delivery Partner

#### 3.10.6.1 Description

The **Review Delivery Partner** use case enables an eligible customer to submit a detailed textual review about the delivery partner after the successful completion of an order. The review captures the customer's experience regarding professionalism, communication, punctuality, behavior, order handling, navigation efficiency, appearance, and overall delivery service. The submitted review helps improve delivery quality, assists the platform in evaluating delivery partner performance, and provides valuable operational insights. All reviews are subject to validation, content moderation, fraud detection, and community guidelines before publication.

#### 3.10.6.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Delivery Partner
- Delivery Management Service
- Review & Rating Service
- Order Management Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Performance Management Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.6.3 Preconditions

1. Customer must be registered on the platform.
2. Customer must be authenticated.
3. Customer account must be active.
4. Customer must have successfully completed an order.
5. The completed order must belong to the authenticated customer.
6. The order must have been delivered by the selected delivery partner.
7. The delivery partner must exist.
8. The delivery partner must be active.
9. The delivery partner must be assigned to the completed order.
10. The review submission window must be valid.
11. Customer must satisfy review eligibility criteria.
12. Customer must not be suspended from submitting reviews.
13. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
14. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
15. Fraud Detection Service must be operational.
16. Performance Management Service must be operational.
17. Database connectivity must be available.
18. Audit Logging Service must be available.

#### 3.10.6.4 Trigger

The customer selects **"Write Review"** for the delivery partner from the completed order details or delivery summary page.

#### 3.10.6.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Delivery Partner ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the delivery partner. |
| Restaurant ID | UUID | Yes | Restaurant associated with the completed order. |
| Order ID | UUID | Yes | Completed order associated with the review. |
| Customer ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Review Title | String | No | Short summary of the delivery experience. |
| Review Description | Text | Yes | Detailed feedback about the delivery partner. |
| Review Language | String | System Generated | Detected language of the review. |
| Contains Images | Boolean | System Generated | Indicates whether review images are attached. |
| Submission Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Date and time of review submission. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Device Identifier | String | System Generated | Customer device identifier. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | Application version used during submission. |
| Moderation Status | Enum | System Generated | Pending, Approved, Rejected, or Hidden. |
| Fraud Check Status | Enum | System Generated | Passed, Pending, or Flagged. |

#### 3.10.6.6 Business Rules

1. Only verified customers who received the delivery shall be allowed to submit delivery partner reviews.
2. Reviews shall always be associated with a completed order.
3. Cancelled orders shall not be eligible for reviews.
4. Each completed order shall allow only one active delivery partner review.
5. Customers may edit reviews within the configured edit period.
6. Reviews shall comply with platform community guidelines.
7. Reviews shall not contain abusive language.
8. Reviews shall not contain hate speech.
9. Reviews shall not contain discriminatory content.
10. Reviews shall not contain threats or harassment.
11. Reviews shall not disclose personal or confidential information.
12. Reviews shall not contain promotional content or advertisements.
13. Reviews shall not contain external website links unless permitted by platform policy.
14. Reviews shall pass profanity detection.
15. Reviews shall pass spam detection.
16. AI-assisted moderation may classify suspicious reviews.
17. Duplicate review content shall be identified.
18. Flagged reviews may require manual moderation.
19. Approved reviews shall be publicly displayed.
20. Hidden or rejected reviews shall not be displayed publicly.
21. Delivery partners shall not modify customer reviews.
22. Administrators may moderate reviews according to platform policies.
23. Every review shall be linked to the customer, delivery partner, restaurant, and completed order.
24. Every review submission shall be recorded in the audit log.
25. Delivery partner review statistics shall be updated after approval.
26. Performance metrics shall be refreshed after review approval.
27. Analytics services shall consume approved review data.
28. Search indexes shall be updated after publication.
29. Notifications may be generated after successful submission.
30. Review processing shall maintain transactional consistency.
31. Customer privacy shall be protected during review processing.
32. Fraudulent reviews shall be excluded from performance analytics.
33. Review history shall remain available for audit purposes.
34. Review data shall comply with applicable privacy regulations.
35. All review operations shall comply with platform governance, moderation, security, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.6.7 Validations

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Delivery Partner ID shall exist.
4. Restaurant ID shall exist.
5. Order ID shall exist.
6. Order shall belong to the authenticated customer.
7. Order status shall be Delivered.
8. Delivery partner shall be assigned to the completed order.
9. Delivery partner shall be active.
10. Customer shall satisfy review eligibility rules.
11. Review submission window shall be valid.
12. Review Description shall be mandatory.
13. Review Description shall not be empty.
14. Review Description shall satisfy the minimum character limit.
15. Review Description shall not exceed the maximum configured character limit.
16. Review Title shall not exceed the configured character limit.
17. HTML tags shall not be accepted.
18. Script injection attempts shall be rejected.
19. SQL injection attempts shall be rejected.
20. Cross-site scripting (XSS) content shall be sanitized.
21. Review shall pass profanity detection.
22. Review shall pass spam detection.
23. Duplicate review validation shall succeed.
24. Fraud detection validation shall complete successfully.
25. Database transaction shall complete successfully.
26. Audit log creation shall succeed.
27. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
28. Validation failure shall prevent review persistence and return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.6.8 Main Flow

1. The customer navigates to the **Completed Orders** section.
2. The system displays the customer's delivered orders.
3. The customer selects a completed order.
4. The system retrieves the delivery information associated with the order.
5. The customer selects the assigned delivery partner.
6. The customer clicks **"Write Review."**
7. The system verifies that the customer is eligible to review the delivery partner.
8. The system verifies that the selected delivery partner completed the delivery.
9. The system checks whether the review submission window is still active.
10. The system checks whether an active review already exists for the selected delivery partner and order.
11. If an existing review is found and editing is permitted, the system redirects the customer to the **Edit Review** use case.
12. Otherwise, the system displays the delivery partner review form.
13. The customer optionally enters a review title.
14. The customer enters the review description.
15. The customer optionally uploads review images.
16. The customer reviews the entered information.
17. The customer submits the review.
18. The system receives the review request.
19. The system authenticates the customer session.
20. The system validates all mandatory fields.
21. The system validates the review title and description length.
22. The system sanitizes the submitted content.
23. The system performs profanity detection.
24. The system performs spam detection.
25. The system performs fraud detection.
26. The system performs AI-assisted content moderation.
27. The system verifies duplicate review rules.
28. The system determines the review moderation status.
29. The system stores the review.
30. The system associates the review with the customer, delivery partner, restaurant, and completed order.
31. The system stores uploaded review images, if any.
32. The system updates delivery partner review statistics, performance metrics, and analytics.
33. The system updates search indexes for approved reviews.
34. The system records the transaction in the audit log and triggers notifications.
35. The system displays a confirmation message indicating that the delivery partner review has been submitted successfully.

#### 3.10.6.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Existing Review Found**

1. The system detects an existing review for the selected delivery partner.
2. If editing is permitted, the customer is redirected to the **Edit Review** use case.
3. Otherwise, the system informs the customer that another review cannot be submitted.

---

**AF-2: Delivery Partner Not Eligible for Review**

1. The selected delivery partner does not satisfy the review eligibility criteria.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer receives an appropriate validation message.

---

**AF-3: Review Submission Window Expired**

1. The configured review submission period has expired.
2. The review request is rejected.
3. The customer is informed that the submission window has expired.

---

**AF-4: Inappropriate Content Detected**

1. The moderation engine detects prohibited or policy-violating content.
2. The review is rejected or moved to manual moderation.
3. The customer receives an appropriate validation message.

---

**AF-5: Duplicate Review Detected**

1. The submitted review matches an existing review.
2. The system prevents duplicate review submission.
3. The customer is informed according to platform policy.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. The fraud detection engine identifies suspicious review activity.
2. The review status becomes **Pending Moderation**.
3. The review remains hidden until moderation is completed.

---

**AF-7: Image Upload Failure**

1. The textual review passes all validations.
2. One or more attached images fail during upload.
3. The system processes the failure according to platform policy and informs the customer.

---

**AF-8: Authentication Session Expired**

1. The customer's authentication session expires before submission completes.
2. The review request is rejected.
3. The customer is redirected to the login page.

---

**AF-9: Database Transaction Failure**

1. A database failure occurs while saving the review.
2. The transaction is rolled back completely.
3. No partial review data is stored.
4. The customer receives an error message requesting another attempt later.

---

**AF-10: Audit Logging Failure**

1. The review is processed successfully.
2. Audit log generation fails.
3. The failure is captured by platform monitoring for administrative investigation.

#### 3.10.6.10 Postconditions

1. The delivery partner review shall be stored successfully.
2. The review shall be associated with the customer, delivery partner, restaurant, and completed order.
3. The moderation status shall be assigned.
4. Uploaded review images shall be linked to the review.
5. Delivery partner review statistics shall be updated according to moderation status.
6. Performance metrics shall be refreshed.
7. Analytics data shall be updated.
8. Search indexes shall be updated for approved reviews.
9. Audit logs shall be generated.
10. The customer's review history shall be updated.

### 3.10.7 Edit Review

#### 3.10.7.1 Description

The **Edit Review** use case enables an eligible customer to modify a previously submitted review for a restaurant, food item, or delivery partner within the platform-defined review edit window. Customers may update the review title, review description, attached images, and rating (where applicable) to reflect an updated experience or correct mistakes. Every modification undergoes validation, content moderation, fraud detection, version tracking, and audit logging before the updated review is published. Previous review versions shall be retained for compliance, dispute resolution, and auditing purposes.

#### 3.10.7.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Restaurant
- Delivery Partner
- Review & Rating Service
- Order Management Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Notification Service
- Recommendation Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Version Management Service

#### 3.10.7.3 Preconditions

1. Customer must be registered on the platform.
2. Customer must be authenticated.
3. Customer account must be active.
4. The review must already exist.
5. The review must belong to the authenticated customer.
6. The review must not be permanently deleted.
7. The review must not be locked by an administrator.
8. The review edit window must still be active.
9. The associated order must exist.
10. The associated entity (restaurant, food item, or delivery partner) must exist.
11. Customer must satisfy review editing eligibility rules.
12. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
13. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
14. Fraud Detection Service must be operational.
15. Version Management Service must be operational.
16. Database connectivity must be available.
17. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
18. Notification Service must be available.

#### 3.10.7.4 Trigger

The customer selects **"Edit Review"** from their review history, completed order details, restaurant page, food item page, or delivery history.

#### 3.10.7.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the review being edited. |
| Customer ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Review Title | String | No | Updated review title. |
| Review Description | Text | Yes | Updated review description. |
| Rating Value | Integer | No | Updated rating where applicable. |
| Existing Image IDs | List<UUID> | No | Images retained after editing. |
| New Images | File[] | No | Newly uploaded review images. |
| Removed Image IDs | List<UUID> | No | Images removed during editing. |
| Edit Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Date and time of review modification. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Device Identifier | String | System Generated | Customer device identifier. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | Application version used during editing. |
| Moderation Status | Enum | System Generated | Pending, Approved, Hidden, or Rejected. |
| Version Number | Integer | System Generated | Current review version after modification. |

#### 3.10.7.6 Business Rules

1. Only the review owner shall be allowed to edit the review.
2. Reviews shall only be editable within the configured edit window.
3. Administratively locked reviews shall not be editable.
4. Deleted reviews shall not be editable.
5. Every review edit shall create a new review version.
6. Previous review versions shall be retained.
7. Original review timestamps shall remain unchanged.
8. Last modified timestamps shall be updated automatically.
9. Updated reviews shall undergo full validation.
10. Updated reviews shall undergo profanity detection.
11. Updated reviews shall undergo spam detection.
12. Updated reviews shall undergo AI-assisted moderation.
13. Updated reviews shall undergo fraud detection.
14. Duplicate content detection shall be performed again.
15. Updated ratings shall recalculate average ratings where applicable.
16. Review statistics shall be refreshed after approval.
17. Recommendation services shall consume only approved review versions.
18. Search indexes shall be refreshed after publication.
19. Customers may modify review images according to platform limits.
20. Image removals shall be recorded.
21. Newly uploaded images shall be scanned before publication.
22. Restaurants and delivery partners shall not edit customer reviews.
23. Administrators may override review visibility according to platform policy.
24. Review editing shall maintain transactional consistency.
25. Partial updates shall not be committed.
26. Every edit operation shall generate an audit log.
27. Every version shall be uniquely identifiable.
28. Analytics shall consume the latest approved review version.
29. Notifications may be generated after successful review updates.
30. Customer privacy shall be protected throughout processing.
31. Review history shall remain available for compliance purposes.
32. Review modifications shall comply with community guidelines.
33. Review modifications shall comply with applicable privacy regulations.
34. Fraudulent review edits shall not influence public ratings.
35. All review editing operations shall comply with platform governance, moderation, security, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.7.7 Validations

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Review ID shall exist.
4. Review shall belong to the authenticated customer.
5. Review shall not be deleted.
6. Review shall not be administratively locked.
7. Review edit window shall be valid.
8. Updated Review Description shall be mandatory.
9. Updated Review Description shall not be empty.
10. Updated Review Description shall satisfy the minimum character limit.
11. Updated Review Description shall not exceed the maximum configured character limit.
12. Updated Review Title shall not exceed the configured character limit.
13. Updated Rating Value, if provided, shall be between **1** and **5**.
14. HTML tags shall not be accepted.
15. Script injection attempts shall be rejected.
16. SQL injection attempts shall be rejected.
17. Cross-site scripting (XSS) content shall be sanitized.
18. Updated review shall pass profanity detection.
19. Updated review shall pass spam detection.
20. Duplicate review validation shall succeed.
21. Fraud detection validation shall complete successfully.
22. Uploaded image validation shall succeed.
23. Database transaction shall complete successfully.
24. Version creation shall succeed.
25. Audit log creation shall succeed.
26. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
27. Dependent service validation shall succeed before persistence.
28. Validation failure shall prevent review modification and return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.7.8 Main Flow

1. The customer navigates to the **Review History** section or opens a previously submitted review.
2. The system displays all editable reviews belonging to the authenticated customer.
3. The customer selects an existing review.
4. The customer clicks **"Edit Review."**
5. The system retrieves the latest approved version of the review.
6. The system verifies that the review belongs to the authenticated customer.
7. The system verifies that the review is eligible for editing.
8. The system verifies that the review edit window has not expired.
9. The system checks whether the review has been administratively locked.
10. The system retrieves the review title, description, rating, and attached images.
11. The system displays the populated review editing form.
12. The customer updates the review title, if required.
13. The customer updates the review description.
14. The customer updates the rating, where applicable.
15. The customer removes existing review images, if required.
16. The customer uploads new review images, if required.
17. The customer reviews the updated information.
18. The customer submits the modified review.
19. The system receives the review update request.
20. The system authenticates the customer session.
21. The system validates all updated fields.
22. The system validates uploaded images.
23. The system sanitizes the submitted content.
24. The system performs profanity detection.
25. The system performs spam detection.
26. The system performs duplicate content detection.
27. The system performs AI-assisted content moderation.
28. The system performs fraud detection.
29. The system creates a new review version.
30. The system stores the updated review.
31. The system recalculates ratings and refreshes review statistics where applicable.
32. The system updates analytics, recommendation data, and search indexes.
33. The system records the complete edit operation in the audit log.
34. The system sends notifications to dependent services where applicable.
35. The system displays a confirmation message indicating that the review has been updated successfully.

#### 3.10.7.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Review Edit Window Expired**

1. The system detects that the configured edit window has expired.
2. The edit request is rejected.
3. The customer is informed that the review can no longer be modified.

---

**AF-2: Review Locked by Administrator**

1. The system detects that the review has been administratively locked.
2. Editing is prohibited.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-3: Review Ownership Validation Failed**

1. The review does not belong to the authenticated customer.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. An authorization error is returned.

---

**AF-4: Prohibited Content Detected**

1. The moderation engine detects prohibited content.
2. The updated review is rejected or moved to manual moderation.
3. The customer receives an appropriate validation message.

---

**AF-5: Duplicate Review Content Detected**

1. The updated review matches previously submitted content.
2. The system prevents duplicate review updates.
3. The customer is informed according to platform policy.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. The fraud detection engine identifies suspicious review modification activity.
2. The review status becomes **Pending Moderation**.
3. The updated review remains hidden until moderation is completed.

---

**AF-7: Image Upload Failure**

1. Updated review content passes validation.
2. One or more uploaded images fail validation or upload.
3. The system processes the failure according to platform policy and informs the customer.

---

**AF-8: Authentication Session Expired**

1. The customer's authentication session expires before the update is completed.
2. The edit request is rejected.
3. The customer is redirected to the login page.

---

**AF-9: Database Transaction Failure**

1. A database failure occurs while updating the review.
2. The entire transaction is rolled back.
3. The previous review version remains active.
4. The customer receives an error message requesting another attempt later.

---

**AF-10: Audit Logging Failure**

1. The review update is completed successfully.
2. Audit log generation fails.
3. The failure is recorded through platform monitoring for administrative investigation.

#### 3.10.7.10 Postconditions

1. The updated review shall be stored successfully.
2. A new review version shall be created.
3. Previous review versions shall remain available.
4. Updated ratings shall be recalculated where applicable.
5. Review statistics shall be refreshed.
6. Analytics and recommendation data shall be updated.
7. Search indexes shall be refreshed.
8. Audit logs shall be generated.
9. Customer review history shall reflect the latest version.
10. Notifications shall be generated where applicable.

#### 3.10.7.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Review updated successfully. |
| Review ID | Unique identifier of the updated review. |
| Version Number | Newly created review version. |
| Moderation Status | Pending, Approved, Hidden, or Rejected. |
| Last Modified Time | Timestamp of the successful update. |
| Updated Rating | Latest rating value where applicable. |

#### 3.10.7.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid request data. |
| 401 | Customer authentication failed. |
| 403 | Customer is not authorized to edit this review. |
| 404 | Review not found. |
| 409 | Review has been locked for editing. |
| 410 | Review edit window has expired. |
| 413 | Updated review exceeds the permitted size. |
| 422 | Updated review violates platform policies. |
| 429 | Too many update requests submitted. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Review service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.7.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only the review owner shall be allowed to edit a review.
2. Review editing shall be permitted only within the configured edit window.
3. Every successful edit shall create a new review version.
4. Previous versions shall remain available for auditing.
5. Updated content shall undergo complete moderation.
6. Updated ratings shall refresh review statistics where applicable.
7. Analytics and recommendation services shall consume only approved review versions.
8. Audit logs shall be generated for every review modification.
9. Unauthorized edit attempts shall be rejected.
10. Customers shall receive confirmation after a successful review update.

#### 3.10.7.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to edit reviews.
2. Authorization shall verify review ownership before modification.
3. Review content shall be sanitized before storage.
4. SQL Injection and Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) attacks shall be prevented.
5. Uploaded images shall undergo malware scanning and validation.
6. Fraud detection mechanisms shall monitor suspicious editing activity.
7. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
8. All communications shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
9. Security-related events shall be logged for monitoring.
10. Review editing shall comply with applicable security and privacy regulations.

#### 3.10.7.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information:

- Audit Log ID
- Review ID
- Previous Version Number
- New Version Number
- Customer ID
- Related Entity Type (Restaurant, Food Item, Delivery Partner)
- Related Entity ID
- Order ID
- Modified Fields
- Edit Timestamp
- Moderation Status
- Fraud Detection Result
- Device Type
- IP Address
- User Session Identifier
- Request Correlation ID
- Processing Status

#### 3.10.7.16 Assumptions

1. Version history is maintained for every review.
2. Previous review versions cannot be modified.
3. Moderation services process updated reviews.
4. Fraud detection services evaluate every modification.
5. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
6. Analytics processing may occur asynchronously.

#### 3.10.7.17 Dependencies

- Customer Management Module
- Restaurant Management Module
- Menu Management Module
- Delivery Management Module
- Order Management Module
- Authentication & Authorization Module
- Review & Rating Service
- Version Management Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Recommendation Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Media Storage Service
- Database Management System

### 3.10.8 Delete Review

#### 3.10.8.1 Description

The **Delete Review** use case enables an eligible customer to permanently or logically remove a previously submitted review for a restaurant, food item, or delivery partner. Customers may delete reviews that they no longer wish to display publicly. The platform shall verify ownership, validate deletion eligibility, update review statistics, refresh recommendation data, maintain audit records, preserve regulatory information where required, and ensure transactional consistency. Depending on platform policy, deleted reviews may be soft-deleted (logical deletion) while remaining available for compliance, dispute resolution, and auditing.

#### 3.10.8.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Restaurant
- Delivery Partner
- Review & Rating Service
- Order Management Service
- Recommendation Service
- Analytics Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Compliance Service

#### 3.10.8.3 Preconditions

1. Customer must be registered on the platform.
2. Customer must be authenticated.
3. Customer account must be active.
4. The review must exist.
5. The review must belong to the authenticated customer.
6. The review must not already be deleted.
7. The review must not be permanently archived.
8. The review must not be under legal retention unless platform policy permits deletion.
9. Customer must satisfy review deletion eligibility rules.
10. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
11. Recommendation Service must be operational.
12. Analytics Service must be operational.
13. Notification Service must be operational.
14. Database connectivity must be available.
15. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
16. Compliance Service must be operational.
17. Required dependent services must be available.
18. Platform security policies must be satisfied.

#### 3.10.8.4 Trigger

The customer selects **"Delete Review"** from the review history, restaurant review page, food item review page, or delivery partner review page and confirms the deletion request.

#### 3.10.8.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the review to be deleted. |
| Customer ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Deletion Reason | Enum | No | Optional customer-selected deletion reason. |
| Additional Comments | String | No | Optional customer comments regarding deletion. |
| Confirmation Flag | Boolean | Yes | Customer confirmation before deletion. |
| Deletion Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time at which deletion is requested. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Device Identifier | String | System Generated | Customer device identifier. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | Application version used during deletion. |
| Deletion Type | Enum | System Generated | Soft Delete or Permanent Delete. |
| Processing Status | Enum | System Generated | Pending, Completed, Failed. |
| Audit Reference ID | UUID | System Generated | Audit transaction identifier. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |

#### 3.10.8.6 Business Rules

1. Only the review owner shall be allowed to delete the review.
2. Administrators may delete reviews according to platform policies.
3. Deleted reviews shall no longer be visible to customers.
4. Platform policy shall determine whether soft deletion or permanent deletion is performed.
5. Soft-deleted reviews shall remain available for compliance and auditing.
6. Permanently deleted reviews shall follow platform data retention policies.
7. Review deletion shall update associated review statistics.
8. Average ratings shall be recalculated where applicable.
9. Recommendation services shall remove deleted reviews from processing.
10. Search indexes shall be refreshed after deletion.
11. Analytics services shall update reporting data.
12. Deleted reviews shall not appear in public review listings.
13. Deleted reviews shall not influence future rating calculations.
14. Review deletion shall maintain transactional consistency.
15. Partial deletion operations shall not be committed.
16. Every deletion request shall generate an audit log.
17. Every deletion shall record the deletion timestamp.
18. Customer confirmation shall be mandatory before deletion.
19. Fraud monitoring shall observe abnormal deletion activity.
20. Platform administrators shall retain access to audit information.
21. Notification services may notify dependent systems after deletion.
22. Deleted review images shall be processed according to media retention policies.
23. Associated moderation records shall remain linked where required.
24. Review version history shall be retained according to platform policy.
25. Compliance records shall not be removed if legal retention applies.
26. Customer privacy shall be protected during deletion.
27. Personally identifiable information shall be handled according to applicable regulations.
28. Deleted reviews shall not appear in recommendation engines.
29. Review deletion shall not affect completed order history.
30. Rating summaries shall be refreshed after successful deletion.
31. Dashboard metrics shall be updated.
32. Platform caching layers shall be invalidated after deletion.
33. All deletion operations shall comply with platform governance policies.
34. All deletion operations shall comply with applicable privacy regulations.
35. All deletion operations shall comply with security, audit, moderation, and compliance requirements.

#### 3.10.8.7 Validations

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Review ID shall exist.
4. Review shall belong to the authenticated customer.
5. Review shall not already be deleted.
6. Review shall not be permanently archived unless permitted.
7. Customer shall satisfy review deletion eligibility rules.
8. Confirmation Flag shall be true before deletion.
9. Invalid authentication tokens shall be rejected.
10. Suspended customer accounts shall not delete reviews.
11. Deletion request shall comply with platform retention policies.
12. Associated legal retention rules shall be verified.
13. Database transaction shall complete successfully.
14. Audit log creation shall succeed.
15. Recommendation service validation shall succeed.
16. Analytics update validation shall succeed.
17. Search index update validation shall succeed.
18. Cache invalidation shall succeed.
19. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
20. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
21. Fraud detection validation shall complete successfully.
22. Compliance validation shall succeed.
23. Notification processing validation shall succeed.
24. Review version history validation shall succeed.
25. Dependent service validation shall complete successfully.
26. Processing status shall be updated correctly.
27. Authorization validation shall succeed before deletion.
28. Validation failure shall prevent review deletion and return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.8.8 Main Flow

1. The customer navigates to the **Review History** section or opens a previously submitted review.
2. The system displays all reviews belonging to the authenticated customer.
3. The customer selects the review to be deleted.
4. The customer clicks **"Delete Review."**
5. The system retrieves the selected review details.
6. The system verifies that the review belongs to the authenticated customer.
7. The system verifies that the review is eligible for deletion.
8. The system verifies that the review is not already deleted.
9. The system checks platform retention and compliance policies.
10. The system displays a deletion confirmation dialog.
11. The customer optionally selects a deletion reason.
12. The customer optionally enters additional comments.
13. The customer confirms the deletion request.
14. The system receives the deletion request.
15. The system authenticates the customer session.
16. The system validates all mandatory request fields.
17. The system validates review ownership.
18. The system validates compliance and legal retention policies.
19. The system determines whether soft deletion or permanent deletion shall be performed.
20. The system marks the review according to the configured deletion policy.
21. The system updates the review status.
22. The system records the deletion timestamp.
23. The system updates average ratings where applicable.
24. The system refreshes review statistics.
25. The system removes the review from public visibility.
26. The system updates recommendation data.
27. The system refreshes analytics and reporting data.
28. The system updates search indexes.
29. The system invalidates cached review information.
30. The system processes associated review images according to media retention policies.
31. The system preserves version history according to platform policy.
32. The system records the complete deletion transaction in the audit log.
33. The system notifies dependent services where applicable.
34. The system commits the transaction successfully.
35. The system displays a confirmation message indicating that the review has been deleted successfully.

#### 3.10.8.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Review Already Deleted**

1. The system detects that the selected review has already been deleted.
2. The deletion request is rejected.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-2: Review Ownership Validation Failed**

1. The review does not belong to the authenticated customer.
2. The system rejects the deletion request.
3. An authorization error is returned.

---

**AF-3: Legal Retention Restriction**

1. The review is under legal or compliance retention.
2. Platform policy prevents deletion.
3. The customer is informed that the review cannot be removed.

---

**AF-4: Customer Cancels Deletion**

1. The confirmation dialog is displayed.
2. The customer selects **Cancel**.
3. The deletion process is terminated.
4. The review remains unchanged.

---

**AF-5: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. The fraud detection engine identifies suspicious deletion activity.
2. The deletion request is flagged for administrative review.
3. Processing continues according to platform security policy.

---

**AF-6: Dependent Service Failure**

1. Recommendation, analytics, or search indexing services are temporarily unavailable.
2. The system executes configured retry or asynchronous processing.
3. The review deletion remains committed if the core transaction succeeds.

---

**AF-7: Authentication Session Expired**

1. The customer's authentication session expires before deletion completes.
2. The request is rejected.
3. The customer is redirected to the login page.

---

**AF-8: Database Transaction Failure**

1. A database failure occurs during deletion.
2. The transaction is rolled back completely.
3. The review remains unchanged.
4. The customer receives an appropriate error message.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Failure**

1. The review deletion succeeds.
2. Audit log generation fails.
3. The failure is recorded through platform monitoring for administrative investigation.

---

**AF-10: Notification Delivery Failure**

1. Notification delivery to dependent services fails.
2. The deletion remains successful.
3. Failed notifications are queued for retry according to platform policy.

#### 3.10.8.10 Postconditions

1. The review shall be deleted according to platform policy.
2. The review shall no longer be publicly visible.
3. Review statistics shall be refreshed.
4. Average ratings shall be recalculated where applicable.
5. Recommendation data shall be updated.
6. Analytics and reporting data shall be refreshed.
7. Search indexes shall be updated.
8. Audit logs shall be generated.
9. Review version history shall be preserved according to retention policies.
10. Dependent services shall be notified where applicable.

#### 3.10.8.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Review deleted successfully. |
| Review ID | Unique identifier of the deleted review. |
| Deletion Type | Soft Delete or Permanent Delete. |
| Deletion Timestamp | Time of successful deletion. |
| Processing Status | Completed. |

#### 3.10.8.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid deletion request. |
| 401 | Customer authentication failed. |
| 403 | Customer is not authorized to delete the review. |
| 404 | Review not found. |
| 409 | Review has already been deleted. |
| 423 | Review is locked due to compliance or legal retention. |
| 429 | Too many deletion requests submitted. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Review service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.8.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only the review owner shall be allowed to delete a review.
2. Customer confirmation shall be mandatory before deletion.
3. Deleted reviews shall no longer appear publicly.
4. Review statistics and average ratings shall be refreshed.
5. Recommendation and analytics services shall be updated.
6. Version history shall be preserved according to platform policy.
7. Audit logs shall be generated for every deletion request.
8. Unauthorized deletion attempts shall be rejected.
9. Compliance and legal retention rules shall be enforced.
10. Customers shall receive confirmation after successful deletion.

#### 3.10.8.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to delete reviews.
2. Authorization shall verify review ownership before deletion.
3. All deletion requests shall be protected against replay attacks.
4. Fraud detection shall monitor suspicious deletion activity.
5. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
6. Compliance retention policies shall be enforced.
7. All communications shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Deleted reviews shall remain protected from unauthorized recovery.
10. Review deletion shall comply with applicable security and privacy regulations.

#### 3.10.8.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information:

- Audit Log ID
- Review ID
- Customer ID
- Related Entity Type (Restaurant, Food Item, Delivery Partner)
- Related Entity ID
- Order ID
- Deletion Type
- Deletion Reason
- Additional Comments
- Deletion Timestamp
- Processing Status
- Device Type
- IP Address
- User Session Identifier
- Request Correlation ID

#### 3.10.8.16 Assumptions

1. Platform policy supports soft deletion by default unless permanent deletion is explicitly required.
2. Compliance records are retained independently of public review visibility.
3. Recommendation services remove deleted reviews from future processing.
4. Analytics services refresh metrics after successful deletion.
5. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
6. Background jobs may asynchronously update search indexes and caches.

#### 3.10.8.17 Dependencies

- Customer Management Module
- Restaurant Management Module
- Menu Management Module
- Delivery Management Module
- Order Management Module
- Authentication & Authorization Module
- Review & Rating Service
- Recommendation Service
- Analytics Service
- Notification Service
- Compliance Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Media Storage Service
- Search & Indexing Service
- Cache Management Service
- Database Management System

## Review Discovery

### 3.10.9 View Restaurant Reviews

#### 3.10.9.1 Description

The **View Restaurant Reviews** use case enables customers to browse detailed reviews submitted by other verified customers for a restaurant. The feature helps users evaluate food quality, service, hygiene, ambience, delivery experience, pricing, consistency, and overall customer satisfaction before placing an order. The system displays only reviews that satisfy moderation, visibility, and publication policies while supporting pagination, filtering, sorting, media viewing, review statistics, and real-time data retrieval.

#### 3.10.9.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Customer
- Guest User

**Secondary Actors**

- Restaurant
- Review & Rating Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Search Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Recommendation Service

#### 3.10.9.3 Preconditions

1. The restaurant must exist.
2. The restaurant must be active.
3. The restaurant profile must be publicly accessible.
4. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
5. Restaurant Management Service must be operational.
6. Search Service must be operational.
7. Database connectivity must be available.
8. Published reviews must exist for the restaurant, or the system shall support empty review results.
9. Moderation service must have processed published reviews.
10. Review visibility policies must be available.
11. Analytics Service must be operational.
12. Recommendation Service must be operational.
13. Pagination configuration must be available.
14. Cache service must be operational if enabled.
15. Public API endpoints must be available.
16. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
17. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.9.4 Trigger

The customer or guest user opens a restaurant profile and selects the **Reviews** section.

#### 3.10.9.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Restaurant ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the restaurant. |
| Customer ID | UUID | No | Authenticated customer identifier if logged in. |
| Page Number | Integer | No | Current page number. |
| Page Size | Integer | No | Number of reviews displayed per page. |
| Sort Option | Enum | No | Latest, Oldest, Highest Rating, Lowest Rating, Most Helpful. |
| Filter Option | Enum | No | Available review filter. |
| Search Keyword | String | No | Keyword to search within reviews. |
| Language Preference | String | No | Preferred review language. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | Application version. |
| Request Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time of request. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | User session identifier. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request identifier. |

#### 3.10.9.6 Business Rules

1. Restaurant reviews shall be publicly viewable unless restricted by platform policy.
2. Only approved reviews shall be displayed.
3. Hidden reviews shall not be displayed.
4. Rejected reviews shall not be displayed.
5. Deleted reviews shall not be displayed.
6. Reviews under moderation shall remain hidden.
7. Reviews shall be displayed according to visibility rules.
8. Restaurant owner replies may be displayed with the review.
9. Review images shall be displayed only after moderation approval.
10. Review statistics shall reflect only published reviews.
11. Average ratings shall exclude hidden and deleted reviews.
12. Search results shall include only eligible reviews.
13. Pagination shall be supported.
14. Sorting shall be supported.
15. Filtering shall be supported.
16. Keyword search shall be supported where applicable.
17. Helpful vote counts shall be displayed where available.
18. Review timestamps shall be displayed in platform format.
19. Customer identity masking shall follow privacy policy.
20. Verified purchase indicators shall be displayed where applicable.
21. Restaurant responses shall be linked to their corresponding reviews.
22. Review media shall comply with platform moderation policies.
23. Recommendation services may consume published reviews.
24. Analytics services shall record review view events.
25. Review retrieval shall maintain transactional consistency.
26. Cached review data shall remain synchronized with published content.
27. Search indexes shall remain synchronized.
28. Review retrieval shall support configurable page sizes.
29. Unauthorized data shall never be exposed.
30. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
31. Review visibility shall comply with moderation policies.
32. Platform caching policies shall be enforced.
33. Every review retrieval request may be logged for analytics.
34. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
35. All review viewing operations shall comply with governance, moderation, privacy, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.9.7 Validations

1. Restaurant ID shall exist.
2. Restaurant shall be active.
3. Page Number shall be greater than zero.
4. Page Size shall be within configured limits.
5. Sort Option shall contain a supported value.
6. Filter Option shall contain a supported value.
7. Search keyword length shall satisfy configured limits.
8. Invalid pagination values shall be rejected.
9. Invalid sorting parameters shall be rejected.
10. Invalid filtering parameters shall be rejected.
11. Unsupported language preferences shall fall back to the default language.
12. Only approved reviews shall be retrieved.
13. Hidden reviews shall be excluded.
14. Deleted reviews shall be excluded.
15. Rejected reviews shall be excluded.
16. Moderation status validation shall succeed.
17. Search service validation shall succeed.
18. Cache validation shall succeed.
19. Database query validation shall succeed.
20. Analytics event generation shall succeed.
21. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
22. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
23. Security validation shall complete successfully.
24. Dependent service validation shall succeed.
25. Response pagination shall be validated.
26. Review media availability shall be verified.
27. Restaurant response visibility shall be validated.
28. Validation failure shall return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.9.8 Main Flow

1. The customer or guest user navigates to a restaurant profile.
2. The system retrieves the restaurant details.
3. The customer selects the **Reviews** tab.
4. The system receives the review retrieval request.
5. The system validates the Restaurant ID.
6. The system verifies that the restaurant is active.
7. The system retrieves all published reviews associated with the restaurant.
8. The system excludes deleted reviews.
9. The system excludes rejected reviews.
10. The system excludes hidden reviews.
11. The system excludes reviews currently under moderation.
12. The system retrieves associated customer ratings.
13. The system retrieves restaurant replies for eligible reviews.
14. The system retrieves approved review images.
15. The system calculates review statistics where required.
16. The system applies the selected filter, if provided.
17. The system applies the selected sorting option.
18. The system performs keyword search, if requested.
19. The system applies language preference, where supported.
20. The system applies pagination.
21. The system retrieves helpful vote counts.
22. The system retrieves verified purchase indicators.
23. The system masks customer identity according to privacy policy.
24. The system prepares the response payload.
25. The system updates review view analytics.
26. The system records operational metrics.
27. The system retrieves cached data where applicable.
28. The system validates response consistency.
29. The system generates response metadata.
30. The system records the request in the audit log where configured.
31. The system returns the review list.
32. The system displays review images, if available.
33. The system displays restaurant replies beneath corresponding reviews.
34. The system displays review statistics and pagination controls.
35. The customer or guest user views the restaurant reviews successfully.

#### 3.10.9.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: No Reviews Available**

1. The restaurant has no published reviews.
2. The system returns an empty review list.
3. The user is informed that no reviews are currently available.

---

**AF-2: Restaurant Not Found**

1. The supplied Restaurant ID does not exist.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. An appropriate error message is returned.

---

**AF-3: Restaurant Inactive**

1. The restaurant is inactive or unavailable.
2. The system prevents review retrieval.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-4: Invalid Pagination Parameters**

1. The supplied page number or page size is invalid.
2. The system applies the default configuration or rejects the request according to platform policy.
3. The customer receives an appropriate response.

---

**AF-5: Invalid Filter or Sort Option**

1. The supplied filter or sort option is unsupported.
2. The system rejects the invalid parameter or falls back to the default configuration.
3. The customer receives an appropriate response.

---

**AF-6: No Matching Search Results**

1. The search keyword does not match any published review.
2. The system returns an empty result set.
3. The customer is informed accordingly.

---

**AF-7: Cache Miss**

1. Cached review data is unavailable.
2. The system retrieves fresh data from the database.
3. The response is returned successfully.

---

**AF-8: Analytics Service Failure**

1. Analytics event generation fails.
2. Review retrieval continues successfully.
3. The analytics event is queued for retry according to platform policy.

---

**AF-9: Database Query Failure**

1. A database error occurs during review retrieval.
2. The system terminates processing.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-10: Search Service Unavailable**

1. The search service becomes unavailable.
2. Keyword search functionality is temporarily disabled.
3. Standard review retrieval continues where possible.

#### 3.10.9.10 Postconditions

1. Published restaurant reviews shall be displayed.
2. Only eligible reviews shall be visible.
3. Review statistics shall be displayed.
4. Pagination metadata shall be generated.
5. Sorting and filtering shall be applied where requested.
6. Restaurant replies shall be displayed where available.
7. Review images shall be displayed where approved.
8. Analytics events shall be generated.
9. Operational metrics shall be updated.
10. Audit records shall be created where applicable.

### 3.10.10 View Restaurant Ratings

#### 3.10.10.1 Description

The **View Restaurant Ratings** use case enables customers and guest users to view the overall rating information of a restaurant. The feature displays the restaurant's average rating, total number of ratings, rating distribution (1–5 stars), recent rating trends, verified customer ratings, and rating summaries. The displayed ratings help customers evaluate the overall quality and reliability of the restaurant before placing an order. Only approved and eligible ratings are included in public rating calculations.

#### 3.10.10.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Customer
- Guest User

**Secondary Actors**

- Restaurant
- Review & Rating Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Analytics Service
- Search Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Recommendation Service

#### 3.10.10.3 Preconditions

1. The restaurant must exist.
2. The restaurant must be active.
3. The restaurant profile must be publicly accessible.
4. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
5. Restaurant Management Service must be operational.
6. Rating aggregation service must be operational.
7. Database connectivity must be available.
8. Published ratings may exist for the restaurant.
9. Hidden ratings must be excluded.
10. Deleted ratings must be excluded.
11. Moderation policies must be applied before ratings are published.
12. Analytics Service must be operational.
13. Search Service must be operational.
14. Recommendation Service must be operational.
15. Cache service must be available if enabled.
16. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
17. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.10.4 Trigger

The customer or guest user opens a restaurant profile and views the **Restaurant Ratings** section.

| Customer ID | UUID | No | Authenticated customer identifier if logged in. |
| Rating Filter | Enum | No | Filter ratings by star value. |
| Time Period | Enum | No | Daily, Weekly, Monthly, Yearly, or All Time. |
| Language Preference | String | No | Preferred display language. |
| Page Number | Integer | No | Current page number. |
| Page Size | Integer | No | Number of rating records displayed per page. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | Application version. |
| Request Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time of request. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | User session identifier. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request identifier. |

#### 3.10.10.6 Business Rules

1. Restaurant ratings shall be publicly viewable unless restricted by platform policy.
2. Only approved ratings shall be included in public calculations.
3. Hidden ratings shall not contribute to average ratings.
4. Deleted ratings shall not contribute to average ratings.
5. Ratings under moderation shall remain excluded.
6. Average ratings shall be recalculated after every approved rating update.
7. Rating distribution shall include only eligible ratings.
8. Rating summaries shall reflect approved ratings only.
9. Verified purchase indicators shall be displayed where applicable.
10. Rating counts shall remain synchronized with published ratings.
11. Rating filters shall be supported.
12. Pagination shall be supported where individual rating records are displayed.
13. Time-based rating summaries shall be supported.
14. Recommendation services may consume approved rating data.
15. Analytics services shall record rating view events.
16. Search indexes shall remain synchronized.
17. Cached rating data shall remain synchronized with source data.
18. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
19. Customer identities shall be masked according to privacy policy.
20. Rating retrieval shall maintain transactional consistency.
21. Unauthorized rating data shall never be exposed.
22. Fraudulent ratings shall not affect public statistics.
23. Administrative rating metadata shall not be displayed.
24. Rating retrieval shall support configurable page sizes.
25. Rating response times shall comply with platform performance requirements.
26. Platform caching policies shall be enforced.
27. Rating calculations shall use the latest approved dataset.
28. Rating statistics shall support analytics reporting.
29. Every rating retrieval request may be logged.
30. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
31. Rating information shall remain consistent across application platforms.
32. Ratings shall comply with moderation policies.
33. Rating visibility shall comply with platform governance policies.
34. Rating data shall comply with applicable privacy regulations.
35. All rating viewing operations shall comply with governance, moderation, security, privacy, and audit policies.

2. Restaurant shall be active.
3. Rating Filter shall contain a supported value.
4. Time Period shall contain a supported value.
5. Page Number shall be greater than zero.
6. Page Size shall be within configured limits.
7. Unsupported language preferences shall fall back to the default language.
8. Hidden ratings shall be excluded.
9. Deleted ratings shall be excluded.

16. Cache validation shall succeed.

18. Analytics event generation shall succeed.

20. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
21. Security validation shall complete successfully.
22. Response pagination shall be validated.
23. Rating summary validation shall succeed.
24. Dependent service validation shall succeed.
25. Invalid pagination values shall be rejected.
26. Invalid filter parameters shall be rejected.
27. Invalid time period values shall be rejected.
28. Validation failure shall return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.10.8 Main Flow

1. The customer or guest user navigates to the restaurant profile.
2. The system retrieves the restaurant details.
3. The customer opens the **Ratings** section.
4. The system receives the rating retrieval request.
5. The system validates the Restaurant ID.
6. The system verifies that the restaurant is active.
7. The system retrieves all approved ratings associated with the restaurant.
8. The system excludes deleted ratings.
9. The system excludes hidden ratings.
10. The system excludes ratings currently under moderation.
11. The system calculates the average restaurant rating.
12. The system calculates the total number of ratings.
13. The system calculates the 1-star rating count.
14. The system calculates the 2-star rating count.
15. The system calculates the 3-star rating count.
16. The system calculates the 4-star rating count.
17. The system calculates the 5-star rating count.
18. The system prepares the rating distribution summary.
19. The system applies the selected rating filter, if provided.
20. The system applies the selected time period filter, if provided.
21. The system retrieves verified purchase indicators where applicable.
22. The system applies pagination to individual rating records.
23. The system masks customer identity according to privacy policy.
24. The system retrieves cached rating information where applicable.
25. The system validates rating aggregation consistency.
26. The system prepares response metadata.
27. The system records rating view analytics.
28. The system records operational metrics.
29. The system records the request in the audit log where configured.
30. The system prepares the response payload.
31. The system returns the rating information.
32. The system displays the average restaurant rating.
33. The system displays the rating distribution and total ratings.
34. The system displays individual rating records where applicable.
35. The customer or guest user successfully views the restaurant ratings.

### 3.10.11 View Food Item Reviews

#### 3.10.11.1 Description

The **View Food Item Reviews** use case enables customers and guest users to browse detailed reviews submitted by verified customers for a specific food item offered by a restaurant. The feature helps users evaluate the taste, freshness, quality, portion size, presentation, packaging, aroma, texture, value for money, and consistency of the food item before placing an order. The system displays only approved and publicly visible reviews while supporting pagination, filtering, sorting, keyword search, review images, restaurant replies, verified purchase indicators, and review analytics.

### 3.10.12 View Food Item Ratings

#### 3.10.12.1 Description

The **View Food Item Ratings** use case enables customers and guest users to view the overall rating information of a specific food item. The feature displays the food item's average rating, total number of ratings, rating distribution (1–5 stars), verified purchase ratings, recent rating trends, and rating summaries. The displayed information helps customers evaluate the popularity, quality, consistency, and customer satisfaction of a food item before placing an order. Only approved and publicly visible ratings are included in rating calculations and public displays.

#### 3.10.12.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Customer
- Guest User

**Secondary Actors**

- Restaurant
- Menu Management Service
- Review & Rating Service
- Rating Aggregation Service
- Search Service
- Recommendation Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.12.3 Preconditions

1. The food item must exist.
2. The associated restaurant must exist.
3. The restaurant must be active.
4. The food item must be active.
5. The food item must be publicly visible.
6. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
7. Rating Aggregation Service must be operational.
8. Menu Management Service must be operational.
9. Database connectivity must be available.
10. Published ratings may exist for the selected food item.
11. Moderation policies must have been applied before ratings are published.
12. Hidden ratings must be excluded.
13. Deleted ratings must be excluded.
14. Analytics Service must be operational.
15. Search Service must be operational.
16. Cache service must be operational if enabled.
17. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
18. Platform security policies must be satisfied.

#### 3.10.12.4 Trigger

The customer or guest user opens a food item's detail page and views the **Ratings** section.

#### 3.10.12.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Food Item ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the selected food item. |
| Restaurant ID | UUID | Yes | Identifier of the restaurant offering the food item. |
| Customer ID | UUID | No | Authenticated customer identifier if logged in. |
| Rating Filter | Enum | No | Filter ratings by star value. |
| Time Period | Enum | No | Daily, Weekly, Monthly, Yearly, or All Time. |
| Page Number | Integer | No | Current page number. |
| Page Size | Integer | No | Number of rating records displayed per page. |
| Language Preference | String | No | Preferred display language. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | Application version. |
| Request Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time of request. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | User session identifier. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |

#### 3.10.12.6 Business Rules

1. Food item ratings shall be publicly viewable unless restricted by platform policy.
2. Only approved ratings shall be included in public calculations.
3. Hidden ratings shall not contribute to average ratings.
4. Deleted ratings shall not contribute to average ratings.
5. Ratings under moderation shall remain excluded.
6. Average ratings shall be recalculated after every approved rating update.
7. Rating distribution shall include only eligible ratings.
8. Rating summaries shall reflect approved ratings only.
9. Verified purchase indicators shall be displayed where applicable.
10. Rating counts shall remain synchronized with approved ratings.
11. Rating filters shall be supported.
12. Pagination shall be supported where individual rating records are displayed.
13. Time-based rating summaries shall be supported.
14. Recommendation services may consume approved rating data.
15. Analytics services shall record food item rating view events.
16. Search indexes shall remain synchronized.
17. Cached rating data shall remain synchronized with source data.
18. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
19. Customer identities shall be masked according to privacy policy.
20. Rating retrieval shall maintain transactional consistency.
21. Unauthorized rating data shall never be exposed.
22. Fraudulent ratings shall not affect public statistics.
23. Administrative rating metadata shall not be displayed.
24. Rating retrieval shall support configurable page sizes.
25. Rating response times shall comply with platform performance requirements.
26. Platform caching policies shall be enforced.
27. Rating calculations shall use the latest approved dataset.
28. Rating statistics shall support analytics reporting.
29. Every rating retrieval request may be logged.
30. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
31. Rating information shall remain consistent across application platforms.
32. Rating visibility shall comply with moderation policies.
33. Rating data shall comply with applicable privacy regulations.
34. Operational metrics shall be updated during rating retrieval.
35. All food item rating viewing operations shall comply with governance, moderation, security, privacy, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.12.7 Validations

1. Food Item ID shall exist.
2. Restaurant ID shall exist.
3. Food item shall be active.
4. Restaurant shall be active.
5. Rating Filter shall contain a supported value.
6. Time Period shall contain a supported value.
7. Page Number shall be greater than zero.
8. Page Size shall be within configured limits.
9. Unsupported language preferences shall fall back to the default language.
10. Only approved ratings shall be retrieved.
11. Hidden ratings shall be excluded.
12. Deleted ratings shall be excluded.
13. Ratings under moderation shall be excluded.
14. Rating aggregation validation shall succeed.
15. Average rating calculation validation shall succeed.
16. Rating distribution validation shall succeed.
17. Search service validation shall succeed.
18. Cache validation shall succeed.
19. Database query validation shall succeed.
20. Analytics event generation shall succeed.
21. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
22. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
23. Security validation shall complete successfully.
24. Response pagination shall be validated.
25. Rating summary validation shall succeed.
26. Invalid filter parameters shall be rejected.
27. Invalid time period values shall be rejected.
28. Validation failure shall return an appropriate error response.

#### 3.10.12.8 Main Flow

1. The customer or guest user navigates to the food item details page.
2. The system retrieves the food item information.
3. The customer opens the **Ratings** section.
4. The system receives the rating retrieval request.
5. The system validates the Food Item ID.
6. The system verifies that the food item is active.
7. The system verifies that the associated restaurant is active.
8. The system retrieves all approved ratings associated with the selected food item.
9. The system excludes deleted ratings.
10. The system excludes hidden ratings.
11. The system excludes ratings currently under moderation.
12. The system calculates the average food item rating.
13. The system calculates the total number of ratings.
14. The system calculates the 1-star rating count.
15. The system calculates the 2-star rating count.
16. The system calculates the 3-star rating count.
17. The system calculates the 4-star rating count.
18. The system calculates the 5-star rating count.
19. The system prepares the rating distribution summary.
20. The system applies the selected rating filter, if provided.
21. The system applies the selected time period filter, if provided.
22. The system retrieves verified purchase indicators where applicable.
23. The system applies pagination to individual rating records.
24. The system masks customer identity according to privacy policy.
25. The system retrieves cached rating information where applicable.
26. The system validates rating aggregation consistency.
27. The system prepares response metadata.
28. The system records food item rating view analytics.
29. The system records operational metrics.
30. The system records the request in the audit log where configured.
31. The system prepares the response payload.
32. The system returns the rating information.
33. The system displays the average food item rating.
34. The system displays the rating distribution and total ratings.
35. The customer or guest user successfully views the food item ratings.

#### 3.10.12.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: No Ratings Available**

1. The selected food item has no published ratings.
2. The system returns an empty rating summary.
3. The customer is informed that ratings are currently unavailable.

---

**AF-2: Food Item Not Found**

1. The supplied Food Item ID does not exist.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. An appropriate error response is returned.

---

**AF-3: Food Item Inactive**

1. The selected food item is inactive or unavailable.
2. The system prevents rating retrieval.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-4: Invalid Rating Filter**

1. The supplied rating filter is unsupported.
2. The system rejects the request or applies the default configuration according to platform policy.
3. The customer receives an appropriate response.

---

**AF-5: Invalid Time Period**

1. The supplied time period is unsupported.
2. The system rejects the request or applies the default configuration.
3. The customer receives an appropriate response.

---

**AF-6: Cache Miss**

1. Cached rating information is unavailable.
2. The system retrieves fresh rating data from the database.
3. The response is returned successfully.

---

**AF-7: Analytics Service Failure**

1. Analytics event generation fails.
2. Rating retrieval continues successfully.
3. The analytics event is queued for retry.

---

**AF-8: Database Query Failure**

1. A database error occurs while retrieving rating information.
2. The request is terminated.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-9: Rating Aggregation Failure**

1. Rating aggregation cannot be completed successfully.
2. The system returns an appropriate processing error.
3. The customer is informed to try again later.

---

**AF-10: Search Service Unavailable**

1. The search or indexing service becomes temporarily unavailable.
2. Rating retrieval continues using available data.
3. Advanced search capabilities remain unavailable until recovery.

#### 3.10.12.10 Postconditions

1. Food item rating information shall be displayed successfully.
2. Average food item rating shall be calculated.
3. Rating distribution shall be generated.
4. Total rating count shall be displayed.
5. Rating filters shall be applied where requested.
6. Time-based summaries shall be generated where requested.
7. Analytics events shall be generated.
8. Operational metrics shall be updated.
9. Audit records shall be created where applicable.
10. Response metadata shall be generated successfully.

#### 3.10.12.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Food item ratings retrieved successfully. |
| Food Item ID | Unique identifier of the selected food item. |
| Restaurant ID | Associated restaurant identifier. |
| Average Rating | Current overall food item rating. |
| Total Ratings | Total number of approved ratings. |
| Rating Distribution | Number of 1-star through 5-star ratings. |
| Rating Summary | Aggregated rating information. |
| Pagination | Pagination information where applicable. |
| Retrieved Timestamp | Response generation timestamp. |

#### 3.10.12.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid request parameters. |
| 401 | Authentication failed where required. |
| 403 | Access denied according to platform policy. |
| 404 | Food item not found. |
| 422 | Invalid rating filter or time period. |
| 429 | Too many requests. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Rating service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.12.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Food item ratings shall be displayed only for existing and active food items.
2. Only approved ratings shall contribute to rating calculations.
3. Hidden, deleted, fraudulent, and moderated ratings shall never be displayed publicly.
4. Average ratings shall be calculated accurately.
5. Rating distribution shall be generated correctly.
6. Rating filters and time period filters shall function correctly.
7. Customer privacy shall be maintained.
8. Analytics events shall be generated successfully.
9. Rating summaries shall remain synchronized with approved ratings.
10. The customer or guest user shall receive a successful response whenever rating information is available.

#### 3.10.12.14 Security Considerations

1. Hidden and moderated ratings shall never be exposed publicly.
2. Personally identifiable information shall be masked according to privacy policies.
3. Administrative rating metadata shall never be exposed.
4. Request parameters shall be validated to prevent injection attacks.
5. Fraudulent ratings shall be excluded from public calculations.
6. All communications shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
7. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Cached rating information shall comply with access control policies.
10. Food item rating viewing shall comply with applicable security and privacy regulations.

#### 3.10.12.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information:

- Audit Log ID
- Food Item ID
- Restaurant ID
- Customer ID (if authenticated)
- Session ID
- Rating Filter
- Time Period
- Page Number
- Page Size
- Request Timestamp
- Device Type
- IP Address
- Request Correlation ID
- Response Status
- Processing Duration

#### 3.10.12.16 Assumptions

1. Rating aggregation is based only on approved ratings.
2. Fraudulent, deleted, hidden, and moderated ratings are excluded from calculations.
3. Rating summaries are periodically synchronized.
4. Analytics events may be processed asynchronously.
5. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
6. Cache invalidation mechanisms maintain rating consistency.

### 3.10.13 View Delivery Partner Ratings

#### 3.10.13.1 Description

The **View Delivery Partner Ratings** use case enables customers and authorized users to view the overall rating information of a delivery partner associated with completed deliveries. The feature displays the delivery partner's average rating, total number of ratings, rating distribution (1–5 stars), verified delivery ratings, recent rating trends, and overall rating summaries. The displayed information helps customers understand the delivery partner's service quality, professionalism, punctuality, communication, and customer satisfaction. Only approved and publicly visible ratings are included in rating calculations and displayed to users.
