### 3.10.14 Mark Review as Helpful

#### 3.10.14.1 Description

The **Mark Review as Helpful** use case enables customers to indicate that a published review was useful in making an informed purchasing decision. Helpful votes assist other customers by highlighting informative, accurate, and trustworthy reviews. The system ensures that only eligible customers can cast a helpful vote, prevents duplicate voting, updates helpful vote counts in real time, records audit information, and uses helpfulness metrics to improve review ranking and recommendations.

#### 3.10.14.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Customer Management Service
- Authentication Service
- Analytics Service
- Recommendation Service
- Search Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Notification Service

#### 3.10.14.3 Preconditions

1. The customer must be authenticated.
2. The customer account must be active.
3. The review must exist.
4. The review must be publicly visible.
5. The review must be approved.
6. The review must not be deleted.
7. The review must not be hidden.
8. The review must not be under moderation.
9. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
10. Authentication Service must be operational.
11. Customer Management Service must be operational.
12. Database connectivity must be available.
13. Analytics Service must be operational.
14. Recommendation Service must be operational.
15. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
16. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
17. The customer must not have exceeded platform rate limits.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.14.4 Trigger

The authenticated customer clicks or taps the **Helpful** button displayed on a published review.

#### 3.10.14.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the selected review. |
| Customer ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Helpful Status | Boolean | Yes | Indicates whether the review is marked as helpful. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Application Version | String | System Generated | Client application version. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| User Agent | String | System Generated | Browser or application information. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | Active session identifier. |
| Request Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time when the request was submitted. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |

#### 3.10.14.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall mark reviews as helpful.
2. Only approved reviews shall accept helpful votes.
3. Deleted reviews shall not accept helpful votes.
4. Hidden reviews shall not accept helpful votes.
5. Reviews under moderation shall not accept helpful votes.
6. A customer may vote only once for a specific review.
7. Duplicate helpful votes shall be prevented.
8. Customers may remove their previous helpful vote if supported by platform policy.
9. Helpful vote counts shall be updated immediately after a successful operation.
10. Review rankings may consider helpful vote counts.
11. Recommendation services may consume helpful vote statistics.
12. Search rankings may consider helpful vote metrics.
13. Helpful votes shall not modify the original review content.
14. Fraud detection policies shall monitor unusual voting activity.
15. Automated voting shall be prevented.
16. Rate limiting shall be enforced.
17. Customer identity shall not be publicly displayed with helpful votes.
18. Analytics events shall be generated for every successful helpful vote.
19. Audit logs shall record every helpful vote operation.
20. Cached review statistics shall be synchronized.
21. Database consistency shall be maintained.
22. Unauthorized requests shall be rejected.
23. Invalid review identifiers shall be rejected.
24. Review visibility policies shall be enforced.
25. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
26. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
27. Operational metrics shall be updated.
28. Failed requests shall not increment helpful counts.
29. Concurrent voting requests shall maintain data consistency.
30. Helpful vote counts shall remain accurate across all platforms.
31. Platform caching policies shall be enforced.
32. The operation shall comply with moderation policies.
33. The operation shall comply with applicable privacy regulations.
34. The operation shall support horizontal scalability.
35. All helpful vote operations shall comply with governance, moderation, security, privacy, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.14.7 Validations

1. Customer authentication shall be verified.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Review ID shall exist.
4. Review shall be approved.
5. Review shall be publicly visible.
6. Review shall not be deleted.
7. Review shall not be hidden.
8. Review shall not be under moderation.
9. Duplicate helpful vote validation shall succeed.
10. Customer eligibility validation shall succeed.
11. Authorization validation shall succeed.
12. Database validation shall succeed.
13. Cache validation shall succeed.
14. Analytics service validation shall succeed.
15. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
16. Rate limit validation shall succeed.
17. Fraud detection validation shall succeed.
18. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
19. Session validation shall succeed.
20. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
21. Security validation shall complete successfully.
22. Review visibility validation shall succeed.
23. Helpful vote count consistency validation shall succeed.
24. Concurrent update validation shall succeed.
25. Invalid Review IDs shall be rejected.
26. Unauthorized requests shall be rejected.
27. Duplicate submissions shall be prevented.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.14.8 Main Flow

1. The customer views a published review.
2. The customer clicks or taps the **Helpful** button.
3. The system receives the helpful vote request.
4. The system authenticates the customer.
5. The system validates the customer session.
6. The system validates the Review ID.
7. The system verifies that the review exists.
8. The system verifies that the review is approved.
9. The system verifies that the review is publicly visible.
10. The system verifies that the review is not deleted.
11. The system verifies that the review is not hidden.
12. The system verifies that the review is not under moderation.
13. The system checks whether the customer has already voted for the review.
14. The system validates platform rate limits.
15. The system performs fraud detection checks.
16. The system records the helpful vote.
17. The system increments the helpful vote count.
18. The system updates the review statistics.
19. The system updates the review ranking metrics.
20. The system updates recommendation metrics.
21. The system synchronizes cached review data.
22. The system updates search indexing metrics where applicable.
23. The system validates data consistency.
24. The system commits the transaction.
25. The system generates analytics events.
26. The system records operational metrics.
27. The system records the activity in the audit log.
28. The system prepares the response payload.
29. The system returns a successful response.
30. The helpful vote count is refreshed.
31. The **Helpful** button state is updated for the customer.
32. The updated review statistics are displayed.
33. Other customers can view the updated helpful count.
34. Recommendation models receive the updated helpfulness information.
35. The helpful vote operation is completed successfully.

#### 3.10.14.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Duplicate Helpful Vote**

1. The customer has already marked the review as helpful.
2. The system rejects the duplicate request.
3. An appropriate message is returned.

---

**AF-2: Review Not Found**

1. The supplied Review ID does not exist.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. An appropriate error response is returned.

---

**AF-3: Review Not Eligible**

1. The review is hidden, deleted, rejected, or under moderation.
2. The system prevents the helpful vote.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-4: Authentication Failure**

1. The customer session is invalid or expired.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer is prompted to authenticate again.

---

**AF-5: Rate Limit Exceeded**

1. The customer exceeds the configured request limit.
2. The system temporarily blocks the operation.
3. An appropriate response is returned.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. Suspicious voting activity is detected.
2. The system blocks or flags the request.
3. The activity is forwarded for further analysis.

---

**AF-7: Cache Update Failure**

1. Cache synchronization fails.
2. The database transaction remains successful.
3. Cache synchronization is scheduled for retry.

---

**AF-8: Analytics Service Failure**

1. Analytics event generation fails.
2. The helpful vote remains successful.
3. The analytics event is queued for retry.

---

**AF-9: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while processing the request.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-10: Concurrent Vote Conflict**

1. Multiple voting requests are processed simultaneously.
2. The system applies concurrency controls.
3. The final helpful vote count remains consistent.

6. Analytics events shall be generated.
7. Operational metrics shall be updated.
8. Audit records shall be created.
9. Transaction consistency shall be maintained.

### 3.10.15 Sort Reviews

#### 3.10.15.1 Description

The **Sort Reviews** use case enables customers and guest users to organize restaurant, food item, or delivery partner reviews using predefined sorting criteria. The feature allows users to sort reviews by **Newest**, **Oldest**, **Highest Rating**, **Lowest Rating**, **Most Helpful**, and other supported options. Sorting improves review discoverability, helps customers quickly find relevant feedback, and enhances the overall review browsing experience. The system applies the selected sorting criteria only to eligible, approved, and publicly visible reviews while maintaining consistent pagination and response performance.

#### 3.10.15.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Customer
- Guest User

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Search Service
- Recommendation Service
- Analytics Service
- Cache Management Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.15.3 Preconditions

1. The reviewable entity (restaurant, food item, or delivery partner) must exist.
2. Published reviews may exist.
3. The review collection must be publicly accessible.
4. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
5. Search Service must be operational.
6. Database connectivity must be available.
7. Cache service must be operational if enabled.
8. Analytics Service must be operational.
9. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
10. Recommendation Service must be operational.
11. Only approved reviews shall be eligible for sorting.
12. Hidden reviews shall be excluded.
13. Deleted reviews shall be excluded.
14. Reviews under moderation shall be excluded.
15. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
16. Pagination configuration must be available.
17. Supported sorting options must be configured.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.15.4 Trigger

The customer or guest user selects a sorting option from the **Sort Reviews** menu.

| Application Version | String | System Generated | Application version. |

23. Invalid sorting options shall be rejected.
24. Unauthorized review data shall never be exposed.

#### 3.10.15.8 Main Flow

1. The customer or guest user opens the review section.
2. The system displays the available sorting options.

### 3.10.16 Filter Reviews

#### 3.10.16.1 Description

The **Filter Reviews** use case enables customers and guest users to refine displayed reviews using predefined filtering criteria. The feature allows users to filter restaurant, food item, or delivery partner reviews based on rating, verified purchase, review media availability, review language, review date, review type, and other supported attributes. Filtering improves review discoverability by helping users quickly find reviews that are most relevant to their purchasing decisions. The system applies filters only to approved and publicly visible reviews while maintaining accurate pagination, review counts, and response performance.

2. The system rejects the request or ignores unsupported filters according to platform policy.
3. The customer receives an appropriate response.

---

**AF-4: Invalid Pagination Parameters**

1. Invalid page number or page size values are supplied.
2. The system rejects the request or applies default pagination.
3. The customer receives an appropriate response.

---

**AF-5: Cache Miss**

1. Cached review information is unavailable.
2. The system retrieves fresh review data from the database.
3. The response is returned successfully.

---

**AF-6: Search Service Failure**

1. The search service becomes temporarily unavailable.
2. Review filtering continues using database records.
3. The response is returned successfully.

---

**AF-7: Analytics Service Failure**

1. Analytics event generation fails.
2. Review filtering continues successfully.
3. The analytics event is queued for retry.

---

**AF-8: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while retrieving reviews.
2. The request is terminated.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-9: Concurrent Review Update**

1. Reviews are updated while filtering is in progress.
2. The system maintains transactional consistency.
3. The latest committed review data is returned.

---

**AF-10: Entity Inactive**

1. The selected entity becomes inactive during processing.
2. The system terminates the request.
3. An appropriate notification is returned.

2. Only eligible reviews shall be displayed.
3. Matching review counts shall be updated.
4. Pagination shall remain consistent.

6. Cached review data shall be updated where applicable.
7. Analytics events shall be generated.
8. Operational metrics shall be updated.
9. Audit records shall be created where applicable.
10. Filtered review data shall be available across supported platforms.

### 3.10.17 Display Review Summary

#### 3.10.17.1 Description

The **Display Review Summary** use case enables customers and guest users to view a consolidated summary of reviews for a restaurant, food item, or delivery partner. Instead of reading every individual review, users can quickly understand overall customer sentiment through average ratings, total review count, rating distribution, verified purchase statistics, review highlights, review trends, common keywords, media counts, and recommendation indicators. The summary is generated only from approved and publicly visible reviews to provide an accurate, trustworthy, and easily understandable overview.

#### 3.10.17.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Customer
- Guest User

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Rating Aggregation Service
- Analytics Service
- Recommendation Service
- Search Service
- Cache Management Service
- Audit Logging Service

2. The entity must be publicly accessible.
3. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
4. Rating Aggregation Service must be operational.
5. Database connectivity must be available.
6. Published reviews may exist.
7. Published ratings may exist.
8. Only approved reviews shall be included.
9. Hidden reviews shall be excluded.
10. Deleted reviews shall be excluded.
11. Reviews under moderation shall be excluded.
12. Search Service must be operational.
13. Analytics Service must be operational.
14. Recommendation Service must be operational.
15. Cache service must be operational if enabled.
16. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
17. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.17.4 Trigger

The customer or guest user opens the **Review Summary** section of a restaurant, food item, or delivery partner.

### 3.10.18 Calculate Average Ratings

#### 3.10.18.1 Description

The **Calculate Average Ratings** use case enables the system to calculate and maintain the average rating for restaurants, food items, and delivery partners based on eligible customer ratings. The calculation is performed using only approved and publicly visible ratings while excluding deleted, hidden, fraudulent, moderated, or otherwise ineligible ratings. The calculated average rating is used throughout the platform for search results, recommendations, review summaries, analytics, restaurant listings, menu displays, and delivery partner profiles. The calculation is automatically triggered whenever rating data changes to ensure accurate and up-to-date rating information.

#### 3.10.18.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service (System)

**Secondary Actors**

- Rating Aggregation Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Menu Management Service
- Delivery Management Service
- Search Service
- Recommendation Service
- Analytics Service
- Cache Management Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.18.3 Preconditions

1. The reviewable entity must exist.
2. Eligible ratings must be available or the entity must support zero ratings.
3. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
4. Rating Aggregation Service must be operational.
5. Database connectivity must be available.
6. Rating calculation rules must be configured.
7. Only approved ratings shall be considered.
8. Hidden ratings shall be excluded.
9. Deleted ratings shall be excluded.
10. Fraudulent ratings shall be excluded.
11. Ratings under moderation shall be excluded.
12. Cache service must be operational if enabled.
13. Search Service must be operational.
14. Recommendation Service must be operational.
15. Analytics Service must be operational.
16. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
17. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
18. Transaction management services must be operational.

#### 3.10.18.4 Trigger

The system receives a new rating, updates an existing rating, deletes a rating, restores a rating, approves a moderated rating, or performs a scheduled rating recalculation.

| Rating ID | UUID | Yes | Unique identifier of the rating. |
| Previous Rating | Decimal | No | Previous rating value before update. |
| Current Rating | Decimal | No | Current approved rating value. |
| Processing Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time when calculation begins. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |
| Service Instance ID | String | System Generated | Processing service identifier. |
| Transaction ID | UUID | System Generated | Transaction identifier. |
| Calculation Version | String | System Generated | Rating calculation algorithm version. |

#### 3.10.18.6 Business Rules

1. Average ratings shall be calculated using only approved ratings.
2. Hidden ratings shall not contribute to calculations.
3. Deleted ratings shall not contribute to calculations.
4. Fraudulent ratings shall not contribute to calculations.
5. Ratings under moderation shall not contribute to calculations.
6. Average ratings shall be recalculated immediately after rating changes.
7. Rating calculations shall maintain transactional consistency.
8. Average ratings shall support decimal precision as configured.
9. Calculation rules shall be centrally configurable.
10. Zero eligible ratings shall produce the configured default rating behavior.
11. Rating counts shall remain synchronized with average ratings.
12. Rating aggregation shall support horizontal scalability.
13. Cached ratings shall remain synchronized.
14. Search indexes shall receive updated ratings.
15. Recommendation services shall receive updated rating information.
16. Analytics services shall receive rating calculation events.
17. Operational metrics shall be updated.
18. Audit records shall be generated.
19. Rating calculation failures shall support retry mechanisms.
20. Concurrent updates shall maintain data consistency.
21. Database consistency shall be maintained.
22. Platform caching policies shall be enforced.
23. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
24. Unauthorized modifications shall be prevented.
25. Calculation results shall remain consistent across all platforms.
26. Calculation latency shall satisfy platform performance requirements.
27. Rating calculations shall support bulk recalculation jobs.
28. Historical calculation records shall be retained according to policy.
29. Administrative overrides shall be audited.
30. Rating calculation shall be deterministic for identical datasets.
31. Review visibility policies shall be enforced.
32. Privacy regulations shall be observed.
33. Aggregated rating statistics shall remain accurate.
34. Service failures shall not corrupt rating data.
35. All rating calculation operations shall comply with governance, security, privacy, moderation, and audit policies.

18. Search synchronization validation shall succeed.
19. Recommendation synchronization validation shall succeed.
20. Analytics event validation shall succeed.
21. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
22. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
23. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
24. Security validation shall complete successfully.
25. Concurrency validation shall succeed.
26. Calculation algorithm validation shall succeed.
27. Data consistency validation shall succeed.

#### 3.10.18.8 Main Flow

1. A rating-related event is triggered within the platform.
2. The Review & Rating Service receives the event.
3. The system identifies the affected entity type.
4. The system identifies the affected entity.
5. The system validates the Entity ID.
6. The system retrieves all ratings associated with the entity.
7. The system excludes deleted ratings.
8. The system excludes hidden ratings.
9. The system excludes fraudulent ratings.
10. The system excludes ratings under moderation.
11. The system retrieves only approved ratings.
12. The system counts the total number of eligible ratings.
13. The system calculates the cumulative rating value.
14. The system computes the average rating according to the configured algorithm.
15. The system applies the configured decimal precision.
16. The system validates the calculated average rating.
17. The system updates the entity's average rating.
18. The system updates the total rating count.
19. The system updates the rating distribution.
20. The system updates review summary statistics.
21. The system synchronizes cached rating information.
22. The system updates search indexes.
23. The system updates recommendation data.
24. The system updates analytics datasets.
25. The system validates synchronization across dependent services.
26. The system records operational metrics.
27. The system records the calculation event in the audit log.
28. The system commits the transaction.
29. The system prepares the processing response.
30. The system notifies dependent services where applicable.
31. The updated average rating becomes available for public viewing.
32. The updated rating is reflected across all supported platforms.
33. The system completes post-processing activities.
34. Background monitoring validates successful completion.
35. The average rating calculation process completes successfully.

---

2. Database updates remain successful.

---

2. Average rating calculation remains successful.
3. Search indexing is retried asynchronously.

---

2. Rating calculation completes successfully.
3. Recommendation synchronization is retried later.

---

**AF-7: Analytics Service Failure**

2. Rating calculation continues successfully.
3. Analytics events are queued for retry.

---

**AF-8: Concurrent Rating Updates**

2. The system applies concurrency controls.
3. A consistent average rating is produced.

---

**AF-9: Database Failure**

2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal processing error is recorded.

## Review Management

### 3.10.19 Verify Review Eligibility

#### 3.10.19.1 Description

The **Verify Review Eligibility** use case enables the system to determine whether a customer is eligible to submit a rating or review for a restaurant, food item, or delivery partner. The verification process ensures that only genuine customers who have completed eligible orders can submit reviews, thereby preventing spam, fake reviews, duplicate reviews, and fraudulent review activities. The verification is performed automatically before allowing any review or rating submission and enforces all platform policies, business rules, and fraud prevention mechanisms.

#### 3.10.19.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Customer
- Review & Rating Service (System)

**Secondary Actors**

- Order Management Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Delivery Management Service
- Authentication Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Customer Management Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.19.3 Preconditions

1. The customer must be authenticated.
2. The customer account must exist.
3. The customer account must be active.
4. The reviewable entity must exist.
5. The related order must exist.
6. The order must belong to the authenticated customer.
7. The order must be successfully delivered or completed.
8. The order must not be cancelled.
9. The order must not be refunded according to configured policies where applicable.
10. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
11. Order Management Service must be operational.
12. Authentication Service must be operational.
13. Fraud Detection Service must be operational.
14. Database connectivity must be available.
15. Platform review policies must be configured.
16. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
17. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.19.4 Trigger

The customer attempts to submit a rating or review for a restaurant, food item, or delivery partner.

#### 3.10.19.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Customer ID | UUID | Yes | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Entity Type | Enum | Yes | Restaurant, Food Item, or Delivery Partner. |
| Entity ID | UUID | Yes | Identifier of the entity being reviewed. |
| Order ID | UUID | Yes | Completed order associated with the review. |
| Review Type | Enum | Yes | Rating, Review, or Both. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | User session identifier. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Request Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time of eligibility verification. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |
| Authentication Token | String | System Generated | Authenticated customer access token. |

#### 3.10.19.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to submit reviews.
2. Customers shall review only their own completed orders.
3. The order shall belong to the authenticated customer.
4. Only completed or delivered orders shall be eligible.
5. Cancelled orders shall not be eligible.
6. Fraudulent orders shall not be eligible.
7. Suspended customer accounts shall not be eligible.
8. Blocked customer accounts shall not be eligible.
9. One review per eligible entity per order shall be allowed unless platform policy permits updates.
10. Duplicate reviews shall be prevented.
11. Duplicate ratings shall be prevented.
12. Restaurant reviews shall require an eligible restaurant order.
13. Food item reviews shall require the purchased food item.
14. Delivery partner reviews shall require the assigned delivery partner.
15. Eligibility verification shall occur before review submission.
16. Fraud detection policies shall be enforced.
17. Suspicious review activities shall be flagged.
18. Eligibility decisions shall remain consistent across platforms.
19. Administrative overrides shall be audited.
20. Review eligibility shall comply with platform policies.
21. Order ownership shall be verified.
22. Authentication shall be verified.
23. Operational metrics shall be updated.
24. Analytics events shall be generated.
25. Audit records shall be generated.
26. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
27. Customer privacy shall be maintained.
28. Unauthorized review attempts shall be rejected.
29. Eligibility checks shall support high-volume processing.
30. Eligibility decisions shall support retry mechanisms where applicable.
31. Verification services shall maintain transactional consistency.
32. Eligibility results shall be deterministic.
33. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
34. Eligibility decisions shall comply with privacy regulations.
35. All eligibility verification operations shall comply with governance, moderation, fraud prevention, security, privacy, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.19.7 Validations

1. Customer ID shall exist.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Authentication token shall be valid.
4. Entity Type shall contain a supported value.
5. Entity ID shall exist.
6. Order ID shall exist.
7. Order ownership validation shall succeed.
8. Order status shall be Completed or Delivered.
9. Cancelled orders shall be rejected.
10. Fraudulent orders shall be rejected.
11. Suspended customer accounts shall be rejected.
12. Blocked customer accounts shall be rejected.
13. Duplicate review validation shall succeed.
14. Duplicate rating validation shall succeed.
15. Restaurant eligibility validation shall succeed.
16. Food item purchase validation shall succeed.
17. Delivery partner eligibility validation shall succeed.
18. Fraud detection validation shall succeed.
19. Database query validation shall succeed.
20. Authentication validation shall succeed.
21. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
22. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
23. Security validation shall complete successfully.
24. Concurrency validation shall succeed.
25. Platform policy validation shall succeed.
26. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
27. Eligibility result validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.19.8 Main Flow

1. The customer attempts to submit a rating or review.
2. The Review & Rating Service receives the request.
3. The system validates the customer's authentication token.
4. The system validates the Customer ID.
5. The system verifies that the customer account exists.
6. The system verifies that the customer account is active.
7. The system validates the Entity Type.
8. The system validates the Entity ID.
9. The system validates the Order ID.
10. The system verifies that the order exists.
11. The system verifies that the order belongs to the authenticated customer.
12. The system verifies that the order status is **Completed** or **Delivered**.
13. The system verifies that the order has not been cancelled.
14. The system verifies that the order satisfies the platform review policy.
15. The system verifies that the customer has not already submitted an eligible review for the same entity and order.
16. The system verifies that the customer has not already submitted an eligible rating where duplicates are prohibited.
17. The system verifies entity-specific eligibility.
18. For restaurant reviews, the system verifies the restaurant belongs to the completed order.
19. For food item reviews, the system verifies that the food item was purchased in the completed order.
20. For delivery partner reviews, the system verifies the assigned delivery partner.
21. The system performs fraud detection checks.
22. The system evaluates suspicious review patterns.
23. The system validates all configured eligibility rules.
24. The system determines the final eligibility status.
25. The system records eligibility verification analytics.
26. The system updates operational metrics.
27. The system records the verification activity in the audit log.
28. The system prepares the eligibility response.
29. The system returns the eligibility decision.
30. If eligible, the system enables the review submission interface.
31. If not eligible, the system displays the appropriate reason according to platform policy.
32. The system maintains transactional consistency.
33. Dependent services are notified where applicable.
34. Eligibility verification is completed.
35. The review eligibility verification process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.19.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Customer Not Authenticated**

1. The authentication token is missing or invalid.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. The customer is prompted to authenticate.

---

**AF-2: Order Not Found**

1. The supplied Order ID does not exist.
2. The system rejects the request.
3. An appropriate error response is returned.

---

**AF-3: Order Ownership Verification Failed**

1. The order does not belong to the authenticated customer.
2. The request is rejected.
3. An unauthorized access response is returned.

---

**AF-4: Order Not Eligible**

1. The order status is not eligible for reviews.
2. The eligibility verification fails.
3. The customer is informed accordingly.

---

**AF-5: Duplicate Review Detected**

1. The customer has already submitted a review for the eligible entity.
2. The system rejects duplicate review creation.
3. The customer may edit the existing review if permitted.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Triggered**

1. Fraud detection identifies suspicious activity.
2. The request is rejected or flagged for manual review.
3. Administrative notifications are generated where applicable.

---

**AF-7: Customer Account Restricted**

1. The customer account is suspended or blocked.
2. The eligibility verification fails.
3. Review submission is denied.

---

**AF-8: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs during verification.
2. The transaction is terminated.
3. An internal processing error is returned.

---

**AF-9: Concurrent Review Submission**

1. Multiple review requests are received simultaneously.
2. The system applies concurrency controls.
3. Only valid submissions proceed.

---

**AF-10: Platform Policy Change During Verification**

1. Review eligibility policies change during processing.
2. The latest committed policy is applied.
3. The final eligibility decision is returned.

#### 3.10.19.10 Postconditions

1. Customer eligibility shall be determined.
2. Eligible customers shall be allowed to continue with review submission.
3. Ineligible customers shall be prevented from submitting reviews.
4. Fraud detection results shall be recorded.
5. Operational metrics shall be updated.
6. Analytics events shall be generated.
7. Audit records shall be created.
8. Platform policies shall remain enforced.
9. Transaction consistency shall be maintained.
10. Eligibility decisions shall be available for dependent services.

|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Review eligibility verified successfully. |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Entity Type | Restaurant, Food Item, or Delivery Partner. |
| Entity ID | Unique identifier of the entity. |
| Order ID | Verified completed order identifier. |
| Eligibility Status | Eligible or Not Eligible. |
| Eligibility Reason | Explanation according to platform policy. |

|------------|-------------|

| 401 | Authentication failed. |
| 403 | Customer is not eligible to submit a review. |
| 404 | Order or entity not found. |

| 422 | Platform eligibility rules not satisfied. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Eligibility verification service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.19.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authenticated customers shall submit reviews.
2. Customers shall review only completed eligible orders.
3. Duplicate reviews shall be prevented according to platform policy.
4. Fraud detection policies shall be enforced.
5. Order ownership verification shall always succeed before review submission.
6. Eligibility verification shall maintain transactional consistency.
7. Customer privacy shall be maintained.
8. Analytics events shall be generated successfully.
9. Audit records shall be generated successfully.
10. Eligibility decisions shall remain consistent across all supported platforms.

#### 3.10.19.14 Security Considerations

1. Authentication shall be mandatory before eligibility verification.
2. Order ownership shall always be validated.
3. Unauthorized review attempts shall be rejected.
4. Fraud detection mechanisms shall protect against fake reviews.
5. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
6. Service-to-service communication shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
7. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Administrative overrides shall require authorization.
10. Eligibility verification shall comply with applicable security and privacy regulations.

2. Order information is accurate and synchronized.
3. Fraud detection services remain operational.
4. Eligibility rules are centrally managed.
5. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
6. Eligibility verification supports future policy enhancements.

### 3.10.20 Upload Review Images

#### 3.10.20.1 Description

The **Upload Review Images** use case enables eligible customers to upload one or more images along with their restaurant, food item, or delivery partner reviews. The uploaded images provide visual evidence of the customer's experience and help other users make informed decisions. Before publication, the system validates image format, file size, image quality, security, moderation policies, and content guidelines. Only approved images are associated with the corresponding review and made publicly visible.

#### 3.10.20.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Media Management Service
- Image Storage Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Authentication Service
- Malware Scanning Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.20.3 Preconditions

1. The customer must be authenticated.
2. The customer account must be active.
3. The customer must be eligible to submit the review.
4. The reviewable entity must exist.
5. The associated review must exist or be created during submission.
6. The uploaded image must originate from the customer.
7. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
8. Media Management Service must be operational.
9. Image Storage Service must be operational.
10. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
11. Malware Scanning Service must be operational.
12. Database connectivity must be available.
13. Sufficient storage capacity must be available.
14. Platform image upload policies must be configured.
15. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
16. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
17. Network connectivity must be available.
18. Supported image formats must be configured.

#### 3.10.20.4 Trigger

The customer selects the **Upload Images** option while creating or editing a review.

| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | User session identifier. |
| Upload Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Image upload time. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |

#### 3.10.20.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall upload review images.
2. Images shall be associated with an eligible review.
3. Only supported image formats shall be accepted.
4. Maximum file size shall comply with platform configuration.
5. Maximum image count per review shall comply with platform policy.
6. Duplicate image uploads may be prevented.
7. Images shall undergo malware scanning.
8. Images shall undergo automated content moderation.
9. Images containing prohibited content shall be rejected.
10. Images containing illegal content shall be rejected.
11. Corrupted image files shall be rejected.
12. Image metadata may be sanitized before storage.
13. Images shall be securely stored.
14. Public access shall be available only after approval.
15. Image uploads shall maintain transactional consistency.
16. Failed uploads shall not create incomplete review records.
17. Storage references shall remain synchronized.
18. Cached image metadata shall remain synchronized.
19. Image thumbnails shall be generated where applicable.
20. Image optimization may be performed automatically.
21. Analytics events shall be generated.
22. Operational metrics shall be updated.
23. Audit records shall be generated.
24. Administrative moderation decisions shall be auditable.
25. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
26. Customer privacy shall be maintained.
27. Personally identifiable information embedded in metadata may be removed.
28. Upload operations shall support retry mechanisms.
29. Upload operations shall support horizontal scalability.
30. Image visibility policies shall be enforced.
31. Image lifecycle management shall follow retention policies.
32. Unauthorized uploads shall be rejected.
33. Image upload performance shall satisfy platform requirements.
34. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
35. All review image upload operations shall comply with governance, moderation, security, privacy, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.20.7 Validations

2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Review ID shall exist.
4. Entity Type shall contain a supported value.
5. Entity ID shall exist.
6. Image file shall be provided.
7. Uploaded file format shall be supported.
8. Uploaded file size shall not exceed configured limits.
9. Maximum image count shall not exceed platform limits.
10. Image integrity validation shall succeed.
11. Corrupted files shall be rejected.
12. Malware scan validation shall succeed.
13. Content moderation validation shall succeed.
14. Image resolution validation shall satisfy platform requirements.
15. Storage availability validation shall succeed.
16. Duplicate image validation shall succeed.
17. Review ownership validation shall succeed.
18. Eligibility validation shall succeed.
19. Database validation shall succeed.
20. Storage service validation shall succeed.
21. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
22. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
23. Security validation shall complete successfully.
24. Transaction validation shall succeed.
25. Metadata sanitization validation shall succeed.
26. Thumbnail generation validation shall succeed where applicable.
27. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.20.8 Main Flow

1. The customer opens the review submission or review editing page.
2. The customer selects the **Upload Images** option.
3. The system displays the supported image upload guidelines.
4. The customer selects one or more image files.
5. The system receives the uploaded images.
6. The system validates the customer's authentication.
7. The system validates the Review ID.
8. The system validates the Entity Type.
9. The system validates the Entity ID.
10. The system verifies the customer's review ownership.
11. The system validates the uploaded file formats.
12. The system validates the file sizes.
13. The system validates the maximum allowed image count.
14. The system performs image integrity validation.
15. The system scans the uploaded images for malware.
16. The system sanitizes image metadata where applicable.
17. The system submits the images for automated content moderation.
18. The system validates that the images comply with platform policies.
19. The system generates optimized image versions where applicable.
20. The system generates image thumbnails.
21. The system securely stores the original images.
22. The system stores optimized image versions.
23. The system creates image metadata records.
24. The system associates the uploaded images with the review.
25. The system updates the review media count.
26. The system synchronizes cached image metadata.
27. The system records upload analytics.
28. The system updates operational metrics.
29. The system records the upload activity in the audit log.
30. The system prepares the upload response.
31. The system returns the uploaded image information.
32. The uploaded images become available according to moderation policy.
33. The review page is refreshed with the uploaded images.
34. The customer verifies the uploaded images.
35. The review image upload process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.20.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Unsupported Image Format**

1. The uploaded image format is not supported.
2. The system rejects the file.
3. The customer is informed to upload a supported image format.

---

**AF-2: File Size Exceeded**

1. One or more uploaded files exceed the configured size limit.
2. The system rejects the oversized files.
3. The customer is notified of the permitted file size.

---

**AF-3: Maximum Image Limit Exceeded**

1. The customer uploads more than the permitted number of images.
2. The system rejects the additional images.
3. The customer is informed of the maximum upload limit.

---

**AF-4: Malware Detected**

1. Malware scanning identifies a malicious file.
2. The upload is immediately rejected.
3. The security event is logged.

---

**AF-5: Content Moderation Failure**

1. Automated moderation detects prohibited or inappropriate content.
2. The image is rejected or held for manual review.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-6: Storage Service Failure**

1. The image storage service becomes unavailable.
2. The upload transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal processing error is returned.

---

**AF-7: Thumbnail Generation Failure**

1. Thumbnail generation fails.
2. The original image remains stored successfully.
3. Thumbnail generation is retried asynchronously.

---

**AF-8: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while saving image metadata.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-9: Concurrent Upload Requests**

1. Multiple upload requests are received simultaneously.
2. The system applies concurrency controls.
3. Upload consistency is maintained.

---

**AF-10: Network Interruption**

1. Network connectivity is interrupted during upload.
2. The upload is terminated or resumed according to platform policy.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

2. Image metadata shall be created successfully.
3. Uploaded images shall be associated with the corresponding review.
4. Image thumbnails shall be generated where applicable.
5. Cached image information shall be synchronized.
6. Analytics events shall be generated.
7. Operational metrics shall be updated.
8. Audit records shall be created.
9. Moderation workflows shall be initiated where applicable.
10. Approved images shall become available across supported platforms.

### 3.10.21 View Customer Review History

#### 3.10.21.1 Description

The **View Customer Review History** use case enables authenticated customers to view the complete history of ratings and reviews they have submitted for restaurants, food items, and delivery partners. The feature provides a centralized view of review activities, including review content, ratings, uploaded images, review status, moderation status, creation date, last modification date, and related entity information. Customers can monitor their review contributions, edit eligible reviews, delete reviews, and track moderation outcomes while maintaining privacy and security.

#### 3.10.21.2 Actors

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Customer Management Service

- Audit Logging Service

12. Cache Management Service must be operational if enabled.
13. Analytics Service must be operational.

17. Network connectivity must be available.

The authenticated customer opens the **My Reviews** or **Review History** section from the profile or account menu.

7. Review Status Filter shall contain supported values.
8. Date Range validation shall succeed.
9. Sort By shall contain supported values.
10. Customer ownership validation shall succeed.
11. Database query validation shall succeed.
12. Cache validation shall succeed.
13. Media reference validation shall succeed.
14. Review status validation shall succeed.
15. Entity reference validation shall succeed.
16. Pagination validation shall succeed.
17. Search parameter validation shall succeed where applicable.
18. Review visibility validation shall succeed.
19. Security validation shall complete successfully.
20. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
21. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
22. Analytics validation shall succeed.
23. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
24. Transaction validation shall succeed.
25. Platform policy validation shall succeed.
26. Privacy policy validation shall succeed.
27. Response validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.21.8 Main Flow

1. The customer logs into the platform.
2. The customer navigates to the **My Reviews** or **Review History** section.
3. The system receives the review history request.
4. The system validates the customer's authentication.
5. The system validates the Customer ID.
6. The system verifies that the customer account is active.
7. The system validates the request parameters.
8. The system applies the selected filters where applicable.
9. The system applies the selected sorting criteria.
10. The system applies pagination settings.
11. The system retrieves all review records belonging to the customer.
12. The system verifies review ownership.
13. The system retrieves associated rating information.
14. The system retrieves related restaurant information.
15. The system retrieves related food item information where applicable.
16. The system retrieves related delivery partner information where applicable.
17. The system retrieves uploaded review images where available.
18. The system retrieves moderation status.
19. The system retrieves review creation timestamps.
20. The system retrieves last modification timestamps.
21. The system determines edit eligibility for each review.
22. The system determines deletion eligibility for each review.
23. The system synchronizes cached review history where applicable.
24. The system validates the retrieved data.
25. The system prepares pagination metadata.
26. The system records review history analytics.
27. The system updates operational metrics.
28. The system records the activity in the audit log.
29. The system prepares the response payload.
30. The system returns the review history.
31. The review history is displayed to the customer.
32. The customer reviews the displayed information.
33. The customer may select a review for further actions where permitted.
34. The system completes post-processing activities.
35. The review history retrieval process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.21.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: No Reviews Available**

1. The customer has not submitted any reviews.
2. The system returns an empty review history.
3. The customer is informed that no reviews are available.

---

**AF-2: Authentication Failure**

1. Customer authentication fails.
2. The request is rejected.
3. The customer is prompted to log in again.

---

**AF-3: Invalid Pagination Parameters**

1. Invalid page number or page size is supplied.
2. The system rejects the request or applies default pagination.
3. The customer receives an appropriate response.

---

**AF-4: Invalid Filter Criteria**

1. One or more filter values are invalid.
2. The system rejects the request or ignores unsupported filters according to platform policy.
3. The customer receives an appropriate response.

---

**AF-5: Cache Miss**

1. Cached review history is unavailable.
2. The system retrieves fresh data from the database.
3. The response is returned successfully.

---

**AF-6: Media Retrieval Failure**

1. Review image retrieval fails.
2. The review history remains available.
3. Missing media is omitted from the response.

---

**AF-7: Analytics Service Failure**

1. Analytics event generation fails.
2. Review history retrieval continues successfully.
3. The analytics event is queued for retry.

---

**AF-8: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while retrieving review history.
2. The request is terminated.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-9: Concurrent Review Update**

1. One or more reviews are updated during retrieval.
2. The system maintains transactional consistency.
3. The latest committed review data is returned.

---

**AF-10: Customer Account Restricted**

1. The customer account becomes restricted during processing.
2. The request is terminated.
3. An appropriate notification is returned.

#### 3.10.21.10 Postconditions

1. Customer review history shall be retrieved successfully.
2. Review ownership shall remain verified.
3. Pagination metadata shall be updated.
4. Cached review history shall remain synchronized.
5. Analytics events shall be generated.
6. Operational metrics shall be updated.
7. Audit records shall be created.
8. Customer privacy shall be maintained.
9. Review edit and delete eligibility shall be determined.
10. Review history shall be available across supported platforms.

#### 3.10.21.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Review history retrieved successfully. |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Total Reviews | Number of reviews submitted by the customer. |
| Reviews | List of customer review records. |
| Pagination | Pagination information. |
| Applied Filters | Active filter criteria. |
| Retrieved Timestamp | Response generation timestamp. |

#### 3.10.21.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid request parameters. |
| 401 | Authentication failed. |
| 403 | Access denied. |
| 404 | Customer review history not found. |
| 422 | Invalid filter or pagination parameters. |
| 429 | Too many requests. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Review history service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.21.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authenticated customers shall access their review history.
2. Customers shall view only their own reviews.
3. Pagination, filtering, and sorting shall function correctly.
4. Review ownership shall always be verified.
5. Review status shall be displayed accurately.
6. Uploaded review images shall be displayed where available.
7. Customer privacy shall be maintained.
8. Analytics events shall be generated successfully.
9. Audit records shall be generated successfully.
10. Review history shall remain consistent across all supported platforms.

#### 3.10.21.14 Security Considerations

1. Authentication shall be mandatory before viewing review history.
2. Customers shall access only their own review records.
3. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
4. Unauthorized review access shall be prevented.
5. Service-to-service communication shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
6. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
7. Security-related events shall be logged.
8. Cached review data shall comply with access control policies.
9. Administrative access shall require appropriate authorization.
10. Review history operations shall comply with applicable security and privacy regulations.

#### 3.10.21.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information:

- Audit Log ID
- Customer ID
- Session ID
- Device Type
- Applied Filters
- Sort Criteria
- Page Number
- Page Size
- Total Reviews Returned
- Request Timestamp
- IP Address
- Request Correlation ID
- Response Status
- Processing Duration

#### 3.10.21.16 Assumptions

1. Customers are authenticated before viewing review history.
2. Review ownership data remains accurate.
3. Cached review history is periodically synchronized.
4. Analytics events may be processed asynchronously.
5. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
6. Platform retention policies govern historical review availability.

#### 3.10.21.17 Dependencies

- Authentication Service

- Media Management Service

### 3.10.22 Notify Review Submission

#### 3.10.22.1 Description

The **Notify Review Submission** use case enables the system to notify customers after a review or rating has been successfully submitted. The notification confirms that the review has been received and informs the customer whether the review is immediately published or pending moderation. The system may deliver notifications through in-app messages, push notifications, email, or other supported communication channels according to customer notification preferences and platform configuration.

#### 3.10.22.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service (System)

**Secondary Actors**

- Notification Service
- Customer
- Authentication Service
- Customer Preference Service
- Email Service
- Push Notification Service
- In-App Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.22.3 Preconditions

1. The review submission must be completed successfully.
2. The review record must exist.
3. The customer account must exist.
4. The customer account must be active.
5. The customer must be authenticated during submission.
6. Notification Service must be operational.
7. Customer Preference Service must be operational.
8. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
9. Database connectivity must be available.
10. Notification templates must be configured.
11. Supported notification channels must be configured.
12. Customer notification preferences must be available.
13. Analytics Service must be operational.
14. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
15. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
16. Notification retry policies must be configured.
17. Communication channels must be available.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.22.4 Trigger

The customer successfully submits a rating or review.

#### 3.10.22.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Submitted review identifier. |
| Customer ID | UUID | Yes | Customer receiving the notification. |
| Entity Type | Enum | Yes | Restaurant, Food Item, or Delivery Partner. |
| Entity ID | UUID | Yes | Reviewed entity identifier. |
| Review Status | Enum | Yes | Pending, Approved, Published, or Under Moderation. |
| Notification Type | Enum | Yes | In-App, Push, Email, SMS (if supported). |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | Customer session identifier. |
| Notification Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Notification generation time. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |
| Template ID | UUID | System Generated | Notification template identifier. |

#### 3.10.22.6 Business Rules

1. Notifications shall be sent only after successful review submission.
2. Failed review submissions shall not trigger notifications.
3. Customer notification preferences shall be honored.
4. Notification templates shall be centrally managed.
5. Notification content shall include the review submission status.
6. Review IDs shall be included only where appropriate.
7. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
8. Notifications shall support multiple delivery channels.
9. Duplicate notifications shall be prevented.
10. Notification delivery shall support retry mechanisms.
11. Failed deliveries shall be logged.
12. Notification status shall be tracked.
13. In-app notifications shall be created where enabled.
14. Push notifications shall be delivered where enabled.
15. Email notifications shall be delivered where enabled.
16. SMS notifications shall be delivered only if supported and enabled.
17. Notification content shall support localization.
18. Notification templates shall support dynamic placeholders.
19. Delivery events shall be recorded.
20. Analytics events shall be generated.
21. Operational metrics shall be updated.
22. Audit records shall be generated.
23. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
24. Customer privacy preferences shall be respected.
25. Unauthorized notification delivery shall be prevented.
26. Notification services shall support asynchronous processing.
27. Delivery latency shall satisfy platform performance requirements.
28. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
29. Notification failures shall not affect review submission.
30. Delivery status shall remain synchronized across services.
31. Historical notification records shall follow retention policies.
32. Notification channels shall be independently configurable.
33. Administrative notification overrides shall be audited.
34. Notification processing shall support horizontal scalability.
35. All notification operations shall comply with governance, security, privacy, communication, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.22.7 Validations

1. Review ID shall exist.
2. Customer ID shall exist.
3. Customer account shall be active.
4. Entity Type shall contain a supported value.
5. Entity ID shall exist.
6. Review Status shall contain a supported value.
7. Notification Type shall contain a supported value.
8. Customer notification preferences shall be available.
9. Notification template validation shall succeed.
10. Notification channel validation shall succeed.
11. Delivery endpoint validation shall succeed.
12. Database validation shall succeed.
13. Notification service validation shall succeed.
14. Email address validation shall succeed where applicable.
15. Push token validation shall succeed where applicable.
16. In-app notification validation shall succeed.
17. Localization validation shall succeed.
18. Duplicate notification validation shall succeed.
19. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
20. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
21. Security validation shall complete successfully.
22. Analytics validation shall succeed.
23. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
24. Retry policy validation shall succeed.
25. Delivery status validation shall succeed.
26. Communication channel availability validation shall succeed.
27. Template placeholder validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.22.8 Main Flow

1. The customer successfully submits a review or rating.
2. The Review & Rating Service receives the successful submission event.
3. The system validates the Review ID.
4. The system validates the Customer ID.
5. The system verifies that the review submission was completed successfully.
6. The system retrieves the review details.
7. The system retrieves the customer notification preferences.
8. The system determines the supported notification channels.
9. The system determines the review moderation status.
10. The system selects the appropriate notification template.
11. The system populates dynamic template placeholders.
12. The system generates the notification content.
13. The system validates the notification payload.
14. The system creates an in-app notification where enabled.
15. The system sends a push notification where enabled.
16. The system sends an email notification where enabled.
17. The system sends an SMS notification where supported and enabled.
18. The system tracks the delivery status of each notification.
19. The system records successful notification deliveries.
20. The system schedules retries for failed notification attempts according to retry policies.
21. The system updates notification history.
22. The system synchronizes notification status across services.
23. The system records notification analytics.
24. The system updates operational metrics.
25. The system records the notification activity in the audit log.
26. The system validates notification completion.
27. The system prepares the notification response.
28. The system returns the notification processing result.
29. The notification becomes visible through the configured channels.
30. The customer receives the notification.
31. The customer views the review submission confirmation.
32. The system completes asynchronous notification processing where applicable.
33. Background monitoring validates successful delivery.
34. Dependent services receive notification status updates where applicable.
35. The review submission notification process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.22.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Customer Disabled Notifications**

1. The customer has disabled one or more notification channels.
2. The system skips the disabled channels.
3. Notifications are delivered only through enabled channels.

---

**AF-2: Push Notification Failure**

1. Push notification delivery fails.
2. The failure is recorded.
3. The system retries delivery according to the configured retry policy.

---

**AF-3: Email Delivery Failure**

1. Email delivery fails.
2. The failure is logged.
3. The email is scheduled for retry.

---

**AF-4: SMS Gateway Failure**

1. SMS delivery fails due to gateway unavailability.
2. The failure is recorded.
3. SMS delivery is retried where supported.

---

**AF-5: Invalid Notification Template**

1. The configured notification template is unavailable or invalid.
2. The system applies the default template where configured.
3. Notification processing continues.

---

**AF-6: Notification Service Failure**

1. The Notification Service becomes unavailable.
2. The notification request is queued.
3. Delivery resumes when the service becomes available.

---

**AF-7: Analytics Service Failure**

1. Analytics event generation fails.
2. Notification delivery continues successfully.
3. Analytics events are queued for retry.

---

**AF-8: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while storing notification history.
2. The transaction is partially rolled back according to platform policy.
3. An internal processing error is recorded.

---

**AF-9: Duplicate Notification Request**

1. A duplicate notification request is received.
2. The system detects the duplicate.
3. Duplicate notification delivery is prevented.

---

**AF-10: Network Failure**

1. Network connectivity is interrupted during notification delivery.
2. Delivery retries are initiated according to retry policies.
3. Notification processing resumes after connectivity is restored.

#### 3.10.22.10 Postconditions

1. Notification shall be generated successfully.
2. Notification delivery status shall be recorded.
3. Notification history shall be updated.
4. Customer notification preferences shall remain enforced.
5. Analytics events shall be generated.
6. Operational metrics shall be updated.
7. Audit records shall be created.
8. Retry requests shall be scheduled for failed deliveries where applicable.
9. Notification status shall remain synchronized across services.
10. Review submission confirmation shall be available through supported communication channels.

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Review submission notification processed successfully. |
| Review ID | Submitted review identifier. |
| Customer ID | Notification recipient identifier. |
| Notification Channels | Channels used for delivery. |

| Review Status | Submitted, Pending Moderation, or Published. |

#### 3.10.22.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Notifications shall be generated only after successful review submission.
2. Customer notification preferences shall be respected.
3. Duplicate notifications shall not be delivered.
4. Notification templates shall be applied correctly.
5. Notification delivery status shall be tracked accurately.
6. Failed notifications shall follow configured retry policies.
7. Customer privacy shall be maintained.
8. Analytics events shall be generated successfully.
9. Audit records shall be generated successfully.
10. Review submission notifications shall be available across all supported communication channels.

#### 3.10.22.14 Security Considerations

1. Notification requests shall originate only from authorized system services.
2. Customer notification preferences shall be protected.
3. Personally identifiable information shall be masked where applicable.
4. Notification payloads shall be securely transmitted.
5. Unauthorized notification delivery shall be prevented.
6. Service-to-service communication shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
7. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Notification templates shall be protected from unauthorized modification.
10. Notification operations shall comply with applicable security and privacy regulations.

#### 3.10.22.15 Audit Log Requirements

2. Customer notification preferences are available and up to date.
3. Notification services remain operational.
4. Retry mechanisms are configured according to platform policy.
5. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
6. Notification templates are centrally managed.
