## Review Moderation

### 3.10.23 Report Inappropriate Review

#### 3.10.23.1 Description

The **Report Inappropriate Review** use case enables authenticated customers to report reviews that violate the platform's community guidelines, content policies, or legal requirements. Customers can report reviews containing spam, abusive language, hate speech, harassment, fake information, offensive content, promotional material, illegal content, or other inappropriate behavior. The reported review is submitted to the Content Moderation Service for automated and/or manual investigation. The reporting customer is notified that the report has been received, while the review remains subject to moderation according to platform policies.

#### 3.10.23.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Customer

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Authentication Service
- Customer Management Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.23.3 Preconditions

1. The customer must be authenticated.
2. The customer account must exist.
3. The customer account must be active.
4. The reported review must exist.
5. The reported review must be publicly accessible.
6. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
7. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
8. Authentication Service must be operational.
9. Database connectivity must be available.
10. Report reason categories must be configured.
11. Moderation workflows must be configured.
12. Notification Service must be operational.
13. Fraud Detection Service must be operational.
14. Analytics Service must be operational.
15. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
16. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
17. Community guidelines must be configured.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.23.4 Trigger

The authenticated customer selects the **Report Review** option for a published review.

#### 3.10.23.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Customer ID | UUID | Yes | Authenticated customer identifier. |
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Reported review identifier. |
| Report Category | Enum | Yes | Spam, Abuse, Fake Review, Hate Speech, Offensive Content, Illegal Content, or Other. |
| Report Description | String | No | Optional explanation provided by the customer. |
| Supporting Evidence | File[] | No | Optional supporting screenshots or documents. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Android, iOS, or Web. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | User session identifier. |
| Report Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time when the report is submitted. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |

#### 3.10.23.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall report reviews.
2. Only existing reviews shall be reported.
3. A customer may report the same review only according to platform policy.
4. Report categories shall be centrally configurable.
5. Report descriptions shall be optional unless required by policy.
6. Supporting evidence shall be optional.
7. Uploaded evidence shall undergo security validation.
8. Reported reviews shall be submitted to the Content Moderation Service.
9. Automated moderation may classify reports before manual review.
10. Fraudulent reporting behavior shall be monitored.
11. Duplicate reports shall be handled according to platform policy.
12. Report submission shall not immediately remove the review.
13. Moderation decisions shall determine review visibility.
14. Customer identities shall remain confidential where applicable.
15. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
16. Community guidelines shall be enforced.
17. Platform content policies shall be enforced.
18. Notification events shall be generated where applicable.
19. Analytics events shall be generated.
20. Operational metrics shall be updated.
21. Audit records shall be generated.
22. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
23. Unauthorized report submissions shall be rejected.
24. Report processing shall maintain transactional consistency.
25. Report status shall be tracked throughout the moderation lifecycle.
26. Moderation queues shall support prioritization.
27. Historical report records shall follow retention policies.
28. Administrative moderation actions shall be auditable.
29. Report processing shall support horizontal scalability.
30. Report workflows shall support automated escalation.
31. False reporting patterns may trigger fraud detection rules.
32. Report visibility shall follow access control policies.
33. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
34. Report processing shall comply with applicable legal requirements.
35. All review reporting operations shall comply with governance, moderation, fraud prevention, security, privacy, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.23.7 Validations

1. Customer ID shall exist.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Authentication validation shall succeed.
4. Review ID shall exist.
5. Report Category shall contain a supported value.
6. Reported review shall be publicly accessible.
7. Duplicate report validation shall succeed.
8. Report ownership validation shall follow platform policy.
9. Supporting evidence format shall be supported where provided.
10. Supporting evidence size shall comply with configured limits.
11. Malware scan validation shall succeed for uploaded evidence.
12. Database validation shall succeed.
13. Content Moderation Service validation shall succeed.
14. Notification Service validation shall succeed.
15. Fraud detection validation shall succeed.
16. Community guideline validation shall succeed.
17. Platform policy validation shall succeed.
18. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
19. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
20. Security validation shall complete successfully.
21. Analytics validation shall succeed.
22. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
23. Transaction validation shall succeed.
24. Evidence integrity validation shall succeed.
25. Report description length shall comply with configured limits.
26. Request rate limiting validation shall succeed.
27. Review status validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.23.8 Main Flow

1. The customer views a published review.
2. The customer selects the **Report Review** option.
3. The system displays the available report categories.
4. The customer selects an appropriate report category.
5. The customer optionally enters a report description.
6. The customer optionally uploads supporting evidence.
7. The customer submits the report.
8. The Review & Rating Service receives the report request.
9. The system validates the customer's authentication.
10. The system validates the Customer ID.
11. The system validates the Review ID.
12. The system verifies that the reported review exists.
13. The system verifies that the review is eligible for reporting.
14. The system validates the selected report category.
15. The system validates the report description where provided.
16. The system validates uploaded supporting evidence where provided.
17. The system scans uploaded evidence for malware.
18. The system checks for duplicate reports according to platform policy.
19. The system performs fraud detection checks.
20. The system creates a review report record.
21. The system assigns a unique Report ID.
22. The system submits the report to the Content Moderation Service.
23. The system assigns the report to the appropriate moderation queue.
24. The system determines the initial moderation status.
25. The system generates an acknowledgment notification.
26. The system records report submission analytics.
27. The system updates operational metrics.
28. The system records the reporting activity in the audit log.
29. The system prepares the response payload.
30. The system returns a successful report submission response.
31. The customer receives confirmation that the report has been submitted.
32. The review continues to follow platform visibility policies until moderation is completed.
33. Background moderation workflows begin processing the report.
34. Dependent services receive report status updates where applicable.
35. The review reporting process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.23.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Authentication Failure**

1. Customer authentication fails.
2. The report request is rejected.
3. The customer is prompted to authenticate.

---

**AF-2: Review Not Found**

1. The specified review does not exist.
2. The report request is rejected.
3. An appropriate error response is returned.

---

**AF-3: Duplicate Report Detected**

1. The customer has already reported the review according to platform policy.
2. The duplicate report is rejected or merged.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-4: Invalid Report Category**

1. An unsupported report category is selected.
2. The request is rejected.
3. The customer is asked to select a valid category.

---

**AF-5: Malware Detected in Supporting Evidence**

1. Uploaded evidence contains malicious content.
2. The upload is rejected.
3. The security event is logged.

---

**AF-6: Fraudulent Reporting Activity Detected**

1. Fraud detection identifies suspicious reporting behavior.
2. The report is flagged for additional verification.
3. Administrative alerts are generated where applicable.

---

**AF-7: Content Moderation Service Failure**

1. The moderation service becomes unavailable.
2. The report is queued for later processing.
3. The customer receives a successful acknowledgment where applicable.

---

**AF-8: Notification Service Failure**

1. Confirmation notification generation fails.
2. Report submission remains successful.
3. Notification delivery is retried asynchronously.

---

**AF-9: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while storing the report.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-10: Network Failure**

1. Network connectivity is interrupted during report submission.
2. The request fails or is retried according to platform policy.
3. The customer receives an appropriate notification.

#### 3.10.23.10 Postconditions

1. The review report shall be created successfully.
2. A unique Report ID shall be assigned.
3. The report shall be added to the moderation queue.
4. Initial moderation status shall be recorded.
5. Notification events shall be generated where applicable.
6. Analytics events shall be generated.
7. Operational metrics shall be updated.
8. Audit records shall be created.
9. Fraud detection results shall be recorded where applicable.
10. The reported review shall continue to follow platform moderation policies.

#### 3.10.23.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Review reported successfully. |
| Report ID | Unique report identifier. |
| Review ID | Reported review identifier. |
| Report Category | Selected reporting reason. |
| Moderation Status | Pending Review. |
| Report Timestamp | Time when the report was submitted. |

#### 3.10.23.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid report request. |
| 401 | Authentication failed. |
| 403 | Customer is not authorized to report the review. |
| 404 | Review not found. |
| 409 | Duplicate report detected. |
| 422 | Report validation failed. |
| 429 | Too many reporting requests. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Content moderation service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.23.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authenticated customers shall report reviews.
2. Only existing reviews shall be eligible for reporting.
3. Duplicate reports shall be handled according to platform policy.
4. Uploaded supporting evidence shall pass security validation.
5. Every valid report shall receive a unique Report ID.
6. Every report shall be submitted to the moderation workflow.
7. Customer privacy shall be maintained.
8. Analytics events shall be generated successfully.
9. Audit records shall be generated successfully.
10. Reported reviews shall follow platform moderation policies until a final decision is made.

#### 3.10.23.14 Security Considerations

1. Authentication shall be mandatory before reporting reviews.
2. Uploaded supporting evidence shall undergo malware scanning.
3. Fraudulent reporting activities shall be detected and monitored.
4. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
5. Unauthorized report submissions shall be rejected.
6. Service-to-service communication shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
7. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Moderation records shall be accessible only to authorized personnel.
10. Review reporting operations shall comply with applicable security, privacy, and legal regulations.

#### 3.10.23.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information:

- Audit Log ID
- Report ID
- Customer ID
- Review ID
- Report Category
- Moderation Status
- Supporting Evidence Count
- Fraud Detection Result
- Device Type
- Session ID
- IP Address
- Report Timestamp
- Request Correlation ID
- Processing Duration
- Service Instance ID

#### 3.10.23.16 Assumptions

1. Customers are authenticated before reporting reviews.
2. Community guideline categories are centrally managed.
3. Content Moderation Service remains operational.
4. Fraud detection rules are periodically updated.
5. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
6. Report retention policies are configured and enforced.

### 3.10.24 Moderate Reviews

#### 3.10.24.1 Description

The **Moderate Reviews** use case enables the platform to review, evaluate, approve, reject, hide, restore, or remove customer reviews that are submitted to the system or reported by users. Moderation ensures that reviews comply with community guidelines, platform policies, legal requirements, and content standards before they become publicly visible or remain published. Moderation may be performed automatically using AI-based content analysis, manually by authorized moderators, or through a hybrid workflow. All moderation decisions are recorded for auditing, analytics, compliance, and future reference.

#### 3.10.24.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Moderator
- Content Moderation Service

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Authentication Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Customer Management Service
- AI Content Moderation Engine

#### 3.10.24.3 Preconditions

1. The review must exist.
2. The review must be accessible to the moderation workflow.
3. The moderator must be authenticated.
4. The moderator must possess review moderation privileges.
5. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
6. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
7. Authentication Service must be operational.
8. AI Content Moderation Engine must be operational where enabled.
9. Fraud Detection Service must be operational.
10. Database connectivity must be available.
11. Community guidelines must be configured.
12. Platform moderation policies must be configured.
13. Notification Service must be operational.
14. Analytics Service must be operational.
15. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
16. Platform security policies must be be satisfied.
17. Moderation queues must be available.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.24.4 Trigger

A newly submitted review, an automatically flagged review, or a customer-reported review enters the moderation workflow.

#### 3.10.24.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Moderator ID | UUID | Yes | Authenticated moderator identifier. |
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Review selected for moderation. |
| Moderation Action | Enum | Yes | Approve, Reject, Hide, Restore, Remove, Escalate. |
| Moderation Reason | Enum | Yes | Spam, Offensive Content, Fake Review, Hate Speech, Harassment, Illegal Content, or Other. |
| Moderator Comments | String | No | Internal moderation notes. |
| Evidence Reference | UUID | No | Supporting moderation evidence if applicable. |
| Moderation Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time of moderation action. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | Moderator session identifier. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Web, Mobile, or Admin Portal. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |
| AI Moderation Score | Decimal | System Generated | AI-generated content risk score where applicable. |

#### 3.10.24.6 Business Rules

1. Only authorized moderators shall moderate reviews.
2. Every review shall have a unique moderation record.
3. Moderation actions shall follow platform policies.
4. AI-assisted moderation may provide recommendations but shall not override configured approval workflows.
5. Community guidelines shall be enforced.
6. Illegal content shall be removed immediately according to platform policy.
7. Hate speech shall be handled according to platform policies.
8. Spam reviews shall be removed or hidden.
9. Fraudulent reviews shall be investigated.
10. Duplicate reviews shall follow moderation policies.
11. Customer-reported reviews shall enter moderation queues.
12. High-risk reviews may be prioritized.
13. Moderation decisions shall be auditable.
14. Moderator comments shall remain confidential.
15. Customers shall be notified where applicable.
16. Review visibility shall follow moderation status.
17. Analytics events shall be generated.
18. Operational metrics shall be updated.
19. Audit records shall be generated.
20. Moderation queues shall support prioritization.
21. Escalation workflows shall be supported.
22. Administrative overrides shall be audited.
23. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
24. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
25. Unauthorized moderation actions shall be prevented.
26. Moderation shall maintain transactional consistency.
27. Moderation actions shall support retry mechanisms where applicable.
28. Historical moderation records shall follow retention policies.
29. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
30. Moderation processing shall support horizontal scalability.
31. Fraud detection signals shall be incorporated into moderation decisions.
32. Automated moderation decisions shall be reviewable where required.
33. Moderation decisions shall comply with applicable legal regulations.
34. Review lifecycle states shall remain synchronized across dependent services.
35. All moderation operations shall comply with governance, compliance, moderation, security, privacy, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.24.7 Validations

1. Moderator ID shall exist.
2. Moderator authentication shall succeed.
3. Moderator authorization shall succeed.
4. Review ID shall exist.
5. Review status shall be valid for moderation.
6. Moderation Action shall contain a supported value.
7. Moderation Reason shall contain a supported value.
8. Community guideline validation shall succeed.
9. Platform policy validation shall succeed.
10. AI moderation score validation shall succeed where applicable.
11. Fraud detection validation shall succeed.
12. Database validation shall succeed.
13. Moderation queue validation shall succeed.
14. Notification Service validation shall succeed.
15. Analytics validation shall succeed.
16. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
17. Transaction validation shall succeed.
18. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
19. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
20. Security validation shall complete successfully.
21. Duplicate moderation request validation shall succeed.
22. Escalation rule validation shall succeed.
23. Review ownership metadata validation shall succeed.
24. Moderator comments shall comply with configured limits.
25. Evidence reference validation shall succeed where applicable.
26. Review lifecycle validation shall succeed.
27. Concurrency validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.24.8 Main Flow

1. A review enters the moderation workflow.
2. The Content Moderation Service receives the moderation request.
3. The system retrieves the review details.
4. The system validates the Review ID.
5. The system validates the moderator's authentication.
6. The system verifies the moderator's authorization.
7. The system retrieves the current moderation status.
8. The system retrieves any previous moderation history.
9. The system retrieves customer reports associated with the review where applicable.
10. The system retrieves AI-generated moderation recommendations where enabled.
11. The system retrieves fraud detection indicators.
12. The moderator reviews the submitted review content.
13. The moderator reviews supporting evidence where applicable.
14. The moderator evaluates the review against community guidelines.
15. The moderator evaluates the review against platform policies.
16. The moderator selects the appropriate moderation action.
17. The moderator optionally records moderation comments.
18. The system validates the selected moderation action.
19. The system updates the moderation status.
20. The system updates the review visibility according to the moderation decision.
21. The system records the moderation reason.
22. The system stores moderator comments.
23. The system updates review lifecycle information.
24. The system synchronizes search indexes.
25. The system synchronizes cached review information.
26. The system generates customer notifications where applicable.
27. The system records moderation analytics.
28. The system updates operational metrics.
29. The system records the moderation activity in the audit log.
30. The system commits the transaction.
31. The system prepares the moderation response.
32. The updated moderation status becomes available to dependent services.
33. The moderation queue is updated.
34. Background monitoring validates successful completion.
35. The review moderation process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.24.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Review Already Moderated**

1. The selected review has already received a final moderation decision.
2. The system informs the moderator.
3. No duplicate moderation action is performed.

---

**AF-2: Moderator Authentication Failure**

1. Moderator authentication fails.
2. The moderation request is rejected.
3. The moderator is prompted to authenticate again.

---

**AF-3: Insufficient Permissions**

1. The authenticated user lacks moderation privileges.
2. The request is denied.
3. An authorization error is returned.

---

**AF-4: AI Moderation Service Unavailable**

1. AI moderation recommendations cannot be generated.
2. Manual moderation continues.
3. The moderation process completes successfully.

---

**AF-5: Escalation Required**

1. The moderator determines that additional review is required.
2. The review is escalated to a senior moderator.
3. The escalation status is recorded.

---

**AF-6: Customer Notification Failure**

1. Notification delivery fails.
2. The moderation decision remains successful.
3. Notification delivery is retried asynchronously.

---

**AF-7: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while updating moderation records.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-8: Concurrent Moderation Attempt**

1. Multiple moderators attempt to moderate the same review.
2. The system applies concurrency controls.
3. Only one moderation decision is committed.

---

**AF-9: Fraud Detection Alert**

1. Fraud Detection Service identifies suspicious review activity.
2. The review is flagged for additional investigation.
3. Administrative alerts are generated.

---

**AF-10: Network Failure**

1. Network connectivity is interrupted during moderation.
2. The request is retried or terminated according to platform policy.
3. The moderator receives an appropriate notification.

#### 3.10.24.10 Postconditions

1. The moderation decision shall be recorded successfully.
2. Review visibility shall be updated according to the moderation decision.
3. Moderation history shall be updated.
4. Search indexes shall be synchronized.
5. Cached review information shall be synchronized.
6. Customer notifications shall be generated where applicable.
7. Analytics events shall be generated.
8. Operational metrics shall be updated.
9. Audit records shall be created.
10. Review lifecycle status shall remain synchronized across dependent services.

|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Review moderated successfully. |
| Review ID | Moderated review identifier. |
| Moderation Action | Approved, Rejected, Hidden, Restored, Removed, or Escalated. |
| Moderation Status | Updated moderation status. |

| Moderation Timestamp | Time when moderation was completed. |

6. Moderation records follow configured retention policies.

### 3.10.25 Detect Fraudulent Review

#### 3.10.25.1 Description

The **Detect Fraudulent Review** use case enables the platform to automatically identify, evaluate, and flag suspicious reviews that may violate platform integrity, authenticity, or community guidelines. Fraud detection uses rule-based validation, behavioral analysis, AI/ML models, device fingerprinting, IP analysis, account activity, review patterns, order verification, and anomaly detection to identify fake, manipulated, incentivized, spam, or coordinated reviews. Reviews identified as suspicious are automatically flagged for moderation, restricted from public visibility where applicable, and routed to the Content Moderation Service for further investigation.

#### 3.10.25.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Fraud Detection Service

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- AI Fraud Detection Engine
- Content Moderation Service
- Authentication Service
- Customer Management Service
- Order Management Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.25.3 Preconditions

1. The submitted review must exist.
2. The associated customer account must exist.
3. The reviewed entity must exist.
4. The associated order must exist where applicable.
5. Fraud Detection Service must be operational.
6. AI Fraud Detection Engine must be operational where enabled.
7. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
8. Authentication Service must be operational.
9. Customer Management Service must be operational.
10. Order Management Service must be operational.
11. Database connectivity must be available.
12. Fraud detection rules must be configured.
13. Behavioral analysis rules must be configured.
14. Device fingerprint information must be available where applicable.
15. Analytics Service must be operational.
16. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
17. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.25.4 Trigger

A customer submits, edits, or updates a review, or the platform performs a scheduled fraud detection analysis.

#### 3.10.25.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Submitted review identifier. |
| Customer ID | UUID | Yes | Review author identifier. |
| Order ID | UUID | No | Associated completed order identifier. |
| Entity Type | Enum | Yes | Restaurant, Food Item, or Delivery Partner. |
| Entity ID | UUID | Yes | Reviewed entity identifier. |
| Review Content | Text | Yes | Review text submitted by the customer. |
| Rating Value | Decimal | Yes | Rating submitted with the review. |
| Device Fingerprint | String | System Generated | Device identification value. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Review Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time of review submission. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |

#### 3.10.25.6 Business Rules

1. Every submitted review shall undergo fraud detection analysis.
2. Fraud detection shall support rule-based validation.
3. AI-based fraud detection may be enabled.
4. Only eligible completed orders shall be considered valid where applicable.
5. Duplicate review patterns shall be analyzed.
6. Duplicate account behavior shall be analyzed.
7. Device fingerprint analysis shall be performed where available.
8. IP reputation analysis shall be supported.
9. Behavioral anomaly detection shall be supported.
10. Rating manipulation patterns shall be detected.
11. Coordinated review campaigns shall be detected where possible.
12. Spam detection rules shall be enforced.
13. Fake review indicators shall be evaluated.
14. High-risk reviews shall be automatically flagged.
15. Fraud scores shall be generated for each analyzed review.
16. Fraud thresholds shall be centrally configurable.
17. High-risk reviews may be hidden according to platform policy.
18. Fraud detection shall not permanently remove reviews automatically unless permitted by policy.
19. Suspicious reviews shall enter the moderation workflow.
20. Customer accounts may be flagged for repeated fraudulent behavior.
21. Operational metrics shall be updated.
22. Analytics events shall be generated.
23. Audit records shall be generated.
24. Fraud detection shall maintain transactional consistency.
25. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
26. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
27. Historical fraud analysis shall support trend detection.
28. Fraud rules shall support continuous improvement.
29. Administrative overrides shall be audited.
30. Detection models shall support retraining where applicable.
31. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
32. Fraud detection shall support horizontal scalability.
33. Detection results shall remain synchronized across dependent services.
34. Fraud detection shall comply with applicable legal and privacy regulations.
35. All fraud detection operations shall comply with governance, compliance, security, privacy, moderation, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.25.7 Validations

1. Review ID shall exist.
2. Customer ID shall exist.
3. Customer account shall be active.
4. Entity Type shall contain a supported value.
5. Entity ID shall exist.
6. Rating value shall be within supported limits.
7. Review content validation shall succeed.
8. Order ownership validation shall succeed where applicable.
9. Device fingerprint validation shall succeed where available.
10. IP address validation shall succeed.
11. Fraud rule validation shall succeed.
12. AI fraud model validation shall succeed where enabled.
13. Behavioral analysis validation shall succeed.
14. Spam detection validation shall succeed.
15. Duplicate review validation shall succeed.
16. Duplicate account validation shall succeed.
17. Database validation shall succeed.
18. Moderation workflow validation shall succeed.
19. Analytics validation shall succeed.
20. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
21. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
22. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
23. Security validation shall complete successfully.
24. Concurrency validation shall succeed.
25. Fraud score calculation validation shall succeed.
26. Risk threshold validation shall succeed.
27. Transaction validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.25.8 Main Flow

1. A customer submits or updates a review.
2. The Review & Rating Service publishes a fraud detection event.
3. The Fraud Detection Service receives the event.
4. The system retrieves the review details.
5. The system validates the Review ID.
6. The system retrieves the customer profile.
7. The system retrieves the customer's review history.
8. The system retrieves the associated order information where applicable.
9. The system verifies review eligibility.
10. The system collects device fingerprint information where available.
11. The system collects IP address information.
12. The system performs duplicate review analysis.
13. The system performs duplicate account analysis.
14. The system analyzes customer behavioral patterns.
15. The system analyzes rating distribution patterns.
16. The system analyzes review submission frequency.
17. The system evaluates review content for spam indicators.
18. The system evaluates review content using AI fraud detection where enabled.
19. The system evaluates fraud detection rules.
20. The system calculates the fraud risk score.
21. The system compares the calculated score against configured thresholds.
22. The system determines the fraud risk classification.
23. The system flags suspicious reviews where applicable.
24. The system updates the review fraud status.
25. The system submits high-risk reviews to the Content Moderation Service.
26. The system updates customer fraud indicators where applicable.
27. The system records fraud detection analytics.
28. The system updates operational metrics.
29. The system records fraud detection activity in the audit log.
30. The system commits the transaction.
31. The system prepares the fraud detection response.
32. The fraud status becomes available to dependent services.
33. Background monitoring validates successful fraud analysis.
34. Fraud detection dashboards receive updated metrics.
35. The fraudulent review detection process completes successfully.

---

### 3.10.26 Manage Review Visibility

#### 3.10.26.1 Description

The **Manage Review Visibility** use case enables the platform to control whether customer reviews are visible to end users based on moderation decisions, fraud detection results, platform policies, legal requirements, business rules, or administrative actions. The system automatically or manually changes the visibility status of reviews to ensure that only appropriate, authentic, and policy-compliant reviews are displayed. Visibility changes are propagated across all dependent services, including search, review summaries, rating calculations, recommendation engines, and customer-facing applications.

#### 3.10.26.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Moderator
- Content Moderation Service

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Authentication Service
- Customer Management Service
- Search Service
- Analytics Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.26.3 Preconditions

1. The review must exist.
2. The review must be accessible.
3. The moderator must be authenticated for manual visibility changes.
4. The moderator must possess review management privileges.
5. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
6. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
7. Fraud Detection Service must be operational.
8. Authentication Service must be operational.
9. Database connectivity must be available.
10. Visibility management policies must be configured.
11. Community guidelines must be configured.
12. Search Service must be operational.
13. Notification Service must be operational.
14. Analytics Service must be operational.
15. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
16. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
17. Review lifecycle states must be available.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.26.4 Trigger

A moderation decision, fraud detection result, administrative action, legal request, or platform policy change requires the review visibility to be updated.

| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Review whose visibility is being managed. |
| Moderator ID | UUID | Conditional | Required for manual visibility updates. |
| Visibility Status | Enum | Yes | Visible, Hidden, Removed, Restored, Restricted. |
| Visibility Reason | Enum | Yes | Moderation, Fraud Detection, Legal Request, Policy Violation, Administrative Action, or Other. |
| Moderator Comments | String | No | Internal visibility management notes. |
| Effective Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time when visibility change becomes effective. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | Moderator session identifier. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Web, Mobile, or Admin Portal. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |
| Previous Visibility Status | Enum | System Generated | Visibility status before the update. |

#### 3.10.26.6 Business Rules

1. Only authorized moderators shall manually manage review visibility.
2. Automated services may update visibility according to configured policies.
3. Every review shall maintain a single active visibility status.
4. Visibility status shall follow the review lifecycle.
5. Hidden reviews shall not be displayed to customers.
6. Removed reviews shall be excluded from customer-facing applications.
7. Restored reviews shall become visible after approval.
8. Fraudulent reviews may be automatically hidden.
9. Legal removal requests shall receive the highest priority.
10. Administrative overrides shall be supported.
11. Visibility changes shall update search indexes.
12. Visibility changes shall update review summaries.
13. Visibility changes shall update rating calculations.
14. Visibility changes shall update recommendation engines where applicable.
15. Customer notifications shall be generated where applicable.
16. Community guidelines shall be enforced.
17. Platform moderation policies shall be enforced.
18. Operational metrics shall be updated.
19. Analytics events shall be generated.
20. Audit records shall be generated.
21. Visibility history shall be maintained.
22. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
23. Unauthorized visibility changes shall be prevented.
24. Visibility updates shall maintain transactional consistency.
25. Cached review data shall be synchronized.
26. Distributed services shall receive visibility updates.
27. Historical visibility records shall follow retention policies.
28. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
29. Visibility management shall support horizontal scalability.
30. Duplicate visibility updates shall be prevented.
31. Visibility decisions shall support rollback where permitted.
32. Review lifecycle states shall remain synchronized across services.
33. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
34. Visibility management shall comply with applicable legal and regulatory requirements.
35. All visibility management operations shall comply with governance, moderation, security, privacy, compliance, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.26.7 Validations

1. Review ID shall exist.
2. Review shall be eligible for visibility modification.
3. Moderator authentication shall succeed where applicable.
4. Moderator authorization shall succeed where applicable.
5. Visibility Status shall contain a supported value.
6. Visibility Reason shall contain a supported value.
7. Review lifecycle validation shall succeed.
8. Previous visibility status validation shall succeed.
9. Duplicate visibility update validation shall succeed.
10. Community guideline validation shall succeed.
11. Platform policy validation shall succeed.
12. Fraud detection validation shall succeed where applicable.
13. Legal request validation shall succeed where applicable.
14. Search Service validation shall succeed.
15. Notification Service validation shall succeed.
16. Analytics validation shall succeed.
17. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
18. Database validation shall succeed.
19. Transaction validation shall succeed.
20. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
21. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
22. Security validation shall complete successfully.
23. Concurrency validation shall succeed.
24. Moderator comments shall comply with configured limits where provided.
25. Visibility history validation shall succeed.
26. Cache synchronization validation shall succeed.
27. Distributed service synchronization validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.26.8 Main Flow

1. A review visibility update request is initiated.
2. The Review & Rating Service receives the visibility management request.
3. The system retrieves the review details.
4. The system validates the Review ID.
5. The system determines whether the request is manual or automated.
6. The system validates moderator authentication where applicable.
7. The system verifies moderator authorization where applicable.
8. The system retrieves the current visibility status.
9. The system retrieves the review lifecycle status.
10. The system retrieves moderation history.
11. The system retrieves fraud detection results where applicable.
12. The system validates the requested visibility action.
13. The system validates the visibility reason.
14. The system evaluates applicable platform policies.
15. The system evaluates community guidelines.
16. The system verifies legal restrictions where applicable.
17. The system updates the review visibility status.
18. The system records the visibility reason.
19. The system records moderator comments where provided.
20. The system updates the review lifecycle information.
21. The system updates search indexes.
22. The system refreshes cached review data.
23. The system recalculates review summaries where required.
24. The system recalculates rating aggregates where required.
25. The system synchronizes recommendation services where applicable.
26. The system generates customer notifications where applicable.
27. The system records analytics events.
28. The system updates operational metrics.
29. The system records the visibility change in the audit log.
30. The system commits the transaction.
31. The system prepares the response payload.
32. Updated visibility information becomes available across dependent services.
33. Background synchronization validates successful propagation.
34. Visibility history is updated.
35. The review visibility management process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.26.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Review Not Found**

1. The specified review does not exist.
2. The request is rejected.
3. An appropriate error response is returned.

---

**AF-2: Unauthorized Moderator**

1. The moderator lacks the required permissions.
2. The request is denied.
3. An authorization error is returned.

---

**AF-3: Invalid Visibility Status**

1. An unsupported visibility status is provided.
2. Validation fails.
3. The request is rejected.

---

**AF-4: Legal Hold Applied**

1. The review is under a legal hold.
2. Visibility changes are restricted according to legal policy.
3. The request is logged for compliance.

---

**AF-5: Fraud Detection Trigger**

1. Fraud Detection Service identifies suspicious activity.
2. The review is automatically hidden according to platform policy.
3. The review is submitted for moderation.

---

**AF-6: Search Index Update Failure**

1. Search index synchronization fails.
2. Visibility status is successfully updated.
3. Search synchronization is queued for retry.

---

**AF-7: Notification Delivery Failure**

1. Customer notification delivery fails.
2. Visibility update remains successful.
3. Notification delivery is retried asynchronously.

---

**AF-8: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while updating visibility.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-9: Concurrent Visibility Update**

1. Multiple visibility update requests occur simultaneously.
2. Concurrency controls are applied.
3. Only one update is committed successfully.

---

**AF-10: Network Failure**

1. Network connectivity is interrupted during processing.
2. Retry policies are applied where supported.
3. Processing resumes after connectivity is restored.

#### 3.10.26.10 Postconditions

1. Review visibility status shall be updated successfully.
2. Visibility history shall be recorded.
3. Review lifecycle information shall be synchronized.
4. Search indexes shall be updated.
5. Cached review data shall be refreshed.
6. Rating calculations shall be updated where applicable.
7. Analytics events shall be generated.
8. Operational metrics shall be updated.
9. Audit records shall be created.
10. Dependent services shall reflect the latest visibility status.

#### 3.10.26.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Review visibility updated successfully. |
| Review ID | Updated review identifier. |
| Previous Visibility Status | Visibility status before the update. |
| Current Visibility Status | Updated visibility status. |
| Visibility Reason | Reason for the visibility update. |
| Updated Timestamp | Time when the visibility update was completed. |

#### 3.10.26.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid visibility update request. |
| 401 | Authentication failed. |
| 403 | Unauthorized visibility update request. |
| 404 | Review not found. |
| 409 | Concurrent visibility update conflict. |
| 422 | Visibility validation failed. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Visibility management service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.26.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authorized users or services shall update review visibility.
2. Every visibility change shall be recorded.
3. Search indexes shall reflect the latest visibility status.
4. Hidden reviews shall not appear in customer-facing applications.
5. Rating summaries shall remain accurate after visibility changes.
6. Customer notifications shall be generated where applicable.
7. Analytics events shall be generated successfully.
8. Audit records shall be generated successfully.
9. Visibility history shall remain available for future reference.
10. Visibility changes shall remain synchronized across all dependent services.

#### 3.10.26.14 Security Considerations

1. Moderator authentication shall be mandatory for manual updates.
2. Authorization shall be verified before every visibility modification.
3. Unauthorized visibility updates shall be prevented.
4. Visibility history shall be protected against unauthorized modification.
5. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
6. Service-to-service communication shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
7. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Administrative overrides shall require appropriate authorization.
10. Visibility management operations shall comply with applicable security, privacy, and legal regulations.

#### 3.10.26.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information:

- Audit Log ID
- Review ID

- Previous Visibility Status

- Visibility Reason

- Fraud Detection Reference ID
- Session ID
- Device Type

- Processing Duration
- Service Instance ID
- Update Source (Manual/Automated)

#### 3.10.26.16 Assumptions

1. Review visibility policies are centrally managed.
2. Moderation decisions are available before visibility updates where applicable.
3. Fraud detection services remain operational.
4. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
5. Search index synchronization is supported.
6. Visibility history follows configured retention policies.

- Authentication Service
- Customer Management Service
- Search Service
- Cache Management Service

## Restaurant Response Management

### 3.10.27 Reply to Customer Review

#### 3.10.27.1 Description

The **Reply to Customer Review** use case enables authorized restaurant owners, restaurant managers, or platform-authorized representatives to respond to customer reviews. Review replies help businesses acknowledge customer feedback, address complaints, answer questions, provide clarifications, apologize for poor experiences, and improve customer engagement. Replies become publicly visible beneath the original review after successful validation and moderation according to platform policies. All reply activities are recorded for auditing, analytics, compliance, and future reference.

#### 3.10.27.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Restaurant Owner
- Restaurant Manager

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Authentication Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Customer Management Service

#### 3.10.27.3 Preconditions

1. The review must exist.
2. The review must be publicly visible.
3. The restaurant associated with the review must exist.
4. The restaurant owner or manager must be authenticated.
5. The actor must have permission to manage the restaurant.
6. Restaurant Management Service must be operational.
7. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
8. Authentication Service must be operational.
9. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
10. Notification Service must be operational.
11. Database connectivity must be available.
12. Platform reply policies must be configured.
13. Community guidelines must be configured.
14. Analytics Service must be operational.
15. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
16. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
17. The review must not be permanently removed.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.27.4 Trigger

The authorized restaurant owner or manager selects the **Reply** option for a customer review.

#### 3.10.27.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Review being replied to. |
| Restaurant ID | UUID | Yes | Restaurant associated with the review. |
| Responder ID | UUID | Yes | Restaurant owner or manager identifier. |
| Reply Content | Text | Yes | Reply message submitted by the restaurant representative. |
| Reply Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time when the reply is submitted. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated session identifier. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Web, Android, iOS, or Admin Portal. |
| IP Address | String | System Generated | Client IP address. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |
| Reply Status | Enum | System Generated | Pending Moderation, Published, Rejected, or Hidden. |

#### 3.10.27.6 Business Rules

1. Only authorized restaurant owners or managers shall reply to reviews.
2. Replies shall only be allowed for reviews belonging to the actor's restaurant.
3. Every review may receive replies according to configured platform policies.
4. Reply content shall comply with community guidelines.
5. Offensive or abusive replies shall be rejected or moderated.
6. Reply length shall comply with configured limits.
7. Reply moderation may be automatic or manual.
8. Published replies shall appear beneath the associated review.
9. Customers may be notified when a reply is published.
10. Reply edits shall follow platform policies.
11. Deleted replies shall follow retention policies.
12. Reply history shall be maintained.
13. Duplicate replies shall be prevented where applicable.
14. Reply timestamps shall be system generated.
15. Reply visibility shall follow moderation decisions.
16. Platform moderation policies shall be enforced.
17. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
18. Analytics events shall be generated.
19. Operational metrics shall be updated.
20. Audit records shall be generated.
21. Unauthorized reply attempts shall be rejected.
22. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
23. Reply processing shall maintain transactional consistency.
24. Notification events shall be generated where applicable.
25. Cached review data shall be synchronized.
26. Search indexes shall be updated where applicable.
27. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
28. Reply workflows shall support moderation queues.
29. Administrative overrides shall be audited.
30. Historical reply records shall follow configured retention policies.
31. Reply operations shall support horizontal scalability.
32. Review lifecycle states shall remain synchronized across dependent services.
33. Reply processing shall comply with applicable legal and regulatory requirements.
34. Business representatives shall only access reviews belonging to managed restaurants.
35. All reply operations shall comply with governance, moderation, security, privacy, compliance, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.27.7 Validations

1. Review ID shall exist.
2. Restaurant ID shall exist.
3. Responder ID shall exist.
4. Responder authentication shall succeed.
5. Responder authorization shall succeed.
6. Review ownership validation shall succeed.
7. Review shall be eligible for replies.
8. Reply Content shall not be empty.
9. Reply Content length shall comply with configured limits.
10. Reply Content shall comply with community guidelines.
11. Profanity validation shall succeed.
12. Spam detection validation shall succeed.
13. Duplicate reply validation shall succeed.
14. Platform policy validation shall succeed.
15. Moderation workflow validation shall succeed.
16. Notification Service validation shall succeed.
17. Analytics validation shall succeed.
18. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
19. Database validation shall succeed.
20. Transaction validation shall succeed.
21. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
22. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
23. Security validation shall complete successfully.
24. Concurrency validation shall succeed.
25. Restaurant ownership mapping shall be validated.
26. Review status validation shall succeed.
27. Reply status validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.27.8 Main Flow

1. The restaurant owner or manager views a customer review.
2. The actor selects the **Reply** option.
3. The system displays the reply input interface.
4. The actor enters the reply content.
5. The actor submits the reply.
6. The Review & Rating Service receives the reply request.
7. The system validates the responder's authentication.
8. The system validates the Responder ID.
9. The system verifies the responder's authorization for the restaurant.
10. The system validates the Review ID.
11. The system verifies that the review belongs to the responder's restaurant.
12. The system validates the reply content.
13. The system validates the reply length.
14. The system performs profanity detection.
15. The system performs spam detection.
16. The system evaluates community guideline compliance.
17. The system determines whether moderation is required.
18. The system assigns the initial reply status.
19. The system creates the reply record.
20. The system associates the reply with the original review.
21. The system records the reply timestamp.
22. The system updates the review conversation.
23. The system updates search indexes where applicable.
24. The system refreshes cached review data.
25. The system generates customer notifications where applicable.
26. The system records reply analytics.
27. The system updates operational metrics.
28. The system records the reply activity in the audit log.
29. The system commits the transaction.
30. The system prepares the response payload.
31. The updated reply becomes available according to its moderation status.
32. Dependent services receive synchronization updates.
33. Background monitoring validates successful processing.
34. Reply history is updated.
35. The customer review reply process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.27.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Authentication Failure**

1. The responder authentication fails.
2. The request is rejected.
3. The responder is prompted to authenticate again.

---

**AF-2: Unauthorized Restaurant Access**

1. The responder is not authorized to manage the restaurant.
2. The reply request is denied.
3. An authorization error is returned.

---

**AF-3: Review Not Found**

1. The specified review does not exist.
2. The request is rejected.
3. An appropriate error response is returned.

---

**AF-4: Community Guideline Violation**

1. The reply violates community guidelines.
2. The reply is rejected or routed for moderation.
3. The responder receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-5: Duplicate Reply Detected**

1. The submitted reply is identified as a duplicate.
2. The duplicate submission is rejected according to platform policy.
3. The responder is informed of the duplicate.

---

**AF-6: Moderation Required**

1. The reply requires manual moderation.
2. The reply status is set to **Pending Moderation**.
3. The reply becomes visible only after approval.

---

**AF-7: Notification Delivery Failure**

1. Customer notification delivery fails.
2. Reply creation remains successful.
3. Notification delivery is retried asynchronously.

---

**AF-8: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while storing the reply.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-9: Concurrent Reply Update**

1. Multiple reply operations occur simultaneously.
2. Concurrency controls are applied.
3. Data consistency is maintained.

---

**AF-10: Network Failure**

1. Network connectivity is interrupted during reply submission.
2. Retry policies are applied where supported.
3. Processing resumes after connectivity is restored.

#### 3.10.27.10 Postconditions

1. The reply shall be created successfully.
2. The reply shall be associated with the corresponding review.
3. Reply status shall be recorded.
4. Reply history shall be updated.
5. Search indexes shall be updated where applicable.
6. Cached review data shall be refreshed.
7. Customer notifications shall be generated where applicable.
8. Analytics events shall be generated.
9. Operational metrics shall be updated.
10. Audit records shall be created.

#### 3.10.27.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Reply submitted successfully. |
| Reply ID | Unique reply identifier. |
| Review ID | Associated review identifier. |
| Reply Status | Published, Pending Moderation, or Hidden. |
| Restaurant ID | Restaurant associated with the reply. |
| Reply Timestamp | Time when the reply was submitted. |

#### 3.10.27.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid reply request. |
| 401 | Authentication failed. |
| 403 | Unauthorized restaurant representative. |
| 404 | Review not found. |
| 409 | Duplicate or conflicting reply detected. |
| 422 | Reply validation failed. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Review service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.27.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authorized restaurant representatives shall reply to reviews.
2. Replies shall be linked to the correct review.
3. Reply content shall comply with community guidelines.
4. Replies requiring moderation shall follow the moderation workflow.
5. Customers shall receive notifications where applicable.
6. Reply history shall be maintained.
7. Analytics events shall be generated successfully.
8. Audit records shall be generated successfully.
9. Reply visibility shall follow moderation decisions.
10. Reply information shall remain synchronized across dependent services.

#### 3.10.27.14 Security Considerations

2. Authorization shall verify restaurant ownership or management privileges.
3. Reply content shall undergo moderation and security validation.
4. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
5. Unauthorized reply attempts shall be rejected.
6. Service-to-service communication shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
7. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Administrative actions shall require appropriate authorization.
10. Reply operations shall comply with applicable security, privacy, and legal regulations.

4. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
5. Reply history follows configured retention policies.
6. Notification preferences are available where applicable.

### 3.10.28 Edit Review Reply

#### 3.10.28.1 Description

The **Edit Review Reply** use case enables authorized restaurant owners or restaurant managers to modify an existing reply previously posted in response to a customer review. Editing allows businesses to correct errors, update information, improve communication, or provide revised responses while maintaining reply history for audit and compliance purposes. Edited replies are validated, optionally re-moderated according to platform policies, and synchronized across all customer-facing applications and dependent services.

#### 3.10.28.2 Actors

- Restaurant Owner
- Restaurant Manager

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service

2. The associated review must exist.
3. The associated restaurant must exist.

7. Restaurant Management Service must be operational.

10. Notification Service must be operational.
11. Database connectivity must be available.

13. Community guidelines must be configured.

17. The reply must not be permanently deleted.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

---

**AF-3: Reply Not Found**

1. The specified reply does not exist.
2. The edit request is rejected.
3. An appropriate error response is returned.

---

**AF-4: Community Guideline Violation**

1. The updated reply violates community guidelines.
2. The reply is rejected or routed for moderation.
3. The responder receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-5: Re-Moderation Required**

1. Platform policy requires moderation after editing.
2. The reply status is updated to **Pending Moderation**.
3. The edited reply becomes visible only after approval.

---

**AF-6: Duplicate Reply Content**

1. The updated reply matches existing content according to platform policy.
2. The update is rejected.
3. The responder is informed accordingly.

---

**AF-7: Notification Delivery Failure**

1. Customer notification delivery fails.
2. Reply editing remains successful.
3. Notification delivery is retried asynchronously.

---

**AF-8: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while updating the reply.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-9: Concurrent Edit Request**

1. Multiple edit requests are received for the same reply.
2. Concurrency controls are applied.
3. Only one edit request is committed successfully.

---

**AF-10: Network Failure**

1. Network connectivity is interrupted during reply editing.
2. Retry policies are applied where supported.
3. Processing resumes after connectivity is restored.

#### 3.10.28.10 Postconditions

1. The reply shall be updated successfully.
2. Reply history shall contain the previous version.
3. Reply status shall be updated according to platform policy.
4. Search indexes shall be synchronized where applicable.
5. Cached review data shall be refreshed.
6. Customer notifications shall be generated where applicable.
7. Analytics events shall be generated.
8. Operational metrics shall be updated.
9. Audit records shall be created.
10. Dependent services shall reflect the updated reply information.

#### 3.10.28.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Reply updated successfully. |
| Reply ID | Updated reply identifier. |
| Review ID | Associated review identifier. |
| Reply Status | Published, Pending Moderation, or Hidden. |
| Updated Timestamp | Time when the reply was edited. |
| Version Number | Latest reply version. |

#### 3.10.28.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid reply edit request. |
| 401 | Authentication failed. |
| 403 | Unauthorized restaurant representative. |
| 404 | Reply not found. |
| 409 | Concurrent edit conflict detected. |
| 422 | Reply validation failed. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Review service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.28.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authorized restaurant representatives shall edit replies.
2. Reply ownership shall always be verified.
3. Updated reply content shall comply with community guidelines.
4. Previous reply versions shall be preserved.
5. Re-moderation shall occur according to platform policy.
6. Customer notifications shall be generated where applicable.
7. Analytics events shall be generated successfully.
8. Audit records shall be generated successfully.
9. Reply information shall remain synchronized across dependent services.
10. Edited replies shall follow configured visibility policies.

#### 3.10.28.14 Security Considerations

1. Authentication shall be mandatory before editing replies.
2. Authorization shall verify restaurant ownership or management privileges.
3. Previous reply versions shall be protected from unauthorized modification.
4. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
5. Unauthorized edit attempts shall be rejected.
6. Service-to-service communication shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
7. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Administrative overrides shall require appropriate authorization.
10. Reply editing operations shall comply with applicable security, privacy, and legal regulations.

2. Community guidelines are centrally managed.
3. Moderation workflows are operational.
4. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
5. Reply version history follows configured retention policies.
6. Notification preferences are available where applicable.

### 3.10.29 Delete Review Reply

#### 3.10.29.1 Description

The **Delete Review Reply** use case enables authorized restaurant owners, restaurant managers, or platform-authorized administrators to permanently or logically remove an existing reply posted in response to a customer review. Reply deletion may occur due to business requirements, policy violations, legal requests, duplicate replies, incorrect information, or administrative actions. Deleted replies are removed from customer-facing applications according to platform policies while preserving audit history, deletion records, and compliance information.

#### 3.10.29.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Restaurant Owner
- Restaurant Manager

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Authentication Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Notification Service
- Analytics Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Customer Management Service

#### 3.10.29.3 Preconditions

1. The review reply must exist.
2. The associated review must exist.
3. The associated restaurant must exist.
4. The restaurant owner or manager must be authenticated.
5. The actor must have permission to manage the restaurant.
6. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
7. Restaurant Management Service must be operational.
8. Authentication Service must be operational.
9. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
10. Notification Service must be operational.
11. Database connectivity must be available.
12. Platform reply deletion policies must be configured.
13. Audit retention policies must be configured.
14. Analytics Service must be operational.
15. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
16. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
17. The reply must not already be deleted.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.29.4 Trigger

The authorized restaurant owner or manager selects the **Delete Reply** option for an existing review reply.

#### 3.10.29.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Reply ID | UUID | Yes | Existing reply identifier. |
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Associated review identifier. |
| Restaurant ID | UUID | Yes | Restaurant associated with the reply. |
| Responder ID | UUID | Yes | Restaurant owner or manager identifier. |
| Deletion Reason | Enum | Yes | Duplicate, Incorrect Information, Policy Violation, Legal Request, Administrative Action, or Other. |
| Additional Comments | String | No | Optional deletion remarks. |
| Delete Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time when the reply was deleted. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated session identifier. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Web, Android, iOS, or Admin Portal. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |

#### 3.10.29.6 Business Rules

1. Only authorized restaurant owners or managers shall delete review replies.
2. Replies shall only be deleted by authorized representatives of the associated restaurant.
3. Reply deletion shall follow platform policies.
4. Deletion reason shall be captured for every deletion request.
5. Logical deletion shall be preferred unless permanent deletion is required by policy.
6. Permanently deleted replies shall follow legal retention policies.
7. Deleted replies shall no longer be visible to customers.
8. Reply deletion history shall be maintained.
9. Original reply records shall remain available for audit purposes where applicable.
10. Customer notifications may be generated according to platform policy.
11. Deleted replies shall be excluded from customer-facing applications.
12. Search indexes shall be updated after deletion.
13. Cached review data shall be synchronized.
14. Platform moderation policies shall be enforced.
15. Administrative overrides shall be supported.
16. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
17. Analytics events shall be generated.
18. Operational metrics shall be updated.
19. Audit records shall be generated.
20. Unauthorized deletion attempts shall be rejected.
21. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
22. Reply deletion shall maintain transactional consistency.
23. Notification events shall be generated where applicable.
24. Historical reply versions shall follow retention policies.
25. Duplicate deletion requests shall be prevented.
26. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
27. Reply deletion shall support horizontal scalability.
28. Review lifecycle states shall remain synchronized across dependent services.
29. Concurrency controls shall prevent conflicting delete operations.
30. Deleted replies shall be recoverable only where permitted by platform policy.
31. Compliance requirements shall be enforced.
32. Legal deletion requests shall receive high priority.
33. Reply ownership shall always be validated.
34. Reply deletion shall comply with applicable legal and regulatory requirements.
35. All reply deletion operations shall comply with governance, moderation, security, privacy, compliance, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.29.7 Validations

1. Reply ID shall exist.
2. Review ID shall exist.
3. Restaurant ID shall exist.
4. Responder ID shall exist.
5. Responder authentication shall succeed.
6. Responder authorization shall succeed.
7. Reply ownership validation shall succeed.
8. Reply status shall allow deletion.
9. Deletion Reason shall contain a supported value.
10. Additional Comments shall comply with configured limits where provided.
11. Platform policy validation shall succeed.
12. Moderation policy validation shall succeed.
13. Notification Service validation shall succeed.
14. Analytics validation shall succeed.
15. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
16. Database validation shall succeed.
17. Transaction validation shall succeed.
18. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
19. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
20. Security validation shall complete successfully.
21. Concurrency validation shall succeed.
22. Duplicate deletion request validation shall succeed.
23. Search index synchronization validation shall succeed.
24. Cache synchronization validation shall succeed.
25. Retention policy validation shall succeed.
26. Legal compliance validation shall succeed where applicable.
27. Deletion permission validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.29.8 Main Flow

1. The restaurant owner or manager views an existing review reply.
2. The actor selects the **Delete Reply** option.
3. The system displays the deletion confirmation dialog.
4. The actor selects a deletion reason.
5. The actor optionally enters additional comments.
6. The actor confirms the deletion request.
7. The Review & Rating Service receives the deletion request.
8. The system validates the responder's authentication.
9. The system validates the Responder ID.
10. The system verifies the responder's authorization for the restaurant.
11. The system validates the Reply ID.
12. The system validates the Review ID.
13. The system verifies reply ownership.
14. The system verifies that the reply is eligible for deletion.
15. The system validates the selected deletion reason.
16. The system evaluates platform deletion policies.
17. The system evaluates legal retention requirements where applicable.
18. The system determines whether logical or permanent deletion is required.
19. The system updates the reply status to the appropriate deletion state.
20. The system records the deletion reason.
21. The system stores additional comments where provided.
22. The system records the deletion timestamp.
23. The system preserves audit history.
24. The system updates search indexes.
25. The system refreshes cached review data.
26. The system generates customer notifications where applicable.
27. The system records deletion analytics.
28. The system updates operational metrics.
29. The system records the deletion activity in the audit log.
30. The system commits the transaction.
31. The system prepares the response payload.
32. The deleted reply is removed from customer-facing applications according to platform policy.
33. Dependent services synchronize the latest reply status.
34. Background monitoring validates successful deletion.
35. The review reply deletion process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.29.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Authentication Failure**

1. The responder authentication fails.
2. The deletion request is rejected.
3. The responder is prompted to authenticate again.

---

**AF-2: Unauthorized Restaurant Access**

1. The responder is not authorized to delete the reply.
2. The request is denied.
3. An authorization error is returned.

---

**AF-3: Reply Not Found**

1. The specified reply does not exist.
2. The deletion request is rejected.
3. An appropriate error response is returned.

---

**AF-4: Reply Already Deleted**

1. The reply has already been deleted.
2. The duplicate deletion request is rejected.
3. The responder is informed accordingly.

---

**AF-5: Legal Retention Restriction**

1. The reply is protected by legal retention policies.
2. Permanent deletion is not permitted.
3. The system applies logical deletion where supported or rejects the request.

---

**AF-6: Customer Notification Failure**

1. Notification delivery fails.
2. Reply deletion remains successful.
3. Notification delivery is retried asynchronously.

---

**AF-7: Search Index Update Failure**

1. Search index synchronization fails.
2. Reply deletion remains successful.
3. Search synchronization is queued for retry.

---

**AF-8: Database Failure**

1. A database error occurs while deleting the reply.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-9: Concurrent Delete Request**

1. Multiple deletion requests are received simultaneously.
2. Concurrency controls are applied.
3. Only one deletion request is committed successfully.

---

**AF-10: Network Failure**

1. Network connectivity is interrupted during reply deletion.
2. Retry policies are applied where supported.
3. Processing resumes after connectivity is restored.

3. Reply visibility shall be updated.
4. Search indexes shall be synchronized.
5. Cached review data shall be refreshed.
6. Customer notifications shall be generated where applicable.
7. Analytics events shall be generated.
8. Operational metrics shall be updated.
9. Audit records shall be created.
10. Dependent services shall reflect the latest reply status.

| Message | Reply deleted successfully. |
