### 3.10.30 Mark Review as Featured

#### 3.10.30.1 Description

The **Mark Review as Featured** use case enables authorized platform moderators or administrators to designate high-quality customer reviews as **Featured Reviews**. Featured reviews are highlighted prominently within the customer interface to showcase authentic, informative, balanced, and helpful feedback. Featured reviews improve customer decision-making, increase platform credibility, and promote meaningful customer engagement. The feature supports manual selection, AI-assisted recommendations, and policy-based eligibility while ensuring compliance with moderation, security, and audit requirements.

#### 3.10.30.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Moderator
- Platform Administrator

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Content Moderation Service
- Authentication Service
- Analytics Service
- Recommendation Service
- Search Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.10.30.3 Preconditions

1. The review must exist.
2. The review must be publicly visible.
3. The review must have an approved moderation status.
4. The moderator or administrator must be authenticated.
5. The actor must possess review management privileges.
6. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
7. Authentication Service must be operational.
8. Content Moderation Service must be operational.
9. Database connectivity must be available.
10. Featured review policies must be configured.
11. Eligibility rules must be configured.
12. Recommendation Service must be operational where applicable.
13. Search Service must be operational.
14. Analytics Service must be operational.
15. Notification Service must be operational.
16. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
17. Platform security policies must be satisfied.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.30.4 Trigger

An authorized moderator or administrator selects the **Mark as Featured** option for an eligible customer review.

#### 3.10.30.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Review ID | UUID | Yes | Review to be marked as featured. |
| Moderator ID | UUID | Yes | Authorized moderator or administrator identifier. |
| Feature Reason | Enum | Yes | Helpful Review, Detailed Feedback, High Quality, Verified Experience, Editorial Selection, or Other. |
| Featured Priority | Integer | No | Display priority among featured reviews. |
| Feature Expiry Date | DateTime | No | Optional expiry date for featured status. |
| Moderator Comments | String | No | Internal comments regarding the featured selection. |
| Feature Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Time when the review is marked as featured. |
| Session ID | UUID | System Generated | Authenticated session identifier. |
| Device Type | Enum | System Generated | Web or Admin Portal. |
| Request Correlation ID | UUID | System Generated | End-to-end request tracking identifier. |

#### 3.10.30.6 Business Rules

1. Only authorized moderators or administrators shall mark reviews as featured.
2. Only approved and publicly visible reviews shall be eligible.
3. Featured reviews shall comply with community guidelines.
4. Reviews under investigation shall not be featured.
5. Fraudulent or suspicious reviews shall not be featured.
6. Featured review eligibility rules shall be centrally configurable.
7. AI recommendations may assist moderators in selecting featured reviews.
8. Manual moderator decisions shall override AI recommendations.
9. Every featured review shall maintain a featured status record.
10. A review may have only one active featured status.
11. Featured reviews shall be displayed prominently in customer interfaces.
12. Featured review priority shall determine display order where applicable.
13. Feature expiry shall be supported where configured.
14. Featured status may be revoked according to platform policy.
15. Search indexes shall be updated after featured status changes.
16. Recommendation services shall receive updated featured information.
17. Review summaries shall reflect featured reviews where applicable.
18. Customer notifications may be generated according to platform policy.
19. Analytics events shall be generated.
20. Operational metrics shall be updated.
21. Audit records shall be generated.
22. Unauthorized feature requests shall be rejected.
23. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
24. Featured review selection shall maintain transactional consistency.
25. Historical featured status changes shall be retained.
26. Administrative overrides shall be audited.
27. Cached review data shall be synchronized.
28. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
29. Featured review management shall support horizontal scalability.
30. Duplicate featured assignments shall be prevented.
31. Featured reviews shall remain synchronized across dependent services.
32. Personally identifiable information shall remain protected.
33. Featured review operations shall comply with applicable legal and regulatory requirements.
34. Feature removal shall follow the same authorization rules as feature assignment.
35. All featured review operations shall comply with governance, moderation, security, privacy, compliance, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.30.7 Validations

1. Review ID shall exist.
2. Moderator ID shall exist.
3. Moderator authentication shall succeed.
4. Moderator authorization shall succeed.
5. Review moderation status shall be Approved.
6. Review visibility shall be Public.
7. Review fraud status shall permit featuring.
8. Feature Reason shall contain a supported value.
9. Featured Priority shall be within configured limits where provided.
10. Feature Expiry Date shall be valid where provided.
11. Duplicate featured status validation shall succeed.
12. Eligibility rule validation shall succeed.
13. Platform policy validation shall succeed.
14. Community guideline validation shall succeed.
15. Recommendation Service validation shall succeed where applicable.
16. Search Service validation shall succeed.
17. Notification Service validation shall succeed.
18. Analytics validation shall succeed.
19. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
20. Database validation shall succeed.
21. Transaction validation shall succeed.
22. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
23. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
24. Security validation shall complete successfully.
25. Concurrency validation shall succeed.
26. Feature expiry validation shall succeed where applicable.
27. Review lifecycle validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.30.8 Main Flow

1. The moderator or administrator opens the review management portal.
2. The actor views an eligible customer review.
3. The actor selects the **Mark as Featured** option.
4. The system displays the featured review configuration screen.
5. The actor selects the feature reason.
6. The actor optionally specifies the featured priority.
7. The actor optionally specifies a feature expiry date.
8. The actor optionally enters moderator comments.
9. The actor submits the feature request.
10. The Review & Rating Service receives the request.
11. The system validates the moderator's authentication.
12. The system validates the moderator's authorization.
13. The system validates the Review ID.
14. The system verifies that the review is publicly visible.
15. The system verifies that the review has an approved moderation status.
16. The system checks fraud detection status.
17. The system evaluates featured review eligibility rules.
18. The system verifies that the review is not already featured.
19. The system validates the feature reason.
20. The system validates the featured priority where applicable.
21. The system validates the feature expiry date where applicable.
22. The system assigns the featured status.
23. The system stores the featured review metadata.
24. The system updates the review display priority.
25. The system updates search indexes.
26. The system refreshes cached review data.

29. The system records analytics events.

32. The system commits the transaction.

34. Dependent services synchronize the featured review information.
35. The review is successfully marked as featured.

#### 3.10.30.9 Alternate Flows

2. The request is rejected.

2. The request is denied.

---

2. The request is rejected.

---

---

---

3. Processing resumes after connectivity is restored.

## Audit & Compliance

### 3.10.31 Generate Review Audit Log

#### 3.10.31.1 Description

The **Generate Review Audit Log** use case enables authorized administrators, compliance officers, auditors, and platform moderators to generate comprehensive audit logs for review-related activities. The audit log provides a complete chronological record of review lifecycle events, including creation, modification, deletion, moderation, reporting, replies, featured status changes, visibility updates, and administrative actions. Audit logs support regulatory compliance, security investigations, operational monitoring, forensic analysis, dispute resolution, and internal governance while ensuring data integrity and controlled access.

#### 3.10.31.2 Actors

**Primary Actors**

- Platform Administrator
- Compliance Officer
- Internal Auditor
- Moderator

**Secondary Actors**

- Review & Rating Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Authentication Service
- Authorization Service
- Analytics Service
- Report Generation Service
- Notification Service
- Database Management System

#### 3.10.31.3 Preconditions

1. The actor must be authenticated.
2. The actor must possess audit log access privileges.
3. Audit Logging Service must be operational.
4. Authentication Service must be operational.
5. Authorization Service must be operational.
6. Review & Rating Service must be operational.
7. Database connectivity must be available.
8. Audit retention policies must be configured.
9. Report generation policies must be configured.
10. Log storage must be available.
11. Platform security policies must be enforced.
12. Analytics Service must be operational.
13. Required audit records must exist.
14. Time synchronization services must be operational.
15. Export policies must be configured.
16. Data privacy policies must be configured.
17. Compliance policies must be configured.
18. Network connectivity must be available.

#### 3.10.31.4 Trigger

An authorized user requests generation of review audit logs by selecting the **Generate Review Audit Log** option from the administration or compliance portal.

#### 3.10.31.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Data Type | Required | Description |
|------------|-----------|----------|-------------|
| Request ID | UUID | System Generated | Unique audit log generation request identifier. |
| Requested By | UUID | Yes | Administrator, auditor, compliance officer, or moderator identifier. |
| Review ID | UUID | No | Specific review identifier. |
| Restaurant ID | UUID | No | Restaurant identifier. |
| Customer ID | UUID | No | Customer identifier. |
| Activity Type | Enum | No | Create, Edit, Delete, Moderate, Report, Reply, Feature, Visibility Change, Rating Update, or All. |
| Date Range | Date Range | Yes | Audit log reporting period. |
| Export Format | Enum | Yes | PDF, CSV, JSON, XLSX. |
| Include System Events | Boolean | No | Include automated system-generated events. |
| Request Timestamp | DateTime | System Generated | Audit request timestamp. |

#### 3.10.31.6 Business Rules

1. Only authorized users shall generate review audit logs.
2. Audit logs shall contain immutable historical records.
3. Audit records shall never be modified after creation.
4. Audit log generation shall follow role-based access control.
5. Users shall only access audit data within their authorization scope.
6. Audit logs shall support filtering by review.
7. Audit logs shall support filtering by restaurant.
8. Audit logs shall support filtering by customer.
9. Audit logs shall support filtering by activity type.
10. Audit logs shall support filtering by date range.
11. Audit logs shall support multiple export formats.
12. Sensitive information shall be masked according to privacy policies.
13. Personally identifiable information shall be protected.
14. Export operations shall be audited.
15. Every report generation request shall be logged.
16. Audit reports shall preserve chronological ordering.
17. Audit timestamps shall use the platform standard time zone.
18. Audit records shall remain tamper resistant.
19. Report generation shall support pagination for large datasets.
20. Platform retention policies shall be enforced.
21. Compliance policies shall be enforced.
22. Security policies shall be enforced.
23. Analytics events shall be generated.
24. Operational metrics shall be updated.
25. Unauthorized requests shall be rejected.
26. Administrative overrides shall be audited.
27. Report generation shall maintain transactional consistency.
28. Cached audit data shall be refreshed where applicable.
29. Platform configuration rules shall be centrally managed.
30. Audit generation shall support horizontal scalability.
31. Audit logs shall remain synchronized across dependent services.
32. Report generation failures shall not modify audit records.
33. Generated reports shall include request metadata.
34. Legal and regulatory requirements shall be enforced.
35. All audit log operations shall comply with governance, security, privacy, compliance, and audit policies.

#### 3.10.31.7 Validations

1. Requested By shall exist.
2. User authentication shall succeed.
3. User authorization shall succeed.
4. Date Range shall be valid.
5. Date Range shall not exceed configured limits.
6. Review ID shall exist where provided.
7. Restaurant ID shall exist where provided.
8. Customer ID shall exist where provided.
9. Activity Type shall contain a supported value.
10. Export Format shall contain a supported value.
11. Audit access policy validation shall succeed.
12. Privacy policy validation shall succeed.
13. Compliance policy validation shall succeed.
14. Security policy validation shall succeed.
15. Report generation policy validation shall succeed.
16. Audit retention validation shall succeed.
17. Database validation shall succeed.
18. Transaction validation shall succeed.
19. Analytics validation shall succeed.
20. Audit logging validation shall succeed.
21. Request integrity validation shall succeed.
22. Mandatory system-generated fields shall be populated.
23. Export permission validation shall succeed.
24. Log availability validation shall succeed.
25. Storage validation shall succeed.
26. Concurrency validation shall succeed.
27. Time synchronization validation shall succeed.
28. Validation failures shall return appropriate error responses.

#### 3.10.31.8 Main Flow

1. The administrator, compliance officer, auditor, or moderator logs into the administration portal.
2. The actor navigates to the **Review Audit Log** module.
3. The actor selects **Generate Audit Log**.
4. The system displays available audit log filters.
5. The actor specifies the required date range.
6. The actor optionally specifies the Review ID.
7. The actor optionally specifies the Restaurant ID.
8. The actor optionally specifies the Customer ID.
9. The actor selects one or more activity types.
10. The actor selects the required export format.
11. The actor chooses whether to include system-generated events.
12. The actor submits the audit log generation request.
13. The Audit Logging Service receives the request.
14. The system authenticates the requesting user.
15. The system verifies authorization to access audit data.
16. The system validates all filter criteria.
17. The system validates the requested export format.
18. The system verifies audit retention policies.
19. The system retrieves matching audit records.
20. The system applies security and privacy masking rules.
21. The system sorts audit records chronologically.
22. The system compiles the audit report.
23. The system calculates report statistics.
24. The system generates report metadata.
25. The system prepares the report in the selected export format.
26. The system stores the generated report temporarily.
27. The system records the report generation activity.
28. The system updates analytics data.
29. The system updates operational metrics.
30. The system records the activity in the audit log.
31. The system commits the transaction.
32. The generated report becomes available for download.
33. Dependent services synchronize report generation information.
34. Background monitoring verifies successful report generation.
35. The audit log generation process completes successfully.

#### 3.10.31.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Authentication Failure**

1. User authentication fails.
2. The request is rejected.
3. The user is prompted to authenticate again.

---

**AF-2: Unauthorized Access**

1. The user lacks permission to access audit logs.
2. The request is denied.
3. An authorization error is returned.

---

**AF-3: Invalid Date Range**

1. The specified date range violates platform policies.
2. Validation fails.
3. The user is requested to provide a valid date range.

---

**AF-4: No Audit Records Found**

1. No audit records match the supplied filters.
2. An empty report is generated or an informational message is displayed.
3. The request completes successfully.

---

**AF-5: Unsupported Export Format**

1. The requested export format is unsupported.
2. Report generation is rejected.
3. The user selects a supported format.

---

**AF-6: Storage Failure**

1. Temporary report storage fails.
2. Report generation is rolled back where applicable.
3. An internal error is returned.

---

**AF-7: Database Failure**

1. Database access fails while retrieving audit records.
2. The transaction is rolled back.
3. An internal server error is returned.

---

**AF-8: Report Generation Failure**

1. Report formatting fails.
2. No report is generated.
3. The user receives an error response.

---

**AF-9: Concurrent Report Requests**

1. Multiple audit generation requests are submitted simultaneously.
2. Concurrency controls are applied.
3. Each request is processed independently according to system capacity.

---

**AF-10: Network Failure**

1. Network connectivity is interrupted during report generation.
2. Retry policies are applied where supported.
3. Processing resumes after connectivity is restored.

#### 3.10.31.10 Postconditions

1. The audit report shall be generated successfully.
2. Report metadata shall be stored.
3. Report generation activity shall be audited.
4. Analytics events shall be generated.
5. Operational metrics shall be updated.
6. Temporary report storage shall be available according to retention policy.
7. Audit records shall remain unchanged.
8. Privacy masking rules shall be enforced.
9. Audit report shall be available for download.
10. Dependent services shall reflect the completed generation request.

#### 3.10.31.11 Success Response

| Response Field | Description |
|----------------|-------------|
| Status | Success |
| Response Code | 200 OK |
| Message | Review audit log generated successfully. |
| Report ID | Generated audit report identifier. |
| Report Name | Generated report filename. |
| Export Format | Selected output format. |
| Record Count | Total number of audit records included. |
| Generated Timestamp | Report generation timestamp. |
| Download Status | Available |

#### 3.10.31.12 Failure Response

| Error Code | Description |
|------------|-------------|
| 400 | Invalid audit log generation request. |
| 401 | Authentication failed. |
| 403 | Unauthorized audit log access. |
| 404 | Requested audit data not found. |
| 409 | Concurrent report generation conflict. |
| 422 | Validation failed. |
| 500 | Internal server error. |
| 503 | Audit Logging Service temporarily unavailable. |

#### 3.10.31.13 Acceptance Criteria

1. Only authorized users shall generate review audit logs.
2. Audit records shall remain immutable.
3. Report filters shall function correctly.
4. Privacy masking shall be applied according to policy.
5. Reports shall be generated in supported export formats.
6. Report generation activity shall be audited.
7. Analytics events shall be generated successfully.
8. Audit records shall remain chronologically ordered.
9. Generated reports shall contain accurate metadata.
10. Audit reports shall comply with regulatory and organizational policies.

#### 3.10.31.14 Security Considerations

1. Authentication shall be mandatory before generating audit logs.
2. Authorization shall verify audit access privileges.
3. Audit records shall be protected against unauthorized modification.
4. Personally identifiable information shall be masked according to privacy policies.
5. Export permissions shall be validated.
6. Service-to-service communication shall use HTTPS/TLS encryption.
7. Platform security policies shall be enforced.
8. Security-related events shall be logged.
9. Administrative overrides shall require appropriate authorization.
10. Audit log generation shall comply with applicable security, privacy, and legal regulations.

#### 3.10.31.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit information:

- Audit Request ID
- Report ID
- Requested By
- User Role
- Applied Filters
- Export Format
- Report Record Count
- Report Generation Status
- Session ID
- Device Type
- IP Address
- Request Timestamp
- Request Correlation ID
- Processing Duration

#### 3.10.31.16 Assumptions

1. Audit records are continuously collected by the platform.
2. Audit retention policies are centrally managed.
3. Report generation infrastructure is operational.
4. Platform clocks remain synchronized.
5. Privacy masking rules are properly configured.
6. Temporary report storage follows retention policies.

#### 3.10.31.17 Dependencies

- Audit Logging Service
- Review & Rating Service
- Authentication Service
- Authorization Service
- Analytics Service
- Report Generation Service
- Notification Service
- Database Management System
- Storage Service
